### [1] [Observational constraints on CO sequestration and disk mass in the planet-hosting PDS 70 disk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14632)
*M. Leemker,S. Facchini,F. Zagaria,K. Doi,J. Bae,M. Benisty,H. B. Liu,C. Longarini,V. Pezzotta,L. Rampinelli,L. M. Stapper,R. Teague*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: 本研究首次在PDS 70原行星盘中探测到N₂H⁺和C¹⁷O，并通过与热化学模型网格对比，量化该盘的峰值CO丰度与盘质量，发现计入C¹⁷O线通量后盘质量降至约0.011 M☉，CO被"封存"至较低有效丰度，暗示行星可能正从CO封存的外盘分子层吸积大气。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系外行星从诞生环境中吸积气体、尘埃和冰，其大气与内部化学成分由此确定；盘中主要挥发物丰度有望将行星大气与其形成位置联系起来。PDS 70是首个在尘埃腔内直接确认两颗吸积行星的盘，因此量化其CO丰度与盘质量对理解行星形成化学有重要价值。

Method: 首次在PDS 70盘中探测N₂H⁺与C¹⁷O；分析C¹⁷O是否存在方位不对称（尾随连续谱已见的非对称结构）；将N₂H⁺、CO同位素体与连续谱的盘积分通量与既有热化学模型网格比较，分别在有/无C¹⁷O线通量参与拟合的情况下反演盘质量；并在考虑CO冻结与同位素选择性光解离后估计有效CO丰度（N₂H⁺发射分别按21 K光学薄与光学厚假设）。

Result: 计入C¹⁷O线通量后，导出的盘质量由0.039(+0.067/−0.018) M☉降至0.011(+0.011/−0.006) M☉，约缩小3.4倍（区间未含系统误差）。CO被封存，在探测盘层中相对H₂的有效丰度为(4.8(+4.3/−2.7)–17(+8/−8))×10⁻⁶，其区间范围反映N₂H⁺发射解释的不确定性。

Conclusion: PDS 70的两颗行星可能正从外盘分子层（CO被封存的区域）吸积其大气的最后部分；因此在解释行星大气化学组成时，需要考虑CO封存效应。

Abstract: Exoplanets accrete gas, dust, and ice from their natal environment, setting the chemical composition of their atmospheres and interiors. The abundance of the major volatiles in disks can be used to possibly link exoplanet atmospheres to their formation location in the disk. In this work, we aim to quantify the peak CO abundance and disk mass of the PDS 70 disk, the first disk with a direct confirmation of two accreting planets in its dust cavity. We present the first detection of N$_2$H$^+$ and C$^{17}$O in this disk. The C$^{17}$O emission shows marginal statistical evidence for an azimuthal asymmetry trailing that previously seen in the continuum emission. We then compare the disk integrated fluxes of N$_2$H$^+$, CO isotopologues, and the continuum to an existing grid of thermochemical models. We find that the derived disk mass drops by a factor of $\sim 3.4$ from $0.039^{+0.067}_{-0.018}$ M$_{\odot}$ to $0.011^{+0.011}_{-0.006}$ M$_{\odot}$ if the C$^{17}$O line flux is included in the fitting procedure; the quoted intervals do not include systematic uncertainties. CO on the other hand is sequestered with an effective abundance of $(4.8^{+4.3}_{-2.7} - 17^{+8}_{-8}) \times 10^{-6}$ relative to H$_2$ in the probed disk layers after accounting for CO freeze-out and isotope selective CO photodissociation for the adopted chemical network and ionization prescription. The range in peak CO abundances reflects the uncertainty in the N$_2$H$^+$ emission, assuming either optically thin emission at 21 K or optically thick emission. Therefore, the PDS 70 planets are possibly accreting the final portion of their atmospheres from the outer disk molecular layer where CO is sequestered. Thus, CO sequestering may need to be considered when interpreting the chemical composition of planetary atmospheres.

</details>


### [2] [Signatures of CH$_4$ and NH$_3$ in the atmosphere of $β$ Pictoris b](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14646)
*A. Barone,M. Janson,A. Brandeker,T. Stolker,A. Vigan,K. Hoy,P. Molliere,A. Denis,H. Anwand-Heerwart,Y. Charles,J. C. Costes,A. Costille,M. El Morsy,J. Garcia,M. Houllé,M. Lopez,G. Murray,E. Muslimov,G. P. P. L. Otten,J. Paufique,M. Phillips,U. Seemann,A. Viret,G. Zins*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: 结合44组CRIRES+近红外光谱数据对β Pic b大气进行分子搜寻，确认H₂O和CO，并给出CH₄与NH₃的暂定信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对β Pic b的高分辨率光谱仅探测到H₂O和CO，未探测到CH₄和NH₃；跨宽波段联合观测可最大化对弱分子信号的灵敏度，因此尝试搜寻此前未探测到的分子种类。

Method: 使用CRIRES+覆盖H、K、L、M波段的44组光谱设置，提取行星光谱并与分子模板做互相关；对可探测性不确定的物种，按分子信息量与数据质量加权，且该加权独立于候选物种的互相关函数测量结果。

Result: H₂O与CO的互相关信噪比分别约200和50；在β Pic b预期径向速度附近发现暂定的CH₄与NH₃信号，信噪比约5.3和5.7。在恒星对侧相同投影距离处做的等效提取未出现可比峰值。

Conclusion: CH₄与NH₃信号被列为暂定，因为其信噪比未完全计入可能的残余系统误差；若得到确认，这将是β Pic b大气中这两种分子的首次探测。

Abstract: $β$ Pictoris is a nearby (19.7 pc), young ($\sim$ 24 Myr), bright ($V = 3.85$) planetary system hosting three directly imaged planets. Previous high-resolution spectroscopy of $β$ Pic b has detected H$_2$O and CO, but not CH$_4$ or NH$_3$. Combining observations across a broad wavelength range has been shown to maximise sensitivity to such weak molecular signals. We searched for previously undetected molecular species in the atmosphere of $β$ Pic b using high-resolution near-infrared spectra. We combined 44 CRIRES+ spectral settings covering the H, K, L, and M bands and cross-correlated the extracted planetary spectra with molecular templates. For species with uncertain detectability, we weighted the observations by their molecular information content and data quality, independent of the measured candidate-species cross-correlation functions. We recover H$_2$O and CO at cross-correlation S/N values of approximately 200 and 50, respectively. We additionally identify tentative CH$_4$ and NH$_3$ signals near the expected radial velocity of $β$ Pic b, with S/N values of approximately 5.3 and 5.7. Equivalent extractions at the same projected separation on the opposite side of the star show no comparable peaks. We classify the CH$_4$ and NH$_3$ signals as tentative because their cross-correlation S/N values do not fully account for possible residual systematics. If confirmed, these would be the first detections of both species in the atmosphere of $β$ Pic b.

</details>


### [3] [Hydrolyzed Hazes on Water-rich Exoplanets: Composition and Prebiotic Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14666)
*Cara Pesciotta,Sarah M. Hörst,Michael J. Radke,Sarah E. Moran,B. P. Blakley,Chao He,Véronique Vuitton,Laurène Flandinet,Kathy Dubuc*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: 本文对温带富水系外行星的雾霭类似物进行液态水解实验，用高分辨质谱表征其化学演化，发现水解后产生数千种CHNO分子、氧含量显著升高且H/C、O/C分布更宽，暗示复杂糖类化学，挑战了现有前生命有机物的输送理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雾霭虽使亚海王星的光谱分析复杂化，但其存在本身可揭示行星宜居性：雾霭是有机物（包括氨基酸、核碱基、糖类等前生命分子）的来源，在温带富水行星上可能与液态水（凝结云或表面储库）发生反应。然而，雾霭在水环境中的化学演化研究几乎空白，且会影响其化学与光学行为。

Method: 在液态水中考察温带富水系外行星雾霭类似物的化学演化，采用高分辨质谱分析水解前后样品的分子组成、键合行为、化学复杂度、元素含量及H/C、O/C比值分布。

Result: 检测到数千种CHNO分子，其键合行为与化学复杂度区别于未改性的雾霭类似物；水解后可溶组分氧质量分数显著升高、不溶组分中度升高，碳键结构变化显示官能团重新分布；水解样品H/C与O/C比值分布更宽，分子式提示复杂的碳水化合物化学。

Conclusion: 水解驱动雾霭有机物的分子重组与氧化，提升前生命化学库存的复杂度，这一基于水环境的演化路径此前未被充分探索，对系外行星雾霭的化学与光学特征具有重要影响，并对当前前生命有机物输送理论提出挑战。

Abstract: Although photochemical hazes complicate the spectroscopic analysis of sub-Neptunes, the very presence of hazes can teach us about the habitability of these planets. Aside from affecting a planet's atmospheric and surface dynamics, hazes offer a source of organics potentially including prebiotic molecules like amino acids, nucleobases, and sugars. These organics are particularly compelling for temperate, water-rich planets where hazes may react with liquid water in condensate clouds or surface reservoirs. Hydrolysis reactions drive molecular reorganization and oxygenation, increasing the chemical complexity of the prebiotic inventory. This water-based evolution of hazes in their environment is largely unexplored and will have consequences for their chemical and optical behavior. In this work, we examine the chemical evolution of temperate water-rich exoplanet haze analogs in liquid water. High-resolution mass spectrometry finds thousands of CHNO molecules with bonding behaviors and chemical complexities distinct from the unaltered haze analogs. The oxygen weight percent increases significantly in the soluble fraction and moderately in the insoluble fraction after hydrolysis, with different carbon bonding structures demonstrating the redistribution of functional groups. The hydrolyzed samples exhibit a broader distribution in H/C and O/C ratios, and the molecular formulas suggest complex carbohydrate chemistry that challenges current theories of prebiotic organic delivery.

</details>


### [4] [Dwarfs of Fire and Ice: UV-Irradiation-Driven Thermal Inversion, Disequilibrium Chemistry, and a Cloud-Free Atmosphere in a Strongly Irradiated Brown Dwarf Probed by JWST/NIRSpec Phase-Resolved Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14703)
*Lael Shin,Daniel Apai,Yifan Zhou,Joshua D. Lothringer,Daphne Broski-Laing,Sarah L. Casewell,Siyi Xu,Ben W. P. Lew,Xianyu Tan,Jenni R. French,Vivien Parmentier,Mark S. Marley,L. C. Mayorga*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: 利用JWST/NIRSpec PRISM对白矮星-褐矮星双星SDSS J141126.20+200911.1进行全轨道时间分辨光谱观测，测得日夜间相对流量变化达约280%，并通过大气反演揭示了日面温度逆温、无云大气、CO2非平衡化学以及较低的热再分配效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 白矮星-褐矮星密近双星具有伴星与主星流量比高、轨道周期短、紫外辐照强的特点，是研究恒星辐照对次恒星大气影响的理想实验室；现有处于化学平衡、无辐照的孤立褐矮星正演模型可能无法描述强辐照环境下的大气结构。

Method: 使用JWST/NIRSpec PRISM对SDSS1411进行时间分辨的相位分辨光谱测量，拟合SDSS1411B的相位曲线（二阶傅里叶模型），分析傅里叶振幅间的相关性，并将观测光谱与无辐照化学平衡正演模型及大气反演结果进行对比，同时开展能量收支分析。

Result: 发现强烈的波长依赖变化，日夜间相对流量变化约280%；二阶傅里叶模型拟合良好，振幅相关性提示上层大气流量与温度变化更大；无辐照化学平衡正演模型无法重现观测相位光谱，而大气反演可同时重现日面与夜面光谱，揭示显著的日面温度逆温、倾向无云大气，并提供CO2非平衡化学证据；能量收支分析表明日夜间热再分配效率低，限制褐矮星冷却年龄约3 Gyr，且推断的褐矮星半径明显小于演化模型预言。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了紫外辐照褐矮星丰富的的大气动力学与非平衡化学，证明全轨道JWST相位分辨光谱在约束强辐照次恒星天体大气物理方面具有强大能力。

Abstract: We present time-resolved JWST/NIRSpec PRISM spectrophotometry of the white dwarf (WD)-brown dwarf (BD) binary SDSS J141126.20+200911.1 (SDSS1411). Close WD-BD binaries provide powerful laboratories for studying the effects of stellar irradiation on substellar atmospheres because of their high companion-to-host flux ratios, short orbital periods, and intense UV irradiation from the WD. We measure strong wavelength-dependent variability, with day-night relative flux variations reaching ~280%. The SDSS1411B phase curves are well described by a second-order Fourier model, and correlations among the Fourier amplitudes suggest larger flux and temperature variations in the upper atmosphere. Non-irradiated forward models under chemical equilibrium do not reproduce the observed phase-resolved SDSS1411B spectra, indicating substantial departures from isolated-BD atmospheric structures. In contrast, atmospheric retrievals successfully reproduce both the dayside and nightside spectra. The retrievals reveal a prominent dayside temperature inversion, favor cloud-free atmospheres, and provide evidence for CO2 disequilibrium chemistry. An energy-budget analysis indicates low day-to-night heat redistribution efficiency and constrains the BD cooling age to ~3 Gyr, while the inferred BD radius is significantly more compact than predicted by BD evolutionary models. These results reveal the rich atmospheric dynamics and disequilibrium chemistry of a UV-irradiated BD and demonstrate the power of full-orbit JWST phase-resolved spectroscopy for constraining the atmospheric physics of strongly irradiated substellar objects.

</details>

TL;DR: 利用NEID新光谱与径向速度观测确认Gaia 3b为超偏心巨行星，精确测定P=331.52±0.02天、e=0.9462±0.0017，并论证近等质量双星等非行星解释不可行；该行星是已知最近（约35 pc）的超偏心巨行星，其偏心率可能由遥远恒星伴星的ZKL振荡激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已识别出超偏心巨行星候选体Gaia 3b，但其轨道周期后验呈三峰分布，且RUWE等天体测量指标提示存在近等质量双星等替代解释，行星身份尚未确证；同时缺乏对如此极端偏心率形成机制的理解。

Method: 用NEID光谱仪新增23条跨越356天的径向速度，其中一条在近星点后数小时内获得；联合全部已有RV重新建模轨道，收敛三峰周期后验；对Gaia天体测量时间序列与RUWE做合成模拟，比较行星伴星与近等质量双星假说；结合Gaia颜色-星等位置及NEID互相关函数中bisector的形状与幅度排除双星情形。

Result: 周期后验由三峰收敛为P=331.52±0.02天；偏心率e=0.9462±0.0017，与先前测量一致，确认其为已知第三大偏心率的行星；模拟显示仅行星伴星与近等质量双星两种情形与RUWE相符，而后者被颜色-星等位置和bisector特征排除；系统距离约35 pc，为已知最近的超偏心巨行星。

Conclusion: Gaia 3b的行星身份得到确认，是距地球约35 pc的最近超偏心巨行星；预期Gaia DR4将在近邻类太阳恒星周围发现更多超偏心行星；系统中存在遥远恒星伴星，为通过von Zeipel-Kozai-Lidov振荡激发行星偏心率提供了可行途径。

Abstract: With new high-resolution spectra and radial velocities (RVs) from the NEID spectrometer, we reaffirm the planetary designation of the previously identified super-eccentric giant planet Gaia~3b. Our additional 23 RVs span 356 days, including an RV observation obtained within hours of the periastron passage. By modeling all of the available RVs we collapse a previously tri-modal orbital period posterior distribution and report a more precisely measured value of $P=331.52\pm0.02$ days. Our measure of the eccentricity ($e=0.9462\pm0.0017$) is in agreement with previous measurements and thus corroborates the status of Gaia~3b as the third most eccentric planet known to date. We generate synthetic realizations of the Gaia astrometric time series and derived RUWE to show that only the planetary-companion and close to equal-mass binary cases are plausible when compared to the reported RUWE. We then show that the close to equal-mass binary case is unlikely based on the Gaia color-magnitude position and the shape and amplitude of the bisectors measured from the NEID cross-correlation functions. The confirmation of Gaia~3b's planetary status makes it the closest known super-eccentric giant planet ($d \approx 35$ pc) and leads us to anticipate that Gaia DR4 will detect more super-eccentric planets orbiting nearby Sun-like stars. Finally, due to the presence of a distant stellar companion, we demonstrate that a plausible pathway exists for excitation of the planet's eccentricity by von Zeipel-Kozai-Lidov oscillations.

</details>


### [6] [Origins of Satellite-System Building Blocks I: Solid Delivery During Jupiter's Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14733)
*Sho Shibata,Andre Izidoro*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: 通过N体模拟耦合演化中的环行星盘，研究了星子物质向木星环行星盘的输送，发现伽利略卫星最可能经由"星子直接俘获提供种子+卵石吸积"的混合路径形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 木星卫星被认为形成于环行星盘（CPD），但这些固体物质的来源与输送机制尚不清楚；需要把木星自身生长、星子库演化与CPD演化放在同一框架下考察。

Method: 采用N体模拟，耦合随时间演化的CPD模型，同时追踪木星的生长及周围星子库的演化；统计经星子烧蚀（ablation）和直接俘获两种途径输入的固体质量，并考察烧蚀效率（气动加热效率）对结果的影响。

Result: 原木星虽迅速在星子盘中打开空隙，星子仍可持续进入CPD超过1 Myr；高效烧蚀时CPD可获得约1 MEarth的沉积质量，约为直接俘获质量的10倍。但CPD内卵石吸积效率低，仅靠烧蚀物质形成伽利略卫星需要极大的卵石库；直接俘获的星子虽总质量较小，却可提供经相互碰撞增长的卫星星子，作为后续卵石吸积的种子。即使星子库固体含量低于最小质量太阳星云，也足以提供形成伽利略卫星所需的构件。

Conclusion: 结果支持"卫星星子吸积+卵石吸积"的混合形成路径；烧蚀与直接俘获的平衡敏感依赖于气动加热效率，若烧蚀弱于常用假设，直接俘获将成为主要来源。研究将木星生长与卫星形成联系起来，说明木星生长如何调控固体物质向其CPD输送的时长与形式。

Abstract: Jupiter's satellites formed within a circumplanetary disk (CPD), but the origin and delivery of the solids from which they accreted remain uncertain. Here, we investigate the delivery of planetesimal material to the CPD while consistently accounting for the growth of Jupiter and the evolution of its surrounding planetesimal reservoir. Using N-body simulations coupled to an evolving CPD model, we quantify the solid mass supplied through planetesimal ablation and direct capture. We find that, although proto-Jupiter rapidly opens a gap in the planetesimal disk, planetesimals continue to enter the CPD for more than 1 Myr. Under efficient ablation, the mass deposited in the CPD can reach approximately 1 Me, about one order of magnitude higher than the mass delivered through direct capture. However, the low efficiency of pebble accretion within the CPD implies that forming the Galilean satellites from ablated material alone may require a very large pebble reservoir. Directly captured planetesimals, despite contributing less total mass, can instead provide satellitesimals that grow through mutual collisions and serve as seeds for subsequent pebble accretion. Our results therefore favor a hybrid formation pathway in which the Galilean satellites grow through a combination of satellitesimal and pebble accretion. We further demonstrate that even a planetesimal reservoir containing less solid material than the minimum-mass solar nebula can supply sufficient building blocks to form the Galilean satellites. The balance between the ablation and direct capture depends sensitively on the efficiency of aerodynamic heating: if ablation is weaker than commonly assumed, direct capture can become the dominant source of satellite building blocks. These results connect the formation of Jupiter and its satellites by showing how Jupiter's growth regulates both the duration and form of solid delivery to its CPD.

</details>


### [7] [A Taxonomic Characterization of Targets in the Emirates Mission to the Asteroid Belt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14813)
*Laura M. Lee,David E. Trilling,Theodore Kareta,Cristina Thomas,Gerard van Belle,Christopher S. Edwards,Christopher Haberle*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: 本文利用可见光波段光谱(0.40–0.95 μm)与Bus-DeMeo分类模板的卡方比对，对阿联酋小行星带任务(EMA)的全部七个目标进行了分类，识别出两颗稀有的B型小行星及S/L、X/C、D型目标。


<details>

To do this, we use a coupled N-body accretion and core-mantle differentiation model (Rubie et al., 2011, 2015b) applied to a range of proposed formation scenarios (Classical, Annulus, and Grand Tack scenarios). This model calculates the mantle chemistry of Earth-like planets in Solar System formation simulations and adjusts the initial redox state of the building blocks in a synthetic protoplanetary disk until the mantle of the simulated Earth closely matches the bulk silicate Earth. We find that a strong oxidizing gradient among the initial solids of the protoplanetary disk is favored to reproduce the chemistry of Earth's mantle, in agreement with Rubie et al. (2015b). The Solar System formation scenarios examined here successfully reproduce the composition of Earth if two related conditions are met: 1) there is a strong positive oxidation gradient with distance in the inner protoplanetary disk and 2) Earth accretes increasingly more oxidized material (on average) as it grows. Furthermore, in simulations that successfully reproduce Earth's composition, Proto-Earth and Theia may possess a wide range of mantle compositions and mass ratios. However, because late accretion is oxidizing across all proposed Solar System formation scenarios, Theia and Proto-Earth cannot both possess mantles that are more oxidized than the present-day Earth's mantle. Similarly, Theia's mantle may be significantly more oxidized than present-day Earth's mantle, but only if it is relatively small compared to the Proto-Earth.

Abstract: Precise planetary mass measurements are essential for constraining the internal structure and envelope properties of exoplanets and to break key degeneracies in atmospheric retrievals. Estimating the observational effort needed to reach a desired mass precision is therefore fundamental for the optimisation of current and future radial velocity (RV) surveys. We present MINERVA, a tool designed to estimate the required number of RV observations to measure transiting planet masses to a given precision, providing a robust scheduling framework for large-scale samples. MINERVA simulates realistic RV time series for targets orbiting FGKM-type stars. The code ingests real-world observational timestamps to account for weather interruptions and seasonal visibility gaps. To model stellar activity, the pipeline draws ages from local neighbourhood distributions, uses empirical calibrations to derive rotation and magnetic activity levels, and translates these into time-varying RV signals. We also derived a new empirical calibration between chromospheric activity and RV dispersion for K-type stars, using Global Architecture of Planetary Systems (GAPS) and Ariel Mass Survey (ArMS) data. We validated MINERVA on targets with known planetary masses, yielding required observational efforts highly consistent literature values. To demonstrate its capabilities on statistically significant samples, we applied the code to a subsample of the Ariel Mission Candidate Sample. Adopting HARPS-N/TNG as the reference instrument, we estimated the observing nights necessary to achieve a 30% planetary mass precision, providing an optimised observational roadmap for atmospheric characterisation. MINERVA's probabilistic and flexible architecture allows the optimisation of ongoing RV campaigns, the evaluating of undersampling risks in active stars, and the dynamic guidance of population-wide characterisation strategies.

We use this to combine the precise along-scan astrometric time series for Gaia-4 from the Gaia DR4 pre-release with 44 radial velocities (RVs) obtained over three years from four independent spectrographs (HPF, HARPS-N, NEID, and FIES). By jointly fitting these two complementary datasets that collectively span a 12-year baseline ($\sim$7 orbital periods of Gaia-4b), we refine the system parameters and determine the true mass of Gaia-4b to be $10.70\pm0.32$~$M_J$. If the terrestrial and/or giant planet formation explanations are correct, then the depletion of refractory elemental abundances in the photospheres of terrestrial/giant exoplanet host stars should be more pronounced and therefore observable even without achieving the highest possible elemental abundance inference precisions in systems with host star surface convective zones less massive than the Sun's. Motivation: 探测长周期冷巨行星有助于约束巨行星形成机制；在低质量恒星（K/M型）周围发现大质量、宽轨道的气态巨行星，可为区分核心吸积与盘引力不稳定性模型提供观测检验。

Method: 使用Magellan Clay望远镜上的Planet Finder Spectrograph进行16年视向速度监测；采用单开普勒与双开普勒轨道拟合评估残差均方根；结合Ca II H线活动指标评估恒星活动导致的抖动；联合Hipparcos与Gaia天体测量解算轨道倾角、真实质量、半长轴与偏心率。

Abstract: Using the Planet Finder Spectrograph (PFS) on the 6.5-m Magellan Clay Telescope at the Las Campanas Observatory we have discovered a cold gas giant exoplanet orbiting the K7V dwarf star HIP88962. 16 yrs of PFS observations imply an exoplanet with an orbital period of $\approx$ 19.3 yrs and a radial velocity semiamplitude (K) of 94.9 m s$^{-1}$. With a host star mass of 0.62 $M_\odot$, this implies a planet mass $M_p sin(i) \sim 5 M_{Jup}$. While stable M dwarfs have a PFS RMS of $\approx$ 3 m s$^{-1}$, the single Keplerian fit for HIP88962 has an RMS of $\approx$ 6 m s$^{-1}$, hinting at a second exoplanet. The best double planet Keplerian fit, with a 4.113 d period, still results in a RMS of $\approx$ 5 m s$^{-1}$. HIP88962 has Ca II H lines with twice the emission of four stable stars, implying that stellar activity may contribute to the RMS jitter error for the single Keplerian fit. When combined with Hipparcos and Gaia astrometry, HIP88962 b has a nearly edge-on orbit with inclination 91 $\pm$ 22 deg, true planet mass $4.83$ M$_{Jup}$, semimajor axis 6.15 $\pm$ 0.5 au, and eccentricity 0.718 $\pm$ 0.02. Including a PFS jitter of $\approx$ 7 m s$^{-1}$ yields $χ^2 = 0.995$ for the combined solution. A massive gas giant orbiting at $\approx$ 6 au around a 0.62 $M_\odot$ star is incompatible with published core accretion population synthesis models, but consistent with formation by gas disk gravitational instability.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 羽流-地表相互作用（PSI）是覆盖风化层天体作业的主要不确定性来源：尾喷流会侵蚀表面、形成坑并加速抛射物，但现有PSI关系多针对月球和火星着陆的特定场景推导，缺乏可推广性。厘清该问题既关乎航天器安全，也因受控推进器作用可充当小行星勘察中的"主动岩土实验"——通过测量喷流坑与抛射物反演风化层密度、强度、孔隙率与颗粒性质。这对行星防御尤为重要，因为近表面性质的不确定性会显著影响动能撞击偏转效果与动量传递的解释。

Method: 在近真空条件下，对无黏性小行星风化层模拟材料开展脉冲N₂气体驱动的挖掘实验；与低重力落塔实验及历史上化学推进器试验结果进行对照；借鉴弹丸成坑的π标度（π-scaling）理论并加以改造，构建以气体参数、靶体性质、重力和喷管参数为自变量的坑体积标度关系，并进一步推广至黏性靶体情形。

Result: 改造后的π标度关系能够描述气体成坑体积随气体、靶体、重力与喷管性质的变化；存在一个统一的"重力机制"关系式可同时拟合实验室数据并与一项低重力实验结果相符；将该关系推广到黏性靶体后，与阿波罗时代的化学推进器实测案例一致。

Conclusion: 气体驱动的喷流成坑可用统一的π标度框架刻画，具备跨重力与跨靶体条件的可推广性；据此可将航天器推进器用作小天体勘察中的主动岩土探测手段，为PSI预测、行星防御动量传递解释及风化层性质反演提供基础框架。

Abstract: Plume-surface interaction (PSI) effects remains a major uncertainty for operations on regolith-covered planetary bodies. Although spacecraft exhaust can erode surfaces, form craters, and accelerate ejecta, existing PSI relationships are largely derived for scenario-specific lunar and Martian landing conditions and are not yet reliably generalizable. Resolving this problem is important not only for spacecraft safety, but also because controlled thruster interactions could provide an active geotechnical experiment during asteroid reconnaissance. Measurements of thruster-formed craters and ejecta may constrain regolith density, strength, porosity, and particle properties that are otherwise difficult to determine remotely. Such information is particularly relevant to planetary defense, where uncertainty in near-surface properties can strongly affect the outcome of kinetic-impact mitigation and the interpretation of momentum transfer. We investigate gas-driven excavation using near-vacuum, pulsed-N$_2$ experiments in a cohesionless asteroid regolith analog and comparisons with a low-gravity drop-tower experiment and historical chemical-thruster tests. We adapt projectile-cratering $π$-scaling, and find that these relationships can describe gas crater volume as a function of gas, target, gravity, and nozzle properties. A common gravity-regime relationship describes the laboratory data and is consistent with an experimental low-gravity result. Generalizing the $π$-scaling relationship for cohesive targets shows consistency with Apollo-era chemical-thruster cases. These results provide a framework for using spacecraft thrusters as active geotechnical tools during small-body reconnaissance.

Abstract: We report a Neptune Trojan found by a shift-and-stack search of archival Zwicky Transient Facility (ZTF) difference images of 2019 October, and recovered in public images of seven other telescopes back to 2008. The orbit uses 88 positions on 54 nights from 2008 to 2025, a 17.22-year arc, all measured on our own Gaia DR3 plate solutions. Its heliocentric osculating elements at JD 2460000.5 are a = 30.2158 +/- 0.0004 au, e = 0.0661 and i = 20.32 deg; a fit of the same positions with the independent orbit code Find_Orb agrees within 0.4 sigma in every element. The nominal orbit librates about Neptune's leading Lagrange point L4 with a period of 9.25 kyr and an amplitude of 8.7 deg over the next Myr, and 84 +/- 2% of 500 clones of the orbit remain L4 Trojans for 4.5 Gyr. Its absolute magnitude is H_V = 6.96 +/- 0.10 (linear phase law), or 6.81 +/- 0.06 on the H, G convention of the Minor Planet Center's list of Neptune Trojans, whose brightest L4 member has H = 7.26, so the object is possibly the brightest known L4 Neptune Trojan. Abstract: Recent JWST and ALMA observations of protoplanetary disks have identified water reservoirs in the inner regions of the disks. This would provide terrestrial planets forming therein access to a water reservoir. In situ formation of water molecules on grain surfaces was proposed as one possible formation process in the PDS 70 system, but transport by small silicate particles coupled to the gas could be another mechanism. Laboratory experiments on the thermally induced desorption of water molecules from different water-containing silicates at elevated temperatures provide another possibility for the enrichment of water molecules in the inner disk. Physically and chemically bound water in silicates can be released upon annealing processes. Water that is physically bound undergoes desorption at temperatures up to 473 K. In contrast, chemically bound water in form of different silanol or Mg-OH groups in amorphous silicates or phyllosilicates, can be released at higher temperatures, ranging from 773 to 923 K. The relevant temperatures are sufficient to trigger the solid-state recrystallization of amorphous silicates to Mg-rich orthopyroxene and olivine. The existence of these crystalline silicates has been substantiated through Spitzer and JWST-MIRI observations. Based on the experimental results, we postulate that the desorption of bound hydroxyl groups may serve as a mechanism for the formation of the water reservoir at temperatures up to 900 K, much higher than the water sublimation temperature of 100 to 200 K.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已发现的系外行星系统普遍呈现"豌豆荚"结构——行星大小相近、对数间距近似均匀；但不少系统在该出现行星的间距处存在"间隙"。常见解释是有尚未探测到的行星隐藏其中，作者提出另一可能：这些间隙由双行星（而非单颗行星）占据。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛N体数值模拟，比较双行星与常规假设的单个行星在系统动力学稳定性上的差异。

Conclusion: 从稳定性角度看，双行星及其"表亲"系外卫星为部分动力学间隙的存在提供了合理解释，值得作为单行星假设之外的另一种解释加以考虑。

Abstract: The "peas-in-a-pod" architecture of planetary systems, where planets are roughly evenly sized and log-spaced, is pervasive throughout known exoplanetary systems. However, many systems deviate slightly from this trend in that they have a "gap" where no planet has been detected, where one might have otherwise expected one to exist. There are many common explanations for the existence of these gaps, including the existence of as-yet-undiscovered planets lurking in these gaps; however, we propose that one potential explanation for some of these gaps is that they are filled by binary planets, a pair of gravitationally bound planets orbiting a common center of mass outside of both bodies. Specifically, in this work, we investigate the dynamical stability of these binary planets as compared to the more conventionally assumed single planets using Monte Carlo N-body simulations. We find that binary planets are slightly less stable than single planets. Overall, we find that binary planets and their cousins in exomoons provide a plausible explanation, at least from a stability perspective, for the existence of some dynamical gaps.

C$_2$ ($T_{\rm rot}\approx9200$ K, $T_{\rm vib}\approx7800$ K) and CN (about 7000 K) are far hotter than the surface. The colour temperature of the heat shield rises from 2720 to 3080 K, in agreement with airborne results. The radiated energy is one to two orders of magnitude below that of natural fireballs, because the light comes from the hot surface rather than from ablation vapour. Using two albedos corrects the overestimation of the northern sublimation event, a major defect of the model. Our study highlights that each parameterization has indirect consequences amplified by the radiatively active clouds. Abstract: A planet moving through a changing magnetic field will have a current induced on it by that magnetic field. If the magnetic Reynolds number in the upper atmosphere of this planet is small, the dissipation of this current could result in observable changes to the planet's properties. In this paper, we estimated the total power that would be dissipated in the observed giant exoplanet population if they orbited stars with misaligned magnetic dipoles. We found that, even if all planets orbit stars with extremely misaligned surface magnetic fields, the vast majority of known exoplanets are unlikely to dissipate enough magnetic power to cause observable changes in their surface temperatures. We also extended our calculations to estimate the impact that a low-but-nonzero planetary eccentricity would have on the induced power, and provide a code that implements these calculations. Overall, while it is unlikely that any currently known exoplanets are experiencing a high enough degree of stellar-induced Ohmic heating to be observable, we cannot rule out the possibility that an eccentric, inclined planet on a short-period orbit around a superlatively magnetically active star could have observable magnetic heating. We note that our calculations assume that magnetic diffusion dominates over induction in the upper atmospheres of these planets. However, hot Jupiters with large wind speeds may have high magnetic Reynolds numbers and therefore be in the inductive regime.

Abstract: The JWST Rocky Worlds Director's Discretionary Time (RWDDT) program uses secondary eclipse observations to determine the conditions under which rocky exoplanets around M dwarfs retain their atmospheres. We present RWDDT MIRI/F1500W observations of LTT 1445 A b and c, the transiting rocky siblings in the nearest such system (6.9 pc), comprising two eclipses of each planet across three visits. We perform three independent reductions and a joint fit that includes all existing JWST data and 220 radial velocities, improving the precision on the planetary radii, masses, and orbital parameters by factors of 1.6-2.3. We measure eclipse depths of $157^{+18}_{-17}$ ppm and $154^{+54}_{-37}$ ppm for planets b and c, corresponding to 15 $μ$m brightness temperatures of $528^{+39}_{-29}$ K and $625^{+109}_{-79}$ K, both consistent with the dark, bare rock limit. We conclude that planet b has no substantial atmosphere. For planet c, a potentially grazing eclipse and overlapping events leave the depth poorly constrained; the data rule out only thick, CO$_2$-bearing atmospheres ($\gtrsim1$ bar with $\geq1\%$ CO$_2$). LTT 1445 A b is the planet closest to the canonical XUV-driven Cosmic Shoreline observed in eclipse so far. ### [25] [MAHA-z: A Mahalanobis distance kNN estimator for photometric redshifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14582)
*Paula S. Ferreira,Ribamar R. R. Reis,Renyue Cen*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: MAHA-z 是一个结合 kNN 回归与从光谱样本协方差学习所得 Mahalanobis 度量的测光红移估计器，在 Euclid Flagship 2 与 DES Y3 等四个数据集上取得 σ_NMAD≈0.015–0.023、离群率<1% 的精度，并能以极低计算成本在数分钟内处理数百万星系并输出校准良好的后验 PDF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练型机器学习测光红移方法精度高但计算开销大、需要大量训练与调参，而传统 kNN 方法又需对特征做经验式归一化且难以处理缺失波段；作者希望获得一种兼顾精度、PDF 质量与计算效率的实用估计器，适用于 Stage-IV 巡天的迭代式测光红移生产与验证。

Method: 方法将 k 近邻回归与从光谱参考样本协方差中学得的 Mahalanobis 距离结合。对每个目标星系，在包含颜色-星等（可选形态参数）的特征空间中进行近邻搜索，并通过对近邻红移做核密度估计得到点估计与后验 p(z|f)。Mahalanobis 度量自动处理特征相关性与量纲不统一，无需额外归一化；其子空间限制使缺失波段可按星系逐个处理而无需插补。验证覆盖 Euclid Flagship 2 模拟、Legacy-KiDZ 标定样本、VIPERS 与 DES Year 3 BAO 样本四个数据集。

Result: 在 Euclid Flagship 2（LSST+NISP）上达到 σ_NMAD=0.0148、离群率 0.27%；在 DES Y3 上 σ_NMAD≈0.023、离群率<1%，后验校准水平与 ANNz2 星表相当。方法可在数分钟内处理数百万星系。

Conclusion: MAHA-z 在保持有竞争力精度的同时显著降低计算成本，能提供质量相当的 PDF，是 Stage-IV 巡天中迭代式测光红移生产与验证的实用方案。

Abstract: We present \texttt{MAHA-z}, a photometric-redshift (photo-$z$) estimator that combines $k$-nearest neighbour ($k$NN) regression with a Mahalanobis distance metric learned from the covariance of a spectroscopic reference sample. For each target galaxy, \texttt{MAHA-z} performs a neighbour search in a colour-magnitude feature space with optional morphological parameters, and returns both a point estimate and a redshift posterior $p(z\mid\mathbf{f})$ obtained by kernel density estimation over the neighbour redshifts. The Mahalanobis metric accounts for correlated features and heterogeneous units, eliminating ad hoc normalisation, and its subspace restriction allows \texttt{MAHA-z} to handle missing bands on a per-galaxy basis without imputation. We validate the method on four datasets spanning simulations and survey photometry: the Euclid Flagship~2 mock, the Legacy-KiDZ calibration sample, VIPERS, and the DES Year~3 BAO sample. On Euclid Flagship~2 (LSST+NISP) we achieve $σ_{\rm NMAD}=0.0148$ with a $0.27\%$ outlier fraction, and on DES Y3 we obtain $σ_{\rm NMAD}\approx0.023$ with $<1\%$ outliers and posterior calibration comparable to the \texttt{ANNz2} catalogue. \texttt{MAHA-z} processes millions of galaxies in minutes and provides competitive PDFs at substantially lower computational cost than trained machine-learning models, making it a practical option for iterative photo-$z$ production and validation in Stage-IV surveys.

</details>


### [26] [galpy with differentiable, accelerated backends: galactic dynamics in jax and PyTorch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14614)
*Jo Bovy*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: galpy 新增 jax 与 PyTorch 后端，保留原有 numpy/C 路径，使引力势、轨道积分、作用量-角度变换与分布函数可在 GPU 上运行、可编译、可自动微分，并在 GPU 上获得 1–5 个数量级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: galpy 原有的 numpy/C 实现难以利用 GPU 并行与自动微分能力，限制了大规模批量轨道、分布函数采样与潮汐流建模的效率，也难以将星系动力学模型嵌入可微的机器学习或参数拟合流程。

Method: 在 galpy 中引入后端抽象框架，将 jax 和 PyTorch 作为与 numpy、C 并列的计算后端；后端由传入数组的类型自动选择，对用户接口保持透明。该框架在各模块中实现，覆盖引力势、轨道积分、作用量-角度正反变换、分布函数及潮汐流模型，并支持编译与对输入和参数的求导（从单点受力到分布函数采样）。论文给出后端框架设计与各模块实现、使用示例及局限性讨论，并以超高速星拟合银河系势、用神经网络通过 galpy 学习逆作用量-角度变换作为能力演示。代码主要由 AI 编码代理在人类作者详细指导与审查下完成，文中也说明了工作组织方式与正确性验证方法。

Result: 在 GPU 上，批量轨道、分布函数采样和流模型比单 CPU 核心快 1–5 个数量级；numpy 路径结果与之前一致，用户接口不变；并成功演示了基于梯度的势参数拟合与神经网络学习逆作用量-角度变换。

Conclusion: 该工作构成 galpy 第 2 版本的起点，已发布并配有文档，为星系动力学提供了可微、可编译、可 GPU 加速的统一计算框架；同时也明确讨论了当前实现的局限性。

Abstract: We describe an extension of galpy, the Python library for galactic dynamics, that adds jax and PyTorch as computational backends alongside its existing numpy and C implementation. With it, galpy's gravitational potentials, orbit integration, action-angle transformations and their inverses, and distribution functions, including those for tidal streams, run on GPUs, can be compiled as part of a user's program, and can be differentiated with respect to their inputs and parameters, from the force at a point to the samples drawn from a distribution function. The user-facing design is kept simple: the active backend follows from the type of the arrays that are passed in, the galpy interface is unchanged, and the numpy path gives the same results as before. We describe the design of the backend framework and its implementation in each of galpy's modules, with examples of use, and we discuss its limitations. On a GPU, batches of orbits, samples from distribution functions, and stream models run one to five orders of magnitude faster than on a single CPU core. To illustrate the new capabilities, we present simple examples of fitting the Galactic potential with a hypervelocity star and of learning the inverse action-angle transformation with a neural network trained through galpy. An AI coding agent wrote essentially all of the code under the detailed direction and review of the human author, and we describe how we organized this work and how we established the correctness of the result. This work is the start of galpy's version 2, which is available at https://github.com/jobovy/galpy and documented at https://docs.galpy.org/en/v2/ .

</details>


### [27] [ViViViT: a Video-to-Video Vision Transformer for Cosmological Lightcone Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14617)
*Federico Semenzato,Alvise Raccanelli,Michele Liguori*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沿视线方向移动同时也在穿越宇宙时间，因此光锥观测本质上类似一段"宇宙结构演化的视频"。传统做法把光锥重建当作三维静态体数据处理，忽视了观测量自身随时间演化的几何性质，缺少天然尊重宇宙时间的场级架构。

Result: 在功率谱与相位对齐上，ViViViT 在直到 Nyquist 尺度范围内与真值误差保持在百分之几；对所有边长至 k≈0.77 h/Mpc 的三角构型，三点相关函数也得到保持；在所有测量统计量上均优于 3D-U-Net 基线。

Conclusion: 通过显式编码宇宙可观测量的演化几何，ViViViT 向"原生尊重宇宙时间"的场级架构迈出一步，为宇宙示踪物的时间感知重建以及 21cm 数据中红移相关特征的定向搜寻开辟了路径。

Abstract: Moving along the line of sight of a cosmological lightcone also moves through cosmic time, as in a video of cosmic structure traced by an evolving observable. However, similar to other deep learning techniques such as Vision Transformers, they require large training samples. Current methods are also limited to classification, rather than regression. Here, we apply the solid harmonic wavelet scattering bispectrum, a method that encodes invariances and symmetries in the data which need to be learned by CNNs, as an alternative to CNNs. We measure the gain of MAGI with statistical amplification $1/χ$ and computational speed-up $S$: the first goes from 150 for high-statistics cosmic rays to 12000 for rare radioactive decays, while the latter ranges from 2.1 to almost 240, depending on source type and mass model. Abstract: Mechanical vibrations in astronomical telescopes can degrade image quality, making their detection and characterization essential. This work presents the development and validation of a low-cost, portable vibration monitoring system based on MEMS accelerometers interfaced with a microcontroller. The system integrates sensor acquisition and data analysis capabilities and was evaluated through controlled laboratory tests.
  The system was subsequently deployed at the historic Schmidt Camera of the Tonantzintla Astronomical Observatory in Mexico. After more than 80 years of operation, this study represents the first characterization of its mechanical vibrations. Preliminary measurements reveal dominant frequency components and their corresponding amplitudes, providing insight into the telescope's dynamic behavior and potential structural couplings that may affect image quality.
  Understanding the vibrational environment of astronomical telescopes is essential for the successful integration of new instrumentation. Identifying vibration frequencies and amplitudes can support the development of mitigation strategies, data filtering techniques, and numerical simulations to predict image quality degradation. The results demonstrate the feasibility of low-cost inertial sensing for monitoring heritage-class telescopes and provide a foundation for future instrumentation and preservation efforts at the Tonantzintla Observatory.

TL;DR: VIDE 2.0 是升级版空洞（void）搜寻与管线，通过无需大量边界粒子的确定性边界/不完备处理、自动掩膜分辨率设定和引擎提速，实现 >100 倍加速与内存大幅下降，可在单台笔记本约 15 分钟处理 1200 万星系的 DESI Y1 型模拟目录，并在两套大型模拟上验证了关键统计量的宇宙方差协方差收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来光谱与测光星系巡天将提供数千万乃至上亿星系，传统空洞识别算法在计算效率、内存占用以及巡天边界与不完备性处理上难以应对；已有 VIDE 1.0 在同类硬件上甚至无法运行 10^6 星系量级的目录，且依赖大量边界粒子处理边界，产生伪空洞并浪费统计潜力。

Method: 在 VIDE 基础上构建 VIDE 2.0：保留基于 Voronoi 划分与分水岭（watershed）的无参数空洞识别方案，新增确定性边界机制——识别边缘星系并标记受其污染的 void（逐 void 污染旗标），替代大量边界粒子；引入掩膜分辨率自动设定规则使全流程无参数；并对底层 VIDE 引擎做性能优化。在 MultiDark-Patchy DR12 与 AbacusSummit DESI Y1 ELG 两套大型模拟上测试空洞数函数、空洞-星系互相关与空洞椭率分布的收敛性。

Result: 实现 >100 倍加速与内存大幅下降：1200 万星系 DESI Y1 型模拟目录在单台笔记本约 15 分钟完成，而 VIDE 1.0 在相同硬件上无法运行 10^6 星系目录；边界处理确定性保证相同输入产生逐位可复现的目录；借助逐 void 污染旗标可识别更靠近巡天边界的空洞以最大化统计潜力；两套模拟均给出空洞数函数、空洞-星系互相关及空洞椭率分布的宇宙方差协方差收敛结果。

Conclusion: VIDE 2.0 高效、确定性且几乎无需参数，适合处理模拟与真实巡天中的海量星系，可支撑 Stage IV 空洞宇宙学及未来巡天计划。

Abstract: Current and future spectroscopic and photometric galaxy surveys will deliver unprecedented numbers of galaxies, with expected counts in the tens of millions. To answer the computational challenges posed by these surveys, in this paper we present VIDE 2.0, a significantly improved and enhanced void finding algorithm and analysis pipeline based on VIDE. Leveraging the parameter-free identification of voids via a Voronoi tessellation and watershed scheme, we implement a robust mechanism for handling survey boundaries and incompleteness without relying on vast numbers of boundary particles. This new method identifies edge galaxies and flags voids contaminated by their presence. The mechanism is fully deterministic, producing bit-for-bit reproducible catalogs from identical inputs. An automatic rule for setting the mask resolution renders the entire pipeline parameter-free. Combined with other performance enhancements to the underlying VIDE engine, we find significant ($>100\times$) speedups and a dramatic reduction in memory usage compared to previous versions: a 12-million-galaxy DESI Y1-class mock catalog now completes in roughly 15 minutes on a single laptop, where VIDE 1.0 could not run a catalog of even $10^6$ galaxies on the same hardware. We are also able to identify voids existing closer to survey boundaries via deterministic per-void contamination flags, maximizing their statistical potential. We exercise the upgraded pipeline on two large mock suites (MultiDark-Patchy DR12 and AbacusSummit DESI Y1 ELG), demonstrating converged cosmic-variance covariances for the void number function, the void-galaxy cross-correlation, and the void ellipticity distribution -- key ingredients for Stage IV void cosmology. VIDE 2.0 is now well suited to handle large numbers of galaxies in mock and real observations, and ready to tackle the challenges of future campaigns.

*Yusuke Okuma,Shoki Iwaguchi,Ryosuke Sugimoto,Yutaro Enomoto,Kiwamu Izumi*

TL;DR: 本文提出利用三角形激光链路三个干涉仪输出构造的零通道(null channel)来标定BLFPI中法布里-珀罗腔响应，通过最小化零通道积分功率优化响应参数，数值结果表明标定后残余激光频率噪声在整个观测频段低于BLFPI设计灵敏度一个量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BLFPI依靠法布里-珀罗腔实现高引力波灵敏度，其腔响应标定误差会直接限制激光频率噪声的抑制，而目前尚无实用的腔响应标定策略，因此需要建立一种可行的标定方法。

Method: 利用三角形激光链路构型的三个干涉仪输出构造零通道，在该通道中引力波信号相互抵消，残余激光频率噪声反映腔响应估计误差；通过优化响应参数使零通道积分功率最小来估计腔响应，并解析推导技术噪声引起的估计偏差，再进行数值评估。

Result: 标定后的残余激光频率噪声在整个观测频段内比BLFPI设计灵敏度低一个数量级以上，技术噪声导致的估计偏差得到解析刻画。

Conclusion: 所提出的零通道标定方法能够达到有效扣除激光频率噪声所需的标定精度，为BLFPI腔响应的实用标定提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate calibration of the detector response is essential for precision gravitational-wave (GW) observations. In the Back-linked Fabry-Perot Interferometer (BLFPI), which employs Fabry-Perot cavities to achieve high GW sensitivity, accurate calibration of the cavity response is particularly important because residual calibration errors directly limit the suppression of laser frequency noise. However, no practical calibration strategy for the cavity response has yet been established. In this paper, we propose a method for calibrating the cavity response using a null channel constructed from the three interferometer outputs of the triangular laser-link configuration. In null channel, GW signals are canceled and residual laser frequency noise indicates errors in the cavity response estimates. Therefore we can estimate the cavity response by optimizing the response parameters to minimize the integrated power of the null channel. We derive the estimation bias introduced by technical noise analytically and evaluate the performance of the proposed method numerically. The results show that the residual laser frequency noise after calibration is more than an order of magnitude below the designed BLFPI sensitivity throughout the observation band. This demonstrates that the proposed null-channel calibration method achieves the accuracy required for effective subtraction of laser frequency noise.

</details>


### [33] [Sky Monitor AI: classifying night-sky conditions from an observatory all-sky camera with a CNN ensemble, label-free sky types and exact evidence maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14987)
*Muhammad Waqas*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该论文研究小型远程天文台的夜间晴/云自动判识问题，基于东山天文台全天相机8806帧（32夜）数据，构建从手工统计特征、小型CNN到ImageNet预训练ResNet-18集成的分类方案，在按整夜留出的测试条件下集成模型达86.7%准确率（AUC 0.924），并给出无梯度证据图、云量估计与开源的Sky Monitor AI应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型/远程天文台每晚都需自行判断天空是否足够晴朗以决定是否观测，人工判读耗时且主观；同时既有做法常存在数据泄漏（如画面中印刷日期戳成为"捷径"）与评估不严谨（按单帧而非整夜划分）的问题，且模型容量与独立夜晚数不匹配易过拟合。论文旨在建立一套严谨、可解释、可落地的全天相机晴/云分类基线。

Method: 数据为东山天文台辅助全天相机的8806帧、32个夜晚；用圆形天空掩膜去除画面中的印刷日期戳以消除捷径，全部评估均以整夜为单位留出（6个测试夜）。对比三类方法：手工设计的天空统计特征、从零训练的小型CNN、以及经验证集挑选的"小型CNN + 两个ImageNet预训练ResNet-18变体"集成。分析手段包括按月亮高度分层的准确率诊断、标签分块（1–2小时）带来的噪声分析、对预训练CNN嵌入的无监督聚类、基于梯度的显著图与边缘（rim）测试，以及针对以全局平均池化结尾的网络将决策精确分解为局部logit，从而得到免梯度的证据图与云量估计。

Result: 6个测试夜上：手工特征77.1%，从零训练小CNN 84.3%，验证集选出的集成86.7%（AUC 0.924）且夜际波动最小。月光是主要难点：随月亮高度变化，集成模型准确率波动5个百分点，手工模型达20个百分点。在约24个训练夜的条件下微调更深的ResNet层出现过拟合，说明模型容量需与独立夜晚数匹配。剩余错误多为误标或部分有云的帧。无标签下对预训练嵌入聚类，可87%纯度地恢复晴/云划分，并自动分离出相机罩上有水滴的帧。梯度图与边缘测试表明网络依赖天空区域。GAP结尾网络的决策可精确分解为局部logit，得到免梯度证据图与云量估计。发布了开源应用Sky Monitor AI，支持单帧、整夜与实时相机。

Conclusion: 在独立夜晚数有限的小型天文台场景下，适度的模型容量配合简单集成优于更深网络的微调，且按整夜留出的评估与去除日期戳等捷径是可信结论的前提。GAP网络的精确局部logit分解为分类器提供了免梯度的可解释性与云量估计，无监督嵌入聚类可作为免标注的辅助质检（含镜头水滴检测）。研究成果以开源工具Sky Monitor AI落地，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Small and remotely operated observatories must decide every night whether the sky is clear enough to observe. We classify frames of the auxiliary all-sky camera of the Higashi-Hiroshima Observatory as clear or cloudy, using 8806 frames from 32 nights. A circular sky mask removes a printed date stamp that would act as a shortcut, and all evaluations use whole held-out nights. On six test nights, hand-crafted sky statistics reach 77.1% accuracy, a small CNN trained from scratch 84.3%, and a validation-selected ensemble of the small CNN and two ImageNet-pretrained ResNet-18 variants 86.7% (AUC 0.924) with the smallest night-to-night scatter. Moonlight is the dominant difficulty: across Moon altitude the ensemble's accuracy varies by 5 percentage points, the hand-crafted model's by 20. Fine-tuning deeper ResNet layers overfits with about 24 training nights, underlining the need to match model capacity to the number of independent nights. The labels were assigned in blocks of 1-2 h, and many remaining errors are mislabelled or partly cloudy frames. Without labels, clustering of pretrained CNN embeddings recovers the clear/cloudy split with 87% purity and isolates frames with water droplets on the camera dome. Gradient-based maps and a rim test indicate that the networks rely on sky regions. For networks ending in global average pooling, the decision decomposes exactly into local logits, giving gradient-free evidence maps and a cloud-cover estimate. We release Sky Monitor AI, an open-source application for single frames, whole nights and live cameras.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观星营地的道路照明会造成眩光与杂散光，干扰天文观测。作者希望在不更换、不废弃现有太阳能路灯系统的前提下，找到一种低成本的减光方案，并量化其实际效果。

Result: 0°方向上，P1四方位平均SQM读数提高1.624 mag/arcsec²，P2朝向营地读数提高2.312 mag/arcsec²，相当于在测量视场内等效SQM信号分别减少约77.6%和88.1%。变化主要集中在低仰角，30°及以上仰角的改善较小。同曝光照片显示遮光罩灯具周围可见眩光减少。

Conclusion: 定向遮光罩是一种成本较低、可保留既有道路照明系统的减光措施；其效果集中在低仰角方向，对高仰角天空贡献有限。因此该方案适合抑制近地面眩光与水平方向杂散光，若要进一步改善天顶方向暗天空条件，仍需配合其他措施。

Abstract: Road lighting at stargazing camps can cause glare and stray light that interfere with observation. We installed directional shields on existing solar-powered streetlights at Bayan Gol Starlight Camp in Inner Mongolia, China, and compared SQM-L measurements before and after installation at the camp center (P1) and a site approximately 500~m away (P2). Measurements were taken at elevations of $0^\circ$, $30^\circ$, $60^\circ$, and $90^\circ$. At $0^\circ$, the mean SQM reading across four directions at P1 increased by 1.624~\sqmUnit{}, and the reading toward the camp at P2 increased by 2.312~\sqmUnit{}. These changes correspond to reductions of approximately 77.6\% and 88.1\% in the equivalent SQM signal within the measured fields of view. The largest changes occurred at $0^\circ$; changes at $30^\circ$ and above were smaller. Photographs taken with identical exposure settings showed less visible glare around the shielded lamps. The measurements indicate that the changes were concentrated in low-elevation views. Directional shields provide a relatively low-cost way to reduce unwanted light while retaining the existing road-lighting system.

Abstract: We have set up a simple experiment to characterise a dual-polarised bowtie dipole antenna at St. Stephens College as part of undergraduate radio astronomy experiments. The antenna provides two orthogonal linear polarisation responses, allowing us to investigate the polarisation properties of radio-frequency signals using a simple and affordable experimental setup. In this article, we present our analysis of the performance of the antenna and demonstrate its use through polarisation-rotation measurements. This antenna is part of the Sky Watch Array Network (SWAN) and this analysis helps us understand the response of each individual array element in the setup.

*David M. Nataf,David L. Nidever,Gagandeep Anand,Magda Arnaboldi,Joss Bland-Hawthorn,Scott Call,Santi Cassisi,Marcio Catelan,Julianne Dalcanton,Ivanna Escala,Mario Gennaro,Benjamin Gibson,Karoline Gilbert,Michael Hayden,Danny Horta,Caroline Huang,Rodrigo Ibata,Christian Johnson,Evan Kirby,Chiaki Kobayashi,Andrea Kunder,Geraint Lewis,Anna Liptrott,Massimo Marengo,Melissa Ness,Rachel Pauline,Henry Prager,Zdenek Prudil,Henrique Reggiani,Michael Rich,Ricardo Schiavon,Sanjib Sharma,Brian Thorsbro,Yuan-Sen Ting,Dan Weisz,Rosemary Wyse,Gail Zasowski,Catherine Zucker*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出一个约800小时的多周期詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（JWST）"金库级"巡天计划，用NIRSpec、NIRCam和MIRI对仙女座星系（M31）进行类似银河系APOGEE/Gaia/WISE式的恒星级测绘，通过"砖块"式并联观测设计使约40%曝光时间"免费"，最终首次构建出另一个L*星系基于单颗恒星的化学动力学、恒星形成与尘埃图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系已被APOGEE、Gaia、WISE等巡天在单星层面详尽刻画，但我们尚无对任何其他L*星系的同等水平测绘。M31作为最近的L*星系，是理解星系化学动力学演化、恒星形成与尘埃分布的关键对照目标，而现有单周期观测在覆盖面积与深度上均无法达到所需规模。

Method: 提出约800小时的多周期JWST观测方案：NIRSpec对约4500颗红巨星和超巨星进行光谱观测（S/N~80，可测16种元素、精度≲0.05 dex，径向速度精度数km/s）；NIRCam在最多10个滤光片中成像约1600平方角分，覆盖约2000万颗恒星，每隔一年重访同一视场，获得约500颗造父变星与约10^4颗米拉变星的两年期红外测光；MIRI在5个滤光片中达到PHANGS巡天深度、覆盖约450平方角分。观测组织为12个"砖块"，每块含三个相邻视场（含两个NIRSpec/MOS指向），一台仪器主观测、另一台并联，覆盖核区、核球、长轴至29 kpc、短轴及臂间盘区；另设100小时短曝光NIRCam网格并配MIRI并联，覆盖HST-PHAT天区。

Result: 预期成果包括：对约4500颗巨星的多元素丰度与运动学测量、约2000万颗恒星的两历元红外测光、以及造父变星与米拉变星的周期—光度关系样本；并首次得到除银河系之外的一个L*星系基于单颗恒星的化学动力学、恒星形成与尘埃分布图，其规模超出任何单周期项目所能达到的水平。

Conclusion: 该多周期巡天通过在JWST上并联使用三种仪器并采用"砖块"式高效设计，可在合理曝光成本下（约40%时间无需额外计费）实现对M31的全方位单星层面测绘，为星系演化研究提供银河系之外的第一个同类标杆数据集。

Abstract: We propose a Multi-Cycle Treasury program of $\sim$800 hours that would survey Andromeda the way the Milky Way has been surveyed by APOGEE, Gaia, and WISE.
  - NIRSpec: spectra of $\sim$4,500 red giants and supergiants at S/N~80, sufficient for 16 elements at $\lesssim$0.05 dex precision and radial velocities to a few km s$^{-1}$.
  - NIRCam: $\sim$1,600 arcmin$^{2}$ in up to ten filters, reaching $\sim$20 million stars, every field imaged twice a year apart: two-epoch infrared photometry of the $\sim$500 Cepheids and $\sim$10$^{4}$ Miras in the footprint.
  - MIRI: $\sim$450 arcmin$^{2}$ in five filters at the depth of PHANGS.
  The observations are predominantly organized as twelve "bricks", each of three adjacent fields including two NIRSpec/MOS pointings, in which one instrument is prime and a second observes in parallel, so that roughly 40% of the exposure time comes at no charge. The bricks sample the nucleus, bulge, major axis to 29 kpc, minor axis, and inter-arm disk. In addition, a 100-hour grid of short NIRCam pointings with MIRI in parallel covers the HST-PHAT footprint. The result would be the first chemodynamical, star-formation, and dust map of an $L^{\star}$ galaxy other than our own built from individual stars, at a scale no single-cycle program can reach.

</details>


### [37] [SimpleClock: a satellite-disciplined oscillator for astronomical instrumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16023)
*Albert Wai Kit Lau,Bruno Pollarolo,Juan Mena-Parra*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种面向天文实验的低成本、高稳定度时间频率参考源SimpleClock，样机硬件成本约500美元，并通过三台设备的相位比对实验验证了其用于低频VLBI等天文授时应用的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文实验（如低频VLBI）需要高稳定度的时间频率参考，但传统商用高稳时频设备成本高昂，难以大规模部署，因此需要一种低成本、易搭建且性能可满足需求的时间参考方案。

Method: SimpleClock由GNSS授时接收机、数字可调的10 MHz MEMS恒温振荡器、外部温控与自主控制部分组成，可输出10 MHz与秒脉冲参考；频率与相位反馈可独立启用，支持Galileo HAS的PPP、SBAS解算及独立运行。采用三台单元进行相位比对，以分离各组件噪声并表征锁定输出的稳定度，共在5种配置下各采集200000 s数据进行等价单台差分Allan偏差与时差分析。

Result: 在1000 s处，等价单台差分Allan偏差为3.5×10⁻¹³至1.0×10⁻¹²，时间偏差为115–447 ps；在1–50000 s平均时间范围内，最大等价时间偏差为143–1090 ps。

Conclusion: SimpleClock以极低成本和简洁结构实现了较高稳定度的时间频率输出，性能可支撑天文授时应用，包括低频VLBI。

Abstract: SimpleClock is a low-cost, high-stability timing reference designed for astronomical experiments that combines a global navigation satellite system (GNSS) timing receiver, a digitally tunable 10 MHz microelectromechanical systems (MEMS) oven-controlled oscillator, external thermal regulation and autonomous control. It provides 10 MHz and pulse-per-second references at an estimated prototype hardware cost of US\$500. Independently enabled frequency and phase feedback support precise point positioning (PPP) with Galileo's High Accuracy Service (HAS), satellite-based augmentation system (SBAS) solutions and standalone operation. Three-unit phase comparisons separate component noise and characterize disciplined-output stability over 200\,000 s recordings. Across five configurations, each analysed over 200\,000 s, equivalent single-unit differential Allan deviation and time deviation at 1000 s span $3.5\times10^{-13}$--$1.0\times10^{-12}$ and 115--447 ps, respectively. The maximum equivalent time deviation over averaging times from 1 to 50\,000 s spans 143--1090 ps. These results support applications in astronomical timing, including low-frequency very long baseline interferometry (VLBI).

</details>

Scott*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: Euclid近红外处理函数（NIR PF）在DR1中完成升级并处理超过1900平方度天区，产出71659幅定标近红外图像，天体测量精度平均10 mas、99%抖动优于20 mas，相对测光精度达0.9%（亮于19 mag），满足任务指标要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euclid任务需要把NISP仪器获取的原始观测转换为可供科学使用的定标图像与源级产品。继Q1首次实现之后，为支撑首个主要数据发布DR1，需对NIR PF在探测器级处理、定标、图像重建与质量控制等方面进行改进，并系统验证其是否达到任务规定的天体测量与测光性能。

Method: 对NIR PF进行多项改进：探测器级处理、定标流程、图像重建和质量控制。将改进后的链路应用于覆盖逾1900 deg²的DR1观测，生成71659幅定标NIR图像，并通过大规模天体测量与测光验证评估链路性能，统计抖动（dither）残差、仪器通量的时间变化、相对测光精度以及焦平面上的空间定标残差。

Result: 天体测量解算平均RMS为10 mas，三个NISP波段中99%的抖动均优于20 mas；仪器通量的时间变化通常校正到2 mmag RMS水平；在Y_E、J_E、H_E波段，亮于19 mag的源相对测光精度达0.9%；全样本焦平面上测光定标的残余空间变化约为10 mmag。

Conclusion: 尽管仍存在一些局限，DR1近红外成像产品在其覆盖的大面积天区上，其天体测量与测光性能与任务要求一致，NIR PF的DR1版本可作为后续数据发布的生产链路基础。

Abstract: The Euclid near-infrared processing function (NIR PF) converts the raw observations acquired by the Near-Infrared Spectrometer and Photometer (NISP) into calibrated images and source-level products. Following the first implementation delivered with the Euclid Quick Data Release 1 (Q1), the NIR PF has been revised for the first major Euclid Data Release (DR1), with improvements to detector-level processing, calibration, image reconstruction, and quality control. The DR1 processing includes observations covering more than $1900\,\rm{deg}^2$ and has produced $71\,659$ calibrated near-infrared (NIR) images. We assess the performance of the processing chain through extensive astrometric and photometric validation. The astrometric solution reaches an average root-mean-square (RMS) of 10 mas, remaining below 20 mas for 99% of the dithers in all three NISP bands. Temporal variations in the instrumental throughput are typically corrected to a level of 2 mmag RMS, while the relative photometric accuracy reaches 0.9% for sources brighter than 19 mag in $Y_\mathrm{E}$, $J_\mathrm{E}$, and $H_\mathrm{E}$, and the residual spatial variations of the photometric calibration across the focal plane are at the level of approximately 10 mmag for the entire sample. Despite some remaining limitations, the DR1 NIR imaging products are consistent with the required astrometric and photometric performance over the large area covered by this release.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍Euclid任务科学地面段（SGS）在DR1数据发布阶段的实现，包括组织结构、架构、处理功能与运行模式，核心为基于通用数据模型的Euclid档案系统（EAS）和基于基础设施抽象层的统一编排框架，并报告DR1生产活动的规模与验证策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euclid任务需通过大天区巡天与深度观测研究暗能量和暗物质本质，其科学目标对科学地面段（SGS）的数据处理与标定性能提出严格要求，因此需要一套分布式、可扩展、流程规范的SGS体系。

Method: 以分布式体系构建SGS，由科学运行中心、科学数据中心、仪器运行团队及支撑单元组成统一架构；核心为Euclid档案系统EAS（含分布式存储系统、数据处理系统、科学档案），并以基础设施抽象层实现工作流管理、资源抽象与分布式计算集群调度，配合通用开发环境支持软件开发、集成与部署；同时建立日常运行、标定与质量控制流程及DR1生产流程与验证策略。

Result: DR1生产活动跨九个科学数据中心运行约3500万个作业、消耗670万核时，表明SGS具备良好的稳健性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该SGS基础设施成功支撑了Euclid DR1的产出，验证了其组织架构、元数据与文件管理、编排调度体系在规模化数据处理中的有效性，为后续数据发布奠定基础。

Abstract: The Euclid mission is designed to address fundamental questions on the nature of dark energy and dark matter by mapping the extragalactic sky with a wide-area survey complemented by targeted deep observations. The required scientific performance places stringent demands on the Euclid Science Ground Segment (SGS), which transforms raw telemetry into calibrated, validated, and scientifically exploitable products. The SGS is a distributed system comprising the Euclid Science Operations Centre, the Science Data Centres, Instrument Operation Teams, and supporting Organisation Units, within a common architectural and procedural framework. This paper presents the SGS as implemented for Data Release 1 (DR1), describing its organisation, architecture, processing functions, and operational model. We outline the main components of the SGS common infrastructure. A key element is the Euclid Archive System (EAS), the central metadata repository based on a common data model. It comprises a Distributed Storage System, providing scalable storage of data files across the Science Data Centres; a Data Processing System used by the pipelines to register and access files and their lineage; and a Science Archive, distributing validated data products. Processing is coordinated by a common orchestration framework based on the Infrastructure Abstraction Layer, providing workflow management and resource abstraction of distributed compute clusters. Software development, integration, and deployment are supported by the Common Development Environment. We describe daily operations, calibration, and quality control, followed by the DR1 production campaign, including processing flows, validation strategy, and computational and data volumes. This infrastructure enabled Euclid DR1, involving 35 million jobs across nine Science Data Centres and 6.7 million core-hours, demonstrating robustness and scalability of the SGS.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文介绍Euclid任务NIR图像在天测定标中的方法与结果：建模并校正仪器畸变，通过Gaia DR3和内部一致性检验评估定标质量，在DR1中97%面积达到30 mas精度，超出DR1要求；剩余3%因persistence（余辉）校正问题精度下降，文中给出改进方法，并讨论解的稳定性与未来改进计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Euclid Data Release 1的近红外图像提供高精度天测定标，满足（并超过）DR1的精度要求，同时识别并解决影响定标精度的关键系统效应。

Method: 构建完整的天测定标流程：对仪器畸变进行建模与校正，实施质量控制测试，并以Gaia DR3星表作为外部参考、结合内部一致性指标，利用覆盖Euclid运行一年以上的数据评估天测残差；针对余辉（persistence）校正问题提出专门的改正方法。

Result: 在97%的总观测面积上天测精度达到30 mas，超过DR1要求；剩余约3%区域精度因persistence校正问题而下降；同时给出了定标解的时间稳定性、对源性质与仪器配置依赖性的分析。

Conclusion: 该天测定标方案在Euclid DR1中达到并超过精度指标，验证了畸变建模与质控流程的有效性；通过改正persistence问题可进一步改善边缘区域精度，论文还规划了未来数据发布中提升定标精度与效率的改进方向。

Abstract: This paper presents the methodology and results of the astrometric calibration applied to the Euclid Near-Infrared (NIR) images in the Euclid Data Release 1, focusing on the techniques used to model and correct instrumental distortions, as well as the assessment of calibration quality. This work details the calibration pipeline, including quality control tests, and the evaluation of astrometric residuals against the Gaia DR3 catalogue and internal consistency metrics based on data encompassing more than one year of Euclid operation: the achieved accuracy is 30 mas for 97% of the total area, thus exceeding the requirements for Data Release 1. For the remaining 3%, accuracy declines due to a persistence correction issue: a method to correct the issue is presented. Furthermore, we discuss the stability of the solution over time, its dependence on source properties and instrument configuration, and outline the improvements planned for future Euclid data releases to enhance the astrometric calibration accuracy and efficiency.

</details>

TL;DR: 利用Euclid卫星NISP仪器光度模式图像，基于Q1与DR1数据改进并验证近红外PSF模型，结果表明更新模型优于Q1版本且满足多项任务关键指标要求，但仍有优化空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euclid卫星NISP仪器的近红外测光依赖精确的点扩散函数（PSF）模型，而早期Q1阶段提供的PSF模型精度有限，需要通过更多数据改进近红外处理功能（NIR PF）中的PSF模块并细化现有模型。

Method: 使用Q1和DR1数据中单帧与组合帧探测到的源，拟合其PSF轮廓；评估若干常用的模型质量检验方法，并分析PSF随源在焦平面位置、观测波长和观测时间的变化；将PSF拟合测光与孔径测光等其他流量估计方法进行对比。

Result: 与孔径测光等方法对比显示，基于DR1更新后的PSF模型在流量估计上优于Q1模型提供的版本；结果在多个关键任务指标上与预期要求一致。

Conclusion: 当前PSF模型满足任务的多项关键需求，但仍存在进一步改进的空间，计划在后续版本中继续研究并实现优化。

Abstract: The images collected in photometric mode by the Near-Infrared Spectrometer and Photometer (NISP) instrument of the Euclid satellite are used to model the point-spread function (PSF) of a selection of sources detected in single and combined frames. We used Q1 and DR1 data to improve the development of the PSF module of the near-infrared processing function (NIR PF) and to refine the models currently provided. We explored a few methods normally used to evaluate the quality of the derived models and discuss possible variations related to the position of the sources in the focal plane and to the wavelength and time of the observations. The procedure of fitting the PSF profile, compared to other methods used for flux estimation, such as aperture photometry, indicates that updated models are better than those provided by Q1 images. The current results are in agreement with several key requirements defined for the mission, but there is still room for further improvements, which we plan to investigate and implement in future releases.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文综述Euclid任务顶层定标需求如何转化为定标计划、在巡天期间如何实施与维护，并评估其在DR1数据发布中的表现；同时给出Euclid与Roman、JWST及CSST之间的合成测光转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要系统检验Euclid的定标体系是否满足其严格的科学精度要求（尤其是分光测光定标、点扩散函数建模与剪切定标），并明确当前DR1数据的达标状况与后续改进方向。

Method: 梳理顶层定标需求到定标计划的转化路径及巡天期间的实现与维护机制；用DR1流水线配置统一重处理全部DR1数据；识别并刻画新发现的细微效应；针对DR2制定改正策略；构建与Roman、JWST、CSST的合成测光转换。

Result: DR1已满足或超过多数任务末期分光测光定标需求；光谱完备且纯净的样本构建依赖Euclid Deep Survey的持续观测；PSF建模与剪切定标仍在向严格精度要求收敛。DR1重处理揭示出更多细微效应，并已在绝对流量定标、天体测量、电荷持续效应改正、快门效应、波长定标和水冰污染的分光测光改正等方面形成DR2改正方案。

Conclusion: Euclid定标体系在DR1阶段已基本达标，但剪切定标与PSF建模、以及深场光谱样本仍待完善；DR2将通过一系列针对性改正进一步提升定标精度，并为与其他空间望远镜的联合测光分析提供转换基础。

Abstract: This paper reviews how Euclid's top-level calibration requirements were translated into a calibration plan, how this plan is implemented and maintained during the survey, and how it performs in the current DR1 release. We show that DR1 already meets or exceeds most end-of-mission spectrophotometric calibration requirements. The construction of spectroscopically complete and pure samples relies on ongoing data acquisition for the Euclid Deep Survey, while point-spread-function modelling and shear calibration are still converging to their stringent accuracy requirements. With all DR1 data reprocessed with the DR1 pipeline configuration, more subtle effects have been detected and characterised, and correction strategies have been developed for DR2. These include improved absolute flux calibration, astrometry, charge-persistence correction, shutter effects, wavelength calibration, and spectrophotometric corrections for water-ice contamination. Finally, synthetic photometric transformations between Euclid and each of Roman, the James Webb Space Telescope, and the Chinese Space-station Survey Telescope, are presented.

</details>

TL;DR: Euclid 在轨三年后在热光稳定性、杂散光抑制、出气和指向等方面性能良好：杂散光整体低、指向优异、温控响应可预测，但仍需持续监测水冰污染与微流星体撞击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euclid 任务以热光与机械稳定性、杂散光抑制、低出气和高精度指向为核心设计要求，在轨三年后需用实际数据检验这些设计是否达标并总结经验。

Method: 综述 L2 晕轨道设计与消耗品预算；分析太阳杂散光随时间的增长并推断其来源；统计高太阳活动造成的数据丢失；用 sigmoid 曲线拟合光学元件对热阶跃的响应；介绍水冰污染的探测与去污方法并梳理污染历史；以高精度黄道光测量检验灰尘分布视点调制和发射前杂散光估计；用遥测数据检测太阳辐射压与热辐射反冲；统计 34 次微流星体撞击的光学与力学特征。

Result: 太阳杂散光每年增加约 14%，与微流星体穿孔一致但总体仍低；第 25 太阳活动周峰值后数据丢失明显下降；光学元件热响应呈 sigmoid 增长，21–46 小时后进入渐近平衡；以 8σ 显著性探测到 L2 晕轨道天平动引起的黄道光调制；发射前估计高估了视场外杂散光，H 波段 4 倍、I 波段 27 倍；遥测显示指向优异，并显著探测到太阳辐射压及暗面热辐射反冲；给出 34 次微流星体撞击及最小可探测撞击体质量 5×10⁻⁷ g。需要继续监测水冰污染。

Conclusion: Euclid 在轨表现总体符合或优于设计预期，热光稳定性、杂散光抑制与指向达到高水平，但水冰污染与微流星体撞击需长期监测；发射前杂散光估计与在轨实测也存在系统性偏差，有待修正。

Abstract: The Euclid mission was designed around the need for thermo-optical and mechanical stability, stray-light suppression, low outgassing, and precise guiding. After three years in space, a wealth of data is available to assess the performance in these areas. We review the L2 halo-orbit design and consumables budget. Solar stray-light gradually increases by 14\% per year and is consistent with meteoroid puncturing, but remains low overall. Data loss from high solar activity has declined notably since the maximum of the 25th Solar cycle passed. The thermodynamical response of the optical elements to a thermal step impulse follows sigmoidal growth curves, entering the asymptotic equilibration regime after 21--46 hours. Water-ice contamination has affected data from both instruments and requires continued monitoring; we review detection and decontamination methods, and Euclid's contamination history. High-precision measurements of the zodiacal background reveal, at eight sigma significance, the subtle modulation caused by the changing perspective on the dust distribution due to the L2 halo-orbit libration. These data also show that conservative pre-launch estimates overestimated the out-of-field stray-light by factors of 4 (H band) to 27 (I band). Telemetry data demonstrate excellent guiding, and enable highly significant detections of solar radiation pressure and of thermal-emission recoil from Euclid's unilluminated dark sides. Lastly, we present a list of 34 meteoroid impacts, discuss their mechanical and optical signatures, and derive a minimum detectable impactor mass of 5e-7 g.

</details>


### [44] [Direct estimation of the lunar-solar precession with geodetic very long baseline interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16549)
*Oleg Titov,Angelina Osetrova*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 基于VLBI时延率观测估计了岁差参数与章动系数，并纳入自由核章动效应，构建了一个由瞬时自转极（IRP）到GCRS的完整转换矩阵经验模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用VLBI时延率可独立于行星运动理论测定地球瞬时自转极分量，从而独立估计岁差参数与章动系数，构建ITRS到GCRS的完整转换矩阵，并进一步纳入自由核章动效应形成简洁解析模型。

Method: 利用VLBI时延率观测对X_IRP、Y_IRP的多项式展开系数作最小二乘拟合以估计岁差参数；同时估计章动系数，并从VLBI群时延中提取自由核章动效应，构建IRP指向（而非CIP）的解析模型。

Result: 得到月日岁差参数估计值ψ_A=5038.500±0.019″/cy（参考历元2000.0），与IAU2006采用的5038.4815″/cy接近；新模型可给出任意历元的IRP位置时间序列，并与IAU2006类似的解析形式。

Conclusion: 新模型以IRP为指向，无需引入无旋转原点概念即可构建ITRS到GCRS的转换矩阵；所得岁差参数与IAU2006一致，且可简洁解析地描述任意历元的自转极位置。

Abstract: Geodetic very long baseline interferometry (VLBI) is capable of measuring the Earth's instantaneous rotation pole (IRP) components (hereafter, $X_{IRP}$ and $Y_{IRP}$) with sufficient accuracy using the VLBI delay rate. The least-squares fit of the $X_{IRP}$ and $Y_{IRP}$ polynomial expansion coefficients produces the precession model parameters independently of planetary motion theories. Our aim was to estimate the precession parameters using VLBI delay rate observations as well as the nutation coefficients in order to produce a full transformation matrix from the IRP to the Geocentric Celestial Reference System (GCRS). Then, the goal was to incorporate the free core nutation (FCN) effect from the VLBI group delay to form a complete and simple analytical model. We obtained an empirical model of the transformation from International Terrestrial Reference System (ITRS) to Geocentric Celestial Reference System (GCRS) based on observational results. Similar to the IAU2006 theory, the new model produces time series of $X_{IRP}$ and $Y_{IRP}$ angles, but these two angles now point out the direction of the IRP rather than the celestial intermediate pole (CIP). A simple analytical model defines the IRP location for any epoch during the interval of observations. Therefore, the non-rotation origin concept is not required for the transformation matrix construction. The estimate of the lunar-solar precessional parameter based on analysis of VLBI delay rate is $ψ_{A}= 5038.500\pm 0.019"$/cy (referred to the observational epoch 2000.0), which is close to the adopted IAU2006 precessional parameter value $ψ_{A}= 5038.4815"$/cy.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文利用地面定标与在轨数据联合表征Euclid NISP红外探测器的persistence（持续性/残影）效应，建立其随时间的经验模型，并评估其对科学观测与数据处理管线的影响，提出Q1与DR1发布所用的掩膜策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Persistence是红外观测中重要的系统误差来源，会直接影响Euclid近红外光谱仪与光度计（NISP）的科学性能，因此需要对其进行表征与建模，并明确受影响的像元及剔除时长。

Method: 结合地面定标数据与在轨飞行数据对persistence进行表征；构建描述其时间演化的经验模型；分析其对平场观测、科学曝光本底、暗电流定标数据以及高能粒子击中事件的影响；并据此制定掩膜策略。

Result: Persistence在焦平面上高度不均匀，探测器之间及探测器内部差异可达两个数量级；其幅度通常低于激励信号的1%，且与前序注量呈非线性关系，亮度越高的源衰减越快。任务运行期间persistence信号逐步下降约10%，衰减速率基本保持稳定。过饱和信号后的persistence不遵循欠饱和注量所导出的经验关系。科学观测中测得的persistence与暗定标数据所得存在系统性差异，高能粒子击中产生的persistence也与同等信号的天然天体源不同。

Conclusion: 本文给出了Euclid NISP persistence的综合表征与经验模型，阐明了其多方面影响，并描述了Q1和DR1发布采用的掩膜策略，重点在于识别受persistence影响的像元及其应从科学观测中剔除的时长。

Abstract: Detector persistence is a significant source of systematic error in infrared observations and affects the scientific performance of the \Euclid Near Infrared Spectrometer and Photometer (NISP). We characterise persistence using complementary ground calibration and in-flight data, develop empirical models of its temporal evolution, and assess its impact on science observations and the \Euclid data-processing pipeline. Persistence is highly non-uniform across the focal plane, with variations of up to two orders of magnitude between and within detectors. Its amplitude is typically below 1\% of the stimulus signal and depends nonlinearly on the preceding fluence. The decay rate is faster for brighter sources. During mission operations, the persistence signal has progressively decreased by roughly 10\%, while the decay rate has remained approximately stable. Persistence following over-saturating signals does not follow the empirical relations derived for under-saturating fluences and plausibly reduces persistence response over longer timescales. The impact of charge trapping and release on flat-field observations and on the background in science exposures is also discussed. Persistence measured in science observations is observed to differ systematically from that derived from dark calibration data. In addition, persistence of energetic particle hits is observed to be different from that of astronomical sources of comparable signal. Finally, we discuss the impact of persistence on \Euclid NISP observations and describe the masking strategy adopted for the Q1 abd DR1 releases, focusing on identifying which pixels are affected by persistence and for how long they should be excluded from science observations.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用探测器响应的参数化方式来评估模拟阵列配置的性能相关量，替代GEANT4全模拟；论文给出了该软件的实现、所开展的验证检查，并将该工具应用于一个代表性的未来地面水切伦科夫探测器阵列。

Result: 给出了参数化软件的实现与验证检查结果，并在代表性未来地面水切伦科夫探测器阵列上完成应用展示（摘要未给出具体数值结果）。

Conclusion: 参数化探测器响应可作为一种计算上可行的替代方案，用于评估大规模阵列配置的重建性能、伽马/强子分离与灵敏度，尤其适用于GEANT4全模拟资源难以承受的高能、大填充因子情形。

Abstract: Surface arrays detecting the secondary particles of Extensive Air Showers (EAS) are one of the techniques used to detect gamma-rays from high-energy sources. To evaluate the reconstruction performance, gamma/hadron separation, and sensitivity of such experiments, many simulations of gamma rays and protons are required. The usual approach is divided into two steps. First, the CORSIKA software is used to generate EAS at the observation level. Then, a GEANT4-based full simulation code is used to simulate the array response. For very large fill factors, above 30-100 TeV, the computational resources required become too demanding. In this work, the authors present an alternative way to evaluate the performance-related quantities of a simulated array configuration through the parametrization of the detector response. We will present the implementation of such software, the validation checks we performed, and the application of this tool to a representative future ground-based water-Cherenkov detector array.

</details>

*Anna C. Tsai,Dylan L. Jow,Ue-Li Pen*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了在脉冲星距离约束较差的情形下，脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）对单个天体物理引力波源的定位精度，推导出残余响应的角度误差随信噪比变化的解析标度律，并与IPTA DR2的实际天区覆盖精度进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTA正逐步具备探测单一天体物理引力波源的能力，但能否以及多精确地在天球上定位此类源，尤其是当脉冲星距离远未确定、相关信息受限时，尚缺乏清晰的理论刻画与定量估计。

Method: 采用简化的匹配滤波与成图（map-making）模拟，计算内禀PTA残余响应对信噪比的角定位精度；从第一性原理推导误差标度律的前置因子，并与高斯波束情形对比；随后用IPTA DR2的脉冲星配置评估全天各方向的定位精度分布。

Result: 角度误差中位值随信噪比按 √(ln2/3)/[SNR√(ln SNR)] 减小；SNR=6时由卷积核奇异核心带来的对数项使角度误差约为高斯波束的1/1.4。IPTA DR2在SNR=6时：内禀阵列精度为3.3°，全天中位精度约8.9°（因脉冲星残余响应采样稀疏），但在14%的天区上因局部脉冲星密度高而优于3.3°。

Conclusion: PTA单源定位精度由信噪比与残余响应核的形态共同决定，存在可解析描述的下限；实际阵列受脉冲星空间采样限制，全天精度不均，但部分天区可达到甚至超过理想内禀精度。

Abstract: We address the astrometric precision with which a pulsar timing array (PTA) can localize a single astrophysical gravitational-wave source, in the regime where pulsar distances are poorly constrained compared to the gravitational wavelength. Using simple matched-filter, map-making simulations, we calculate the astrometric precision of the intrinsic PTA residual response as a function of the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), where the SNR is that of the source in the matched-filter map, and find that the median angular error decreases as $\sqrt{\ln 2/3}\,/[\mathrm{SNR}\sqrt{\ln\mathrm{SNR}}]$ over the relevant SNR range, with a pre-factor we derive from first principles. At an SNR of 6, this log term coming from the singular core of the convolution kernel produces an angular error approximately $1.4$ times smaller than for a Gaussian beam. We compare this sky-averaged maximum precision with the precision achieved across the sky by the pulsars of the International Pulsar Timing Array (IPTA) DR2. For a map with SNR of 6, the intrinsic array precision is $3.3^\circ$. For the IPTA configuration at the same SNR, the median precision over the sky is worse due to sparse sampling of the underlying pulsar residual response, approximately $8.9^\circ$; however, for sources in $14\%$ of the sky, the high local pulsar density yields a precision better than $3.3^\circ$.

</details>
