<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MKID作为低温探测器技术在射电天文与宇宙微波背景刻画中潜力巨大，但大规模阵列需要复用读出，多载波叠加会产生高峰均比，压缩电子链路的有效动态范围，因此需要改善激励与读出效率。

Method: 梳理大规模MKID阵列复用读出实验对读出系统的总体性能需求；聚焦载波激励的峰均比优化问题，提出一种对载波相位生成进行优化的算法以降低峰均比。

Result: 所提出的相位优化算法可获得较低的峰均比，有利于更充分地利用电子链路的动态范围，支撑大规模阵列的复用激励与读出。

Conclusion: 峰均比优化是MKID大阵列复用读出中的关键环节，通过优化载波相位生成可有效降低峰均比并提升读出系统动态范围利用率，为射电天文与CMB观测中的大规模MKID阵列应用提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Microwave kinetic inductance detectors (MKID) are a growing technology of cryogenic detectors and have a great potential in radio-astronomy and cosmic microwave background (CMB) characterization. A review of the general readout performance requirements for experiments requiring the multiplexing of large arrays (~10.000 pixels) of such cryogenic detectors is presented. The focus is on the crest factor optimization process for proper excitation and readout of these large arrays of detectors. It is shown an algorithm to optimize the phase generation of the carriers to obtain a low crest factor, favoring the dynamic range usage of the electronic chain.

</details>


### [50] [Peak Performance in Matched Filtering: Fused Correlation and Statistically Controlled Screening on CPUs and GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17229)
*Alexander H. Nitz,Keisi Kacanja,Collin D. Capano*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 将共轭乘积融合进首级逆变换蝶形运算、峰值归约融合进末级蝶形，并对批次做2D分块复用缓存，使大批量匹配滤波从DRAM带宽受限转为片上缓存带宽受限；进一步提出基于频谱功率剖面的分层筛选方法，在8种CPU架构和2块GPU上实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模模板库上的批量匹配滤波与互相关是引力波天文、地震学和射电瞬变搜索的主要计算开销。现有流水线把通用逆FFT与独立的乘法、寻峰核串接，每个模板都要把中间频谱和完整相关序列写入内存，受DRAM带宽限制、效率低。同时，信噪比往往集中在低频，而变换长度由高频决定，存在可提前筛选的冗余计算。

Method: 1) 融合：把共轭乘积并入第一步逆变换蝶形，把峰值归约并入最后一步蝶形，仅让候选峰离开寄存器；2) 2D分块批处理，使K²个相关复用2K个缓存频谱；3) 分层筛选：先做一系列短的低频变换，再评估全频带，阶段阈值由频谱功率剖面与匹配滤波噪声自协方差推导，以命中给定的误剔除概率目标；4) 在开源matchedfilter库中跨8种CPU架构及Vulkan/AMD Radeon 8060S、Metal/Apple M2实现验证。

Result: 在AMD Ryzen AI Max+ 395、N=4096下，仅峰融合滤波单核比AOCL-FFTW独立逆变换快1.4倍，比8060S上rocFFT快6.2倍，比M2上MLX FFT快3.1倍。在基准引力波搜索（ρ_th=5.5、误剔除目标1e-2）中，分层筛选相对AOCL-FFTW（CPU）加速26倍、相对rocFFT（8060S GPU）加速21倍、相对MLX FFT（M2 GPU）加速19倍。

Conclusion: 通过核融合、缓存分块与统计阈值驱动的分层筛选，可把批量匹配滤波的计算范式由内存带宽瓶颈转为片上缓存带宽瓶颈，并跨多种CPU/GPU架构获得数量级加速，适合大规模模板库的实时搜索场景。

Abstract: Batched matched filtering and cross-correlation over large template banks are a primary computational cost in gravitational-wave astronomy, seismology, and radio transient searches. Standard pipelines pair a general-purpose inverse Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) with separate multiplication and peak-finding kernels, writing intermediate spectra and full correlation series to memory for every template. We fuse the conjugate product into the first inverse-transform butterflies and the peak reduction into the last so only candidate peaks leave processor registers, and tile batches in 2D so $K^2$ correlations reuse $2K$ cached spectra. This shifts filtering from a DRAM-bandwidth-bound to an on-chip cache-bandwidth-bound regime. In some situations, the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) is concentrated toward lower frequencies while higher frequencies dominate the transform length. We develop a hierarchical screening method that exploits this via a sequence of short low-frequency transforms before evaluating the full band, deriving stage thresholds from the spectral power profile and matched-filter noise autocovariance to target a specified false-dismissal probability. We demonstrate these methods in the open-source matchedfilter library across eight CPU architectures and two integrated GPUs (AMD Radeon 8060S via Vulkan and Apple M2 via Metal). On an AMD Ryzen AI Max+ 395 at $N=4096$, fused peak-only filtering is $1.4\times$ faster than a standalone AOCL-FFTW inverse transform on one CPU core and $6.2\times$ faster than rocFFT on its Radeon 8060S GPU ($3.1\times$ over MLX FFT on the M2 GPU). For a fiducial gravitational-wave search ($ρ_\mathrm{th}=5.5$, $10^{-2}$ false-dismissal target), hierarchical screening yields a $26\times$ speedup over AOCL-FFTW on CPU, $21\times$ over rocFFT on the 8060S GPU, and $19\times$ over MLX FFT on the M2 GPU.

</details>


### [51] [Estimating distances from parallaxes. VII. Computing the three-dimensional kinegeometric posterior with one-dimensional sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17231)
*Coryn A. L. Bailer-Jones*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文将运动学几何后验（kinegeometric posterior）重写为"距离1维后验 + 速度2维高斯混合"的1D+2D表示，使采样更快、更准确，便于对大规模恒星样本进行计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视差质量较差时，可借助已知的恒星物理速度约束、结合自行来改进距离估计。此前的工作通过MCMC显式采样这个三维（距离+速度）后验，计算代价高、难以推广到大量源。

Method: 该后验是视差与自行的协变高斯似然，与由银河系模型构造的距离-速度先验之乘积。作者推导证明该3D后验可等价分解为距离上的1维后验，再与速度方向上的2D高斯分布之和（混合）相结合。

Result: 1D+2D表示在采样时比原MCMC显式采样更快且更准确，从而大幅降低了为大量源计算kinegeometric后验的难度。

Conclusion: kinegeometric后验具有可分解结构，可用更高效的1D+2D形式替代MCMC，适合对Gaia等大规模星表进行距离与速度的联合推断。

Abstract: When parallaxes are of poor quality, proper motions can improve stellar distance estimates using known constraints on stars' physical velocities. Formally this may be achieved via the kinegeometric posterior, a three-dimensional (3D) posterior in distance and velocity for given measured parallax and 2D proper motion. This posterior is the product of a covariant Gaussian likelihood in the parallax and proper motion with a distance and velocity prior that can be constructed from a Galaxy model. In earlier work, I showed how to infer this posterior for stars in Gaia DR3 by explicitly sampling it with a Markov Chain Monte Carlo method. Here I show that this posterior is equivalent to a 1D posterior in distance combined with a sum of 2D Gaussian distributions in velocity. This 1D+2D representation is both faster and more accurate to sample, thereby making the computation of the kinegeometric posterior for a large set of sources much easier.

</details>


### [52] [Global Fit Inference for LISA with samsara++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17266)
*Lorenzo Valbusa Dall'Armi,Gabriele Astorino,Riccardo Buscicchio,Angelo Ricciardone,Walter Del Pozzo*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了一种名为 samsara++ 的 C++/CUDA 采样器，用于解决 LISA 引力波数据的全局拟合（Global Fit）跨维度问题，能够对未知数量的引力波源、源参数和仪器噪声进行联合推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA 将观测到多个引力波信号的叠加，需要同时推断未知数量的源、参数以及仪器噪声。这些信号在时间和频率上都相互重叠，因此必须进行全局拟合分析。现有方法缺乏能高效处理跨维度全局拟合的计算工具。

Method: 基于 samsara 的连续时间 MCMC 框架，samsara++ 结合了用于增加和移除源的 birth-death 过程与用于参数估计的 mutation 移动；birth 和 death 通过依赖后验的速率来选择。采用共享内存并行、增量式似然更新和索引存储降低计算与内存开销，并用并行回火改善多峰后验的探索。

Result: 在包含 143 和 866 个银河系双白矮星双星的一年期模拟 LISA 数据集上，从空源模型出发实现了源重构和星表生成，并揭示了源数量、重叠信号和不完全混合对联合源-噪声推断的影响。在包含 16,435 个双星、对应 131,480 个源参数的种群压力测试中，约 16 小时完成 1500 万次迭代，使用约 3 GB 内存。

Conclusion: 这些基准测试表明，birth-death 采样对大规模银河系双星种群在计算上是可行的，并为将 samsara++ 扩展为完整的 LISA 全局拟合奠定了基础。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will observe a superposition of gravitational-wave signals, requiring joint inference of an unknown number of sources, parameters, and instrumental noise. To disentangle these contributions, that overlap in both time and frequency, a Global Fit (GF) analysis is needed. We present samsara++, a C++/CUDA sampler designed to address this trans-dimensional GF problem. Building on the continuous-time MCMC framework of samsara, the algorithm combines birth-death processes for adding and removing sources, with mutation moves for parameter estimation. Birth and death are selected through posterior-dependent rates. Shared-memory parallelism, incremental likelihood updates, and indexed storage reduce the computational and memory costs, while parallel tempering improves the exploration of multimodal posteriors. We assess the performance using simulated one-year LISA datasets containing Galactic double white dwarf binaries and stationary Gaussian instrumental noise. Analyses of populations containing 143 and 866 binaries start from empty source models, without preliminary searches or proposals tailored to the injected population. These tests demonstrate source reconstruction and catalog generation, highlighting, at the same time, the importance of source-count, overlapping signals, and incomplete mixing on joint source and noise inference. Then we perform a separate stress test, initialized at the injected parameters, exploring a population of 16,435 binaries, corresponding to 131,480 source parameters. This run completes 15 million iterations in approximately 16 hours using about 3 GB of RAM. All together, these benchmarks show the computational feasibility of birth-death sampling for large Galactic-binary populations, and provide a foundation for extending samsara++ toward a comprehensive LISA global fit.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文详细描述了Euclid任务中NISP无缝光谱(SIR)定标流水线，用于为DR1科学流水线生成定标产品，并验证其满足任务指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euclid任务旨在通过测绘宇宙大尺度结构研究暗能量与暗物质。NISP近红外光谱仪产出的光谱由SIR处理功能归算，但无缝光谱数据本身复杂，无法仅靠科学流水线自定标，因此必须依赖专门指向序列与观测天区构建独立的SIR定标流水线，以保证归算光谱在波长和流量上满足要求。

Method: 为DR1构建并描述专用的SIR定标流水线，采用特定指向序列和观测天区获取定标数据；核心难点与策略是将整个焦平面作为统一整体进行全局建模，以维持全视场内天体测量与光谱的连续性；随后对定标产品及由此产生的科学光谱进行综合分析。

Result: DR1在红、蓝两种棱栅模式下共提供超过1.7亿条经过验证的光谱；对定标产品与科学光谱的全面分析表明，当前输出成功满足任务要求，同时识别出若干可优化环节。

Conclusion: SIR定标流水线生成的定标产品可确保归算光谱在波长与流量上达标，全局焦平面建模策略有效；针对已识别的优化点，团队正持续开发以进一步提升未来数据发布的数据质量。

Abstract: The Euclid space mission is dedicated to probing the nature of dark energy and dark matter, through a comprehensive mapping of the Universe's large-scale structure. Central to these cosmological goals are the spectra produced by the Near-Infrared Spectrometer and Photometer (NISP) and reduced by the scientific pipelines of the SIR (Spectral near InfraRed) processing function. Due to the intrinsic complexity of slitless spectroscopic data, scientific observations cannot be self-calibrated by the science pipelines. Consequently, the development of dedicated SIR calibration pipelines, utilising specific pointing sequences and observation fields, is essential. The resulting calibration products are fundamental to ensuring that reduced spectra are characterised within requirements in both wavelength and flux. This paper provides a detailed description of the SIR calibration pipelines responsible for generating the products utilised by the science pipelines for Data Release 1 (DR1). A significant challenge in these calibration tasks arises from the requirement to model the entire focal plane as a unified entity. Such a global modelling approach is critical for maintaining astrometric and spectroscopic continuity across the entire field of view. DR1 provides more than 170 millions validated spectra in both red- and blue-grism modes. The accuracy of the reduced spectra is strictly dependent on the performance of the calibration pipelines. Comprehensive analyses of the calibration products and the resulting science spectra demonstrate that current outputs successfully meet the mission requirements. Nevertheless, specific areas for optimisation have been identified; ongoing development is focused on addressing these critical points to further enhance data quality for future data releases.

</details>

### [54] [FRIDA: Finding Rotation In ultra-Diffuse gAlaxies. I. Programme overview, resolved gas density profiles, and HI mass-size relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14635)
*Pavel E. Mancera Piña,Tom Oosterloo,Filippo Fraternali,Elizabeth A. K. Adams,Andrea Afruni,Cecilia Bacchini,Enrico M. Di Teodoro,Lexi Gault,Anastasia A. Ponomareva,Justin I. Read,Barbara Šiljeg*

TL;DR: 本文介绍FRIDA项目（MeerKAT与VLA对12个富气超弥散星系的高分辨率HI观测），给出样本、数据处理与HI流量/质量/面密度剖面结果，发现HI分布延展规则、中心平坦、外侧指数下降，且UDG在质量-尺度关系上偏上侧、更延展、平均面密度更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富气超弥散星系（UDG）是检验低表面密度下星系形成模型的有力探针，但其中性氢（HI）结构约束不足，限制了对该星系族群的理解。

Method: 依托FRIDA项目，利用MeerKAT和VLA对12个富气UDG开展高分辨率HI观测；论文介绍样本、观测与数据归算流程，测量HI流量、HI质量M_HI与分辨的面密度剖面Σ_HI(R)，并通过HI形态建模估计倾角，将样本置于M_HI–R_HI与M_HI–R_50,HI质量-尺度关系中，比较归一化剖面Σ_HI(R/R_HI)与累积质量分数M_HI(<R)/M_HI。

Result: UDG呈现半径约7–30 kpc的延展且规则的HI辐射，并在其中一个星系旁发现伴星系；多数UDG的Σ_HI(R)内区平坦、中心附近约4 M_⊙ pc^-2，外侧指数衰减；中位倾角约43°，与UDG选择效应下盘星系的理论预期一致；UDG落在质量-尺度关系散布内但系统性偏上侧，尺度约大15–25%、平均Σ_HI低约25–35%；归一化剖面介于晚型盘星系与早型星系之间，与Sa星系重叠较大。

Conclusion: 富气UDG的HI结构延展、规则且具有可分辨的平坦内核与指数外廓，其在质量-尺度关系上的系统性偏移及介于盘星系与早型星系之间的归一化剖面特征，为理解UDG的形成与盘星系选择效应提供了新的观测约束。

Abstract: Gas-rich ultra-diffuse galaxies (UDGs) provide powerful tests of galaxy formation models at low surface densities. However, the structure of their neutral atomic hydrogen (HI) remains poorly constrained, hampering our understanding of this population.
  We present FRIDA (Finding Rotation In ultra-Diffuse gAlaxies), a MeerKAT and VLA programme providing high-resolution HI data of 12 gas-rich UDGs. In this paper, we introduce our sample and delve into the associated observations and data reduction. We derive HI fluxes, masses ($M_{\rm HI}$), and resolved surface density profiles ($Σ_{\rm HI}(R)$). We find extended ($\sim 7-30\ \rm{kpc}$ in radius) and regular HI emission in our UDGs, and we report the discovery of a satellite around one of them. Most UDGs in FRIDA have $Σ_{\rm HI}(R)$ profiles with flat inner parts reaching $Σ_{\rm HI}(R)\approx 4\ M_\odot\ \rm{pc^{-2}}$ near their centres, which then decline exponentially in their outer parts. By modelling the HI morphology of the UDGs, we find a median inclination of $43^\circ$, in agreement with theoretical expectations for disc galaxies under UDG selection effects. Moreover, we place our UDGs in the $M_{\rm HI}-R_{\rm HI}$ and $M_{\rm HI}-R_{\rm 50,HI}$ mass-size relations, where $R_{\rm HI}$ is radius at which $Σ_{\rm HI}(R)=1\ M_\odot \rm{pc^{-2}}$, and $R_{\rm 50,HI}$ is the radius enclosing $50\%$ of $M_{\rm HI}$. While our UDGs lie within the scatter of the relations, they systematically fall on their upper side, being slightly (by $\sim 15-25\%$) more extended and of lower mean $Σ_{\rm HI}$ (by $\sim 25-35\%$) than average galaxies at similar $M_{\rm HI}$. Moreover, we find that $Σ_{\rm HI}(R/R_{\rm HI})$ and $M_{\rm HI}(<R)/M_{\rm HI}$ for UDGs follow an intermediate trend between late-type discs and early-type galaxies, with a large overlap with Sa galaxies.

</details>

The normalization $C(T_{\rm th})$ declines monotonically, saturating at $C_{\rm sat} \approx$ 0.35 km/s and implying a surface density of ~46 Msun/pc^2 under a virial approximation. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系形成模型常用对整个初始质量函数平均的连续能量注入率来代表超新星反馈，但在低质量暗物质晕中，单次离散超新星即可吹散比气体储量更多的气体，这一近似可能失效，需要定量评估其后果。

Conclusion: 匹配单位恒星质量释放的能量并不保证反馈等效，尤其是对于在首次超新星前仍保有气体的晕；反馈建模需考虑事件的离散性与时间延迟。

Abstract: Many galaxy formation models represent supernova feedback by a continuous energy injection rate averaged over the stellar initial mass function (IMF). This approximation can fail in low-mass dark matter halos, where single, discrete supernovae can unbind more gas than the reservoir holds. We investigate this using a one-zone model that characterises continuous and discrete supernova feedback at redshifts $z\geq5$. Both feedback modes inject the same expected energy per unit stellar mass formed. In the discrete model, individual supernovae are resolved and energy is injected instantaneously; in the continuous model, supernovae are IMF-averaged and energy injection is spread over a finite time interval. Once stars form, a supernova can unbind 1.5-2.7 times the gas present. Continuous feedback begins removing gas after $\sim 4$ Myr, whereas the first discrete supernova occurs after $\sim 10$ Myr, which produces a stellar mass excess before feedback takes effect. The excess persists because the reservoir responds nonlinearly: an event removes at most the gas present, and star formation halts once it is empty. It depends strongly on halo mass: from 135 per cent at $10^7\, \rm{M}_\odot$, to 15 per cent at $10^8\, \rm{M}_\odot$, to negligible at $10^9\, \rm{M}_\odot$ by $z=5$. Fixing the number and timing of supernovae does not remove the stellar excess, which indicates that it is not caused by stochastic IMF sampling. Instead, it vanishes when the same energy is divided among more, smaller events. Matching the energy released per unit stellar mass therefore does not guarantee equivalent feedback in halos that retain gas until the first supernova.

Abstract: Radial surface brightness profiles (SBPs) and color gradients are useful tools for studying galaxy structure, formation, and evolution. Using SKIRT radiative transfer models, we simulated simple Sérsic galaxies, with spherically-symmetric stellar densities, constant stellar-to-dust ratios, and a spatially constant spectral energy distribution (SED). We find that attenuated SBPs differ significantly from dust-free profiles, particularly in the central galaxy regions. These differences become more pronounced at bluer wavelengths and for higher core dust densities, leading to variations in the fitted Sérsic index of up to ~25%. This can lead to incorrect conclusions about the intrinsic stellar distribution and mimic a central mass deficit. For galaxies with very centrally-dense dust distributions, a core-Sérsic model results in a better fit of the attenuated SBP. This occurs even for a spatially invariant SED and can lead to wrong conclusions about intrinsic stellar populations and related astrophysical questions, such as their formation histories or the mechanisms of inside-out star formation quenching. A full 3D approach is necessary to properly model attenuation effects, which, if not accounted for, can bias inferences made about stellar populations, their evolutionary histories, and spatial distributions.

</details>

We combine high-resolution MUSE H$α$ kinematics in the inner region with high-, medium-, and low-resolution MeerKAT HI observations from the MHONGOOSE survey tracing the galaxy out to large radii. After accounting for an additional non-circular kinematic component identified in the central HI spectra, we construct an extended rotation curve to constrain the dark matter distribution. Measured as the 5th-to-95th percentile spread across the three galaxies analysed in detail, the empirical libraries recover variations of 1.11 and 0.98 dex, whereas the theoretical ones return 0.40 and 0.49 dex; the gas in the same regions spans 0.49 dex. Gas metallicities are indeed lower in the central star-forming regions, but the amplitude of that depression (about 0.3 dex in NGC 1097) exceeds what bar-driven inflow of disc gas can account for, given typical abundance gradients in literature. The age-metallicity relations consistently reveal two distinct components: an older population consistent with a self-enrichment scenario, and a younger population formed from gas inflows with a different chemical enrichment history. The empirical libraries produce a monotonic decline of metallicities from old to young populations, which would require a sustained inflow of metal-poor gas outpacing its own enrichment. The theoretical libraries instead recover a young component whose metallicity departs from the older populations only at the most recent ages and by a few tenths of a dex, compatible with a single dilution episode. Our results demonstrate that model choice critically affects inferred metallicities, with empirical stellar libraries recovering age-metallicity relations that demand physical conditions the gas measurements cannot sustain.

Conclusion: 大样本三维运动学示踪星给出自洽的银河系质量测量，表明模拟校准的系统偏差不可忽略；修正后银河系维里质量约9.2–9.8×10^11 M⊙，为星系形成与暗物质研究提供更可靠的观测约束。

Abstract: Measuring the mass of the Milky Way is essential for understanding galaxy formation and evolution, characterizing the Galactic potential and assembly history, and probing the nature of dark matter. We derive the enclosed mass profile of the Milky Way out to Galactocentric distances of $\sim80\,\mathrm{kpc}$ using two halo tracers with full 3D kinematics: RR Lyraes (RRLs) and blue horizontal-branch stars (BHBs). Our sample consists of 7,550 RRLs and 8,230 BHBs constructed from the Large Sky Area Multi-Object Fiber Spectroscopic Telescope (LAMOST), the Sloan Digital Sky Survey (SDSS), and the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI), spanning Galactocentric distances from $10$ to $100\,\mathrm{kpc}$. We estimate the enclosed mass using two independent methods: the spherical Jeans equation and the tracer mass estimator (TME). The two tracers yield consistent results, with $M(<81.7\,\mathrm{kpc}) = (4.76 \pm 0.23)\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ from RRLs and $M(<82.8\,\mathrm{kpc}) = (4.66 \pm 0.35)\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ from BHBs. Adopting an NFW profile to extrapolate the enclosed mass beyond the outermost radius probed by our tracers, we infer virial masses of $M_{200} = 8.12^{+0.72}_{-0.65}\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ from RRLs and $M_{200} = 8.13^{+0.78}_{-0.67}\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ from BHBs. We further validate our methodology using mock RRL and BHB samples constructed from two Auriga halos, finding that the inferred masses are underestimated by $15.5\%\pm2.8\%$ for RRLs and $11.9\%\pm3.4\%$ for BHBs, respectively. After correcting for these systematic errors, the Milky Way virial masses are $M_{200} = 9.79^{+0.93}_{-0.84}\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ from RRLs and $M_{200} = 9.24^{+0.95}_{-0.84}\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ from BHBs.

Abstract: In this work we present a new approach to derive HII region metal abundances based on sulfur emission lines. The new methodology is based on the derivation of the electron temperature of the double ionised sulfur, either by the direct measurement of the [SIII] auroral line or, at supersolar metalicities, from the $SO_{23}$ parameter. This new indirect method is able to extend the abundance gradient up to the very centre of the M101 galaxy (0.04 $R_e$). This new methodology mitigates systematic biases in direct abundance determinations based on auroral lines, while opening up new opportunities to improve commonly used strong line abundance calibrations in the intermediate to high metallicity regime.

Abstract: Omega Centauri ($ω$ Cen; NGC 5139), the likely stripped nucleus of a dwarf galaxy accreted by the Milky Way, is one of the few galactic nuclei resolvable into individual stars. The chromosome-map division separates two dynamically distinct components. The enriched stars are consistent with $ε=0$ in the line-of-sight velocities and are no more flattened than the full sample in the proper motions, and the two components show no strong difference in rotation. Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文基于Euclid DR1强引力透镜样本，利用PyAutoLens去混合测光数据，结合Phosphoros测光红移与Prospector SED拟合，构建了9538个星系尺度强透镜的光度红移与物理性质均匀星表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euclid将已知强透镜数量提升两个数量级，但标准星表测光会把前景透镜星系与背景被透镜源的光混合，导致推导出的星系参数可靠性受限，因此需要一套基于去混合测光的均匀物理性质星表。

Method: 对Euclid与地面图像的透镜建模（PyAutoLens）获得去混合多波段测光；用模板拟合代码Phosphoros估计测光红移；用Prospector进行SED拟合，采用灵活的非参数恒星形成历史与Salpeter初始质量函数，推断恒星质量、SFR、恒星年龄与金属丰度；并与光谱红移及外部星系巡天对比验证稳健性。

Result: 透镜样本红移覆盖0.13≲z_phot≲1.98，中位数z_phot=0.68(+0.33/-0.29)；样本以大质量宁静星系为主，M*范围为10^10–10^12.1 M⊙，中位数log10(M*/M⊙)=11.39(+0.29/-0.33)。

Conclusion: 该星表是Euclid时代首批大规模强透镜星系表征之一，通过去混合测光与均匀的测光红移及SED拟合分析，为星系演化研究、透镜星系标度关系以及强透镜中恒星质量与总质量关系的研究奠定基础。

Abstract: The Euclid mission is revolutionising the study of strong gravitational lenses by providing high-resolution imaging over an unprecedented survey area, increasing the number of known lenses by two orders of magnitude compared to previous surveys. Characterising this sample of lens-galaxies requires accurate measurements of their physical properties. However, standard catalogue photometry often blends the light from the foreground lens and the lensed background source, limiting the reliability of derived galaxy parameters. We present a homogeneous catalogue of photometric redshifts and physical properties for 9538 galaxy-scale strong lenses identified in Euclid's DR1 strong-lens sample. We use deblended multiwavelength photometry obtained from the lens modelling of Euclid and ground-based images with PyAutoLens. The photometric redshifts of the lens galaxies are estimated using the template-fitting code Phosphoros. The stellar masses, SFRs, stellar ages, and metallicities are inferred through SED fitting with Prospector, adopting a flexible non-parametric SFH and a Salpeter IMF. The robustness of the inferred parameters is assessed through comparisons with spectroscopic redshifts and external galaxy surveys. The sample of lenses spans a broad redshift range of $0.13\lesssim z_{\rm phot} \lesssim 1.98$, with a median $z_{\rm phot} = 0.68^{+0.33}_{-0.29}$. The sample is dominated by massive quiescent galaxies with a $M_\ast$ range $[10^{10},\,10^{12.1}]\,M_\odot$ and a median $\log_{10}(M_\ast/M_\odot) = 11.39^{+0.29}_{-0.33}$. This catalogue represents one of the first large-scale characterisations of strong-lens galaxies in the Euclid era. By combining deblended photometry with homogeneous photometric redshift and SED-fitting analyses, it provides a foundation for studies of galaxy evolution, lens-galaxy scaling relations, and the connection between stellar and total mass in strong lenses.

</details>


### [76] [Identification of extragalactic radio sources in the VISTA survey of the Magellanic Clouds using an unsupervised machine learning algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15880)
*Clara M. Pennock,Jacco Th. van Loon,Mohammed Iddrisu Nlowie,Elizabeth Joy Leese,Maja Nowaczyk,Heinz Andernach,Duncan Farrah,Valentin D. Ivanov,Michał J. Michałowski,Joana M. Oliveira,Jahang Prathap,Syed F. Rahman,Mara Salvato,Tayyaba Zafar*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用t-SNE无监督机器学习，结合光学到射电的多波段测光与颜色特征，在大、小麦哲伦云中分类了6,652个和1,356个源，成功区分恒星与河外天体、AGN与星系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 麦哲伦云拥有丰富的全电磁波段数据，但在如此密集的星场中提取河外源十分困难，传统方法难以有效分离恒星与河外天体。

Method: 采用无监督机器学习算法t-SNE，以VMC（VISTA麦哲伦云巡天）和EMU/ASKAP巡天探测到的源为样本，使用从光学到射电的多波段测光与颜色作为特征，将相似源在二维t-SNE图上聚类；并借助已分类源把簇划分为五类：AGN、低红移星系、耀变体、含尘恒星天体（如年轻恒星体、行星状星云等）和高自行恒星。

Result: 文献中已有的测光分类与所识别的特定簇相吻合，同时揭示了t-SNE图上尚无历史分类的区域；配套的SALT光谱观测还新发现了红移高达3.3的AGN以及麦哲伦发射线天体。

Conclusion: 无监督机器学习结合光学至射电测光能有效分离恒星与河外天体，并区分AGN与星系，是处理拥挤星场中源分类的有力工具。

Abstract: The Magellanic Clouds benefit from a wealth of data across the electromagnetic spectrum, but extracting extragalactic sources from these crowded fields is a less than straightforward endeavour. We used an unsupervised machine learning algorithm (t-distributed stochastic neighbourhood embedding; t-SNE) to classify 6,652 and 1,356 sources in the Large and Small Magellanic Clouds, respectively, that were detected in the VISTA Survey of the Magellanic Clouds (VMC) and the Evolutionary Map of the Universe (EMU) survey with the Australian Square Kilometre Array Pathfinder (ASKAP). Multi-wavelength photometry and colours from optical to radio were used as features to cluster similar sources together in 2D space on a t-SNE map. We used previously classified sources to separate the clusters into five classes: AGN, low-redshift galaxies, blazars, dusty stellar objects (e.g. young stellar objects, planetary nebulæ etc) and high proper-motion stars. Previous photometric classifications from the literature match with specific clusters identified, as well as provide insight on areas of the t-SNE map for which there are no previous classifications. This work shows that the combination of unsupervised machine learning with photometry from optical to radio has proven adept at separating the stellar from the extragalactic, and the AGN from the galaxies. Our associated new spectroscopy with SALT includes new discoveries of AGN up to redshift 3.3 and Magellanic emission-line objects.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文在Euclid DR1（2000 deg²）中发布15072个高置信度强引力透镜候选体（6073个A级+8999个B级），并另附9010个C级候选体；估计Euclid宽场巡天将有超过10万个高置信度候选体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强引力透镜此前多为少量偶发发现，样本小、选择不均匀，难以作为统计工具使用；Euclid高分辨率成像为大样本、均匀的星系尺度强透镜搜寻提供条件。

Method: 从DR1约9亿源中筛出6800万源，用专门流水线整合5个机器学习模型得分，并结合约7000名公民科学家与67位强透镜专家的目视分类进行分级；同时提供A+B级的自动透镜模型、偏折星系的光度红移与星族性质。

Result: 发现84个双源面透镜、超1000个盘状透镜候选，以及数百个被透镜化的AGN/类星体候选；偏折星系多为大质量宁静星系，log10(M*/M⊙)=11.39(+0.29/-0.33)（Salpeter IMF），光度红移z=0.68(+0.33/-0.29)，典型爱因斯坦半径0.85(+0.44/-0.24) arcsec。

Conclusion: 该样本候选体数量超过以往所有星表之和，使强引力透镜成为可用于天体物理与宇宙学的稳健统计工具；按面积外推，Euclid宽场巡天预期有超10万个高置信度强透镜候选体。

Abstract: We present 15 072 high-confidence strong lens candidates in Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1), spanning 2000 deg$^2$. These comprise 6073 grade A ('sure lens') and 8999 grade B ('probable lens') systems, which we publish alongside a further 9010 grade C ('possible lens') candidates. With more high-confidence candidates than all previous catalogues combined, this sample establishes a large, homogeneous data set of galaxy-scale strong lenses with high-resolution imaging, transforming strong lensing from a field of rare discoveries to a robust statistical tool for astrophysics and cosmology. Of the roughly 900 million sources in DR1, we searched the 68 million passing our catalogue selection using a bespoke discovery pipeline that synthesises scores from five machine-learning models with visual classifications from around 7000 citizen scientists and 67 strong lensing experts. Beyond classical Einstein rings, our catalogue includes 84 candidate double-source-plane lenses and over 1000 candidate disc lenses, two exotic classes with few previously known examples, as well as several hundred candidate lensed AGN and quasars. We deliver automated lens models for the combined grade A+B systems, and photometric redshifts and stellar population properties for the deflector galaxies, providing immediate science-ready data products. We find that the majority of the deflector galaxies are massive and quiescent, with stellar masses of $\log_{10}(M_\ast /M_\odot) = 11.39^{+0.29}_{-0.33}$ (Salpeter initial mass function; 16th-84th percentile range). The deflector galaxies span a range of photometric redshifts ($z=0.68 ^{+0.33}_{-0.29}$), extending to higher redshifts than previous ground-based lens samples, and the lenses have a typical Einstein radius of $0.85 ^{+0.44}_{-0.24}$ arcsec. Extrapolating by area, we expect over 100 000 high-confidence strong lens candidates in the Euclid Wide Survey.

</details>


### [78] [A statistical study of ionized outflows in low-mass galaxies from DESI DR1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15884)
*Michael Romano,Amélie Saintonge,Chiara Circosta,Claudia Cicone,Darko Donevski,Dirk Scholte,Bendix Hagedorn*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 基于DESI DR1，对约5800个低质量星系（logM*≲10, z<0.45）的[OIII]和Hα发射线进行统计分析，刻画电离气体外流的质量加载因子与速度，并考察其随恒星质量、sSFR及星系类型的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低质量星系中的反馈（外流）对重子循环和星系演化至关重要，但大样本、系统的观测约束仍然缺乏；同时宇宙学模拟预测的质量加载因子与观测之间存在显著差异，需要用大样本观测检验这些差异的来源。

Method: 利用DESI DR1光谱，基于[OIII]与Hα发射线识别约5800个具有稳健电离外流信号的低质量星系；计算质量加载因子η和外流速度；比较单源探测与一般低质量恒星形成种群的叠加光谱；并将结果与宇宙学模拟的预测进行对比。

Result: 质量加载因子介于η=0.001–1，中位数约0.01，与同恒星质量区间的以往研究一致，但比宇宙学模拟低1–2 dex；外流速度在约80%以上的源中足以富集环星系介质，而逃逸到星系际介质主要发生在复合（composite）和AGN宿主中；η随恒星质量下降，趋势与模拟大致相符但归一化偏低；叠加谱中的η系统性低于单源探测，差异随偏离恒星形成主序程度的增大而减小；η与sSFR呈正相关。

Conclusion: 电离相仅追踪了总外流质量的一小部分；增强的恒星形成和爆发性（burstiness）调节反馈效率并塑造星系风；电离外流在低质量星系重子循环中扮演重要角色，要完整理解反馈还需要多相介质的研究。

Abstract: We present a statistical analysis of ionized gas outflows in a large sample of low-mass ($\mathrm{log(M_{*}/M_{\odot})\lesssim10}$) galaxies at $z<0.45$ from the first data release of the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI DR1). Using [OIII] and H$α$ emission lines, we identify $\sim5800$ dwarf galaxies with robust signatures of ionized outflows. Most are star-forming systems above the star-forming main sequence, with a smaller fraction of composites and AGN hosts. The derived mass-loading factors span $η=0.001-1$, with a median $η\sim0.01$, consistent with previous studies at similar stellar masses. These values are $1$--$2$ dex lower than predicted by cosmological simulations, suggesting that the ionized phase traces only a minor fraction of the total outflow mass. Outflow velocities are sufficient to enrich the circumgalactic medium in $\gtrsim80\%$ of cases, while escape into the intergalactic medium appears efficient mainly in composite and AGN hosts. We find that mass-loading factors decrease with stellar mass, broadly reproducing simulated trends despite the normalization offset. Comparing individual detections with stacked spectra of the general low-mass star-forming population, we find systematically lower mass-loading factors in the stacks, although the difference decreases toward larger offsets above the star-forming main sequence. Together with a positive correlation between mass-loading factor and specific star-formation rate, this suggests that enhanced star formation and burstiness regulate feedback efficiency and shape galactic winds. Our results highlight the role of ionized outflows in the baryon cycle of low-mass galaxies and the need for multiphase studies to fully understand feedback in galaxy evolution.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文介绍在Euclid DR1数据中识别15,000个高等级星系—星系强引力透镜候选体的多阶段搜索方法：目录级筛选、机器学习集成搜索、两阶段公民科学搜索，以及强透镜研究者的分级评定，并测量了搜索完备度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强引力透镜本质上是稀有系统，而Euclid DR1数据集极其庞大，单一手段难以兼顾召回率与人工检查成本，因此需要设计分层、互补的搜索流程以最大化发现数量并最小化人工审核负担。

Method: 采用多阶段流程：(1) 目录级筛选；(2) 机器学习与集成搜索，由神经网络自动分类超过6800万个天体；(3) 两阶段公民科学搜索，志愿者对30万个天体完成逾450万次分类；(4) 强透镜研究者对超过3万个天体进行检查与分级；并对分级结果进行校准以修正个体检查者的偏差，同时评估透镜建模（ECLIPSE-C）在判定候选体性质上的作用。

Result: 共识别出15,000个高等级星系—星系强透镜候选体。综合搜索对A级（确定）系统的完备度为0.73_{-0.15}^{+0.08}，对A+B级（确定+可能）系统的完备度为0.34_{-0.06}^{+0.05}，即已识别DR1区域内大部分确定的强透镜系统，其余可能为更暗或更模糊的情形。

Conclusion: 多阶段方法能最大化识别透镜数量并最小化公民科学家与研究者的检查需求；研究者分级仍具主观性，需通过校准修正个体偏差，透镜建模有助于判定候选体性质。本文属于ECLIPSE系列，聚焦DR1候选体发现流程，透镜总体概览见ECLIPSE-A。

Abstract: We present the method used to identify the $15\,000$ high-grade galaxy-galaxy strong lens candidates in Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1). Given that lenses are intrinsically rare systems and the DR1 data set is vast, this lens search was conducted in a number of stages: a catalogue-level cut; a machine-learning and ensemble search; a two-stage citizen-science search, and finally grading of lens candidates by strong lens researchers. In this paper, we describe each search stage and their respective results. This search was enabled by the automated classification of over 68 million objects by neural networks, more than 4.5 million classifications of $300\,000$ objects by citizen-scientist volunteers, and the inspection of over $30\,000$ objects by strong lens researchers. We find this multi-stage approach helps maximise the number of lenses identified, while minimising inspection requirements of both citizen scientists and strong lens researchers. We measure a completeness in our comprehensive search of $0.73_{-0.15}^{+0.08}$ to grade A ('definite') systems and $0.34_{-0.06}^{+0.05}$ to grade A+B ('definite' + 'probable') systems. Our search therefore identifies the majority of the definitive strong lens systems in the DR1 area with the remainder likely to be fainter or more ambiguous cases. We find that researcher grading remains subjective; in this work we implement calibration to account for biases of individual inspectors and highlight the benefit of lens modelling (ECLIPSE-C) in determining the nature of a lens candidate. This paper forms part of the ECLIPSE series, here focussing on the discovery pipeline used to identify lens candidates in Euclid DR1; an overview of the lenses discovered is presented in ECLIPSE-A.

</details>

TL;DR: 该论文对Euclid DR1中的15 072个强引力透镜候选体进行了自动化建模（PyAutoLens-JAX），并由20余名专家分级评估模型质量，公开释放了ECLIPSE星表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系尺度强透镜能精确测量星系质量分布、放大遥远星系并提供宇宙学探针，但以往研究仅限于数百个透镜，自动化建模方法难以规模化，亟需在Euclid量级样本上实现可扩展的建模与验证。

Method: 使用PyAutoLens-JAX对15 072个Euclid DR1强透镜候选体做三种源模型拟合（多高斯展开MGE、像素化、Sérsic），采用带外部剪切的等温椭球质量模型；拟合实现前景透镜光减除、自适应Delaunay源重建与去混光电测。随后由20余名专家目视检查，按≥75%专家一致意见为每个模型分类。

Result: 在6073个A级（确证）候选中，4098个（67.5%）建模成功、580个（9.6%）失败、317个（5.2%）为多星系透镜、15个（0.2%）为双源面透镜、7个（0.1%）不确定、1032个（17.0%）未达共识、24个（0.4%）模型不完整；8999个B级（可能）候选成功率降至43.1%，主要因无共识比例升至37.6%，而一致失败率仅12.9%。成功建模的A、B级系统有效爱因斯坦半径中位数为0.85角秒，I_E波段总源放大率中位数为8.1。

Conclusion: 研究证明自动化透镜建模可扩展到Euclid样本量级，成功模型及其推断性质与放大的源重建通过ECLIPSE星表公开释放，支撑群体层面的强透镜科学研究。

Abstract: Galaxy-scale strong lenses provide precise measurements of galaxy mass distributions, magnified views of distant galaxies, and a competitive cosmological probe. Historically, such studies have been limited to hundreds of lenses, with automated modelling methods proving difficult to scale. We present automated lens modelling of 15 072 strong-lens candidates from Euclid Data Release 1 (DR1), over an order of magnitude larger than any previously modelled sample. Using PyAutoLens-JAX, we perform three fits with multi-Gaussian expansion (MGE), pixelised, and Sérsic representations of the source, adopting an isothermal ellipsoid mass model with external shear. These fits provide foreground lens-light subtraction, adaptive Delaunay source reconstructions, and deblended photometry. More than 20 expert inspectors visually assess the resulting models, and each model is assigned the classification on which at least 75% of its inspectors agree. Of the 6073 grade A (secure) lens candidates, inspectors classify 4098 (67.5%) as successfully modelled, 580 (9.6%) as failures, 317 (5.2%) as multi-galaxy lenses, 15 (0.2%) as double-source-plane lenses, and 7 (0.1%) as unsure if lens, while 1032 (17.0%) reach no consensus and 24 (0.4%) have incomplete models. For the 8999 grade B (probable) candidates, the modelling success rate falls to 43.1%, primarily reflecting an increase in cases without consensus (37.6%), rather than a comparable increase in agreed failures (12.9%). Successfully modelled grade A and B systems have a median effective Einstein radius of 0.85 arcsec and a median total $I_E$-band source magnification of 8.1. We publicly release the successful lens models, their inferred properties, and magnified-source reconstructions through the ECLIPSE catalogue, demonstrating automated modelling at Euclid sample sizes and enabling population-level strong-lensing science.

</details>

*Elizabeth Bryce,Alessandra Venditti,Jenna Samuel,Volker Bromm,Julian B. Muñoz*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用CampFIRE-6k宇宙学zoom-in模拟，系统考察了PopIII恒星形成历史与环境，发现其形成率在z~6之前近乎恒定（约10^-3.5 M☉/yr/cMpc³），且晚期仍出现在原子冷却晕中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PopIII恒星通常被认为在宇宙黎明时期于迷你晕中形成，之后被PopII取代；但模拟显示由于宇宙富集不均匀，PopIII可能在再电离时期仍以低速率形成。然而，晚期PopIII形成的速率与典型环境仍存在很大不确定性。

Method: 采用CampFIRE-6k宇宙学zoom-in模拟，追踪至z~6的PopIII恒星形成历史及其所处环境，并考察其在不同质量晕（含原子冷却晕）中的分布。

Result: PopIII恒星形成率在z=6之前近乎恒定，约为10^-3.5 M☉/yr/cMpc³；晚期PopIII恒星还出现在质量约10^7–10^8 M☉的原子冷却晕中。

Conclusion: 晚期PopIII恒星形成并非可忽略，其在原子冷却晕中的持续出现表明PopIII形成环境比传统认知更广泛，对再电离时期恒星形成与金属富集的认识具有重要影响。

Abstract: Population III (PopIII) stars are the first generation of metal-free stars, thought to form in minihalos ($M_\mathrm{halo} \sim10^5-10^6\,\mathrm{M_{\odot}}$) at Cosmic Dawn ($z\gtrsim15$) before largely giving way to Population II stars formed from the newly enriched medium. Simulations show, however, that PopIII stars may continue forming at low rates through the Epoch of Reionization (EoR, $z \sim 6 - 10$), due to the inhomogeneous enrichment of the Universe. Nonetheless, significant uncertainty remains on the rates and typical environments of late PopIII formation. We use the CampFIRE-6k cosmological zoom-in simulation to examine PopIII star formation history and environments through $z\sim6$. We find that PopIII stars form at a near constant rate ($\sim 10^{-3.5} ~\mathrm{M_{\odot}\,yr^{-1}\,cMpc ^{-3}}$) through $z=6$, including within atomic-cooling halos ($M_\mathrm{halo}\sim 10^7-10^8\,\mathrm{M_\odot}$) at late times.

</details>


### [82] [Anatomy of LyC Escape in MEGATRON: Connecting Feedback Physics to Demographics, Leakage Timescales, and Explorations of ISM Density as a Tracer of Escape Fraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15931)
*Nicholas Choustikov,Harley Katz,Taysun Kimm,Matthew J. Hayes,Aaron Smith,Crystal L. Martin,Shyam H. Menon,Larrance Xing,Joki Rosdahl,John Chisholm,Aayush Saxena,Martin P. Rey,Adrianne Slyz,Julien Devriendt,Alex J. Cameron,Anatole Storck,Corentin Cadiou*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用MEGATRON zoom模拟套件研究反馈物理如何同时调控高红移星系的UV源数量、LyC逃逸分数与逃逸电离光子产率，发现反馈通过"星暴-LyC逃逸延迟"决定再电离历史，并提出[O II]λ3727双线电子密度可作为f_esc的诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST揭示了大量高红移星系，但要将这些星系数量与宇宙再电离联系起来，必须约束其Lyman连续谱（LyC）逃逸分数f_esc。以往工作多单独研究UV光度函数或逃逸分数，缺乏对反馈物理如何同时调控源数量、逃逸分数与逃逸电离光度、进而产生不同再电离历史的系统认识。

Method: 采用MEGATRON zoom模拟套件，包含详细的辐射/化学计算与多种反馈方案；分析星暴与LyC逃逸之间的时间延迟、ISM结构与f_esc的关系，并检验[O II]λ3727双线电子密度及UV斜率定标作为f_esc/逃逸电离光度诊断的有效性。

Result: 反馈物理同时调控UV源计数、f_esc与逃逸电离光度，产生不同再电离历史；差异由星暴到LyC逃逸的延迟主导——早期高效反馈与低密度环境使光子在超新星爆发前的强电离星族阶段即可逃逸。UV亮星系较少的模型反而逃逸更早、再电离更早。UV暗星系随f_esc增大呈现共同的ISM演化序列：强泄漏者（1%≤f_esc≤50%）显示强光致电离与SNe反馈证据及星暴后恒星形成历史，极端泄漏者（f_esc≥50%）还清空了冷/密ISM。H II区附近[O II]双线电子密度越低对应越强泄漏者，可示踪f_esc的早期演化；而基于UV斜率的定标对单个年轻星系可将逃逸电离光度高估达两个数量级。

Conclusion: 在利用UV源计数解读再电离时必须考虑LyC泄漏的延迟占空比；[O II]λ3727电子密度是有前景的f_esc诊断量，而UV斜率定标不可靠。

Abstract: Constraining the Lyman Continuum (LyC) escape fraction ($f_{\rm esc}$) of high-redshift galaxies is essential to connect the abundant populations revealed by \textit{JWST} to cosmic reionization. We investigate this interplay using {\small MEGATRON}, a suite of zoom simulations with detailed radiation/chemistry and varied feedback prescriptions. We find that feedback physics simultaneously regulates UV source counts, LyC escape fractions, and escaping ionizing luminosities ($\dot{N}_{\rm ion,esc}$), producing different reionization histories. These differences are governed by the delay between a starburst and LyC escape: efficient early feedback and lower-density environments enable photons to escape while pre-supernovae stellar populations remain strongly ionizing. Consequently, models that produce fewer UV-bright galaxies yield earlier LyC escape and reionize sooner than models with more abundant UV sources and delayed LyC emission. Furthermore, UV-faint galaxies follow a common physical sequence in ISM structure with increasing $f_{\rm esc}$ irrespective of feedback physics: stronger leakers ($1\%\leq f_{\rm esc}\leq 50\%$) exhibit evidence of strong photoionization and SNe feedback alongside post-starburst star formation histories, while extreme leakers ($f_{\rm esc}\geq 50\%$) have also cleared their cold/dense ISM. We find that electron densities inferred from the $\rm [O\,{II}]\,λ\lambda3727$ doublet are a candidate diagnostic of $f_{\rm esc}$, with lower densities around $\rm H~{\small II}$ regions selecting stronger leakers. Moreover, this density can trace the early evolution of $f_{\rm esc}$, whereas UV slope-based calibrations over-predict $\dot{N}_{\rm ion,esc}$ for individual young galaxies by up to two orders of magnitude. Overall, the delayed duty cycle of LyC leakage must be accounted for when UV source counts are interpreted in the context of reionization.

</details>


### [83] [Mass Loss Rates of Evolved Stars in Isolated Dwarf Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15948)
*Narges Rostami,Hamidreza Mahani*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用自动化SED拟合流程SEDust估算NGC 6822中AGB星族的质量损失率与尘埃产生率，发现AGB星不足以解释该星系现今的尘埃储量，需要ISM尘埃生长等额外来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矮不规则星系NGC 6822是孤立系统，缺乏与外部相互作用带来的尘埃污染，是检验AGB星对星系尘埃贡献的理想实验室；而AGB星是否是早期星系尘埃的主要来源仍存争议。

Method: 使用自动化流程SEDust对AGB星的光谱能量分布（SED）进行拟合，从而估算单颗星及整体的质量损失率和尘埃产生率；再将总尘埃注入率按气尘比200换算，并与Herschel观测给出的尘埃质量比较。

Result: AGB星族总质量损失率为(3.7±1.1)×10⁻³ M☉/yr，对应尘埃注入率约2.0×10⁻⁵ M☉/yr；若持续10 Gyr，累计尘埃约2×10⁵ M☉，低于Herschel实测的2.7×10⁵ M☉。

Conclusion: AGB星单独无法解释NGC 6822现今的尘埃库，需要星际介质中尘埃生长等其他来源或过程来补足尘埃质量。

Abstract: We derive the mass-loss rates of asymptotic giant branch (AGB) stars in the isolated dwarf irregular galaxy NGC 6822 using SEDust, an automated pipeline that fits spectral energy distributions (SEDs) to estimate stellar mass-loss and dust-production rates. The total mass-loss rate of the AGB population is $(3.7 \pm 1.1) \times 10^{-3} M_\odot\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$. For a gas-to-dust ratio of 200, this corresponds to a dust injection rate of $2.0 \times 10^{-5} M_\odot\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$. If sustained over 10 Gyr, this rate would yield approximately $2 \times 10^{5} M_\odot$ of dust, which is lower than the dust mass of $2.7 \times 10^{5} M_\odot$ estimated from Herschel observations. AGB stars alone therefore cannot account for the present-day dust reservoir of NGC 6822, and additional sources or processes, such as dust growth in the interstellar medium, are required.

</details>

TL;DR: 利用Euclid Wide Survey首批数据（约1900 deg²），通过等时线过滤技术探测到15个可分辨恒星系统，包括5个全新发现和10个此前无可靠距离的矮星系，并测量了它们的基本参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在10 kpc至数Mpc范围内系统搜寻可分辨恒星系统，尤其是低表面亮度、极暗弱的星系与星团，此前受限于地面巡天深度与分辨率，这类天体（如M81群成员）几乎未被发现或缺乏可靠距离。

Method: 在Euclid Wide Survey首批数据（~1900 deg²）中应用等时线过滤（isochrone filtering）技术，筛选可分辨为恒星的系统；对15个系统测量距离模数，并对其中14个测量半光半径、椭率、位置角、绝对星等与恒星质量。

Result: 探测到15个此前未被分辨的系统：5个为新发现（3个位于银河系晕，2个在本地群之外），10个为浅地面巡天中作为未分辨源编目、缺乏可靠距离的矮星系。银河系晕中，Ursa Minor II（~20 kpc，Mv~+2.0，恒星质量~40 Msun）是最暗弱的恒星系统之一；Fornax 7 位于Fornax矮椭球星系潮汐极限附近，可能与其物理相关；Eridanus V（~165 kpc，~550 Msun）处于球状星团与超暗弱矮星系的交叠区。本地群之外12个矮星系距离3–5.5 Mpc，其中3个半光半径内平均表面亮度达到或超过mu_V~28 mag/arcsec²；10个为M81群候选成员，其中8个三维距离小于或相当于M81零速度面半径，2个靠近外围成员NGC 4236，其一可能是其卫星。

Conclusion: Euclid在单一、均匀巡天中实现了从银河系晕到本地体积的低表面亮度科学，展示出变革性潜力，可覆盖前所未有的环境范围。

Abstract: We searched within the first data release of the Euclid Wide Survey (~1900 deg^2) for resolved stellar systems between distances from 10 kpc and several Mpc, using an isochrone filtering technique. We detect 15 systems that have never been resolved into stars before. Five are new discoveries, three in the Milky Way halo and two beyond the Local Group. The remaining ten are dwarf galaxies that were previously catalogued as unresolved sources in shallower ground-based surveys and lack reliable distance measures. We measured distance moduli for all 15 systems, and half-light radii, ellipticities, position angles, absolute magnitudes, and stellar masses for 14 of them. In the Milky Way halo, Ursa Minor II, at ~20 kpc, has Mv ~ +2.0 and a stellar mass of ~40 Msun, and is one of the least luminous stellar systems known. Fornax 7 is located near the tidal limit of the Fornax dwarf spheroidal galaxy and may be physically associated with it. Eridanus V lies at ~165 kpc and has a stellar mass of ~550 Msun, which places it in the region of the size-luminosity plane where globular clusters and ultra-faint dwarf galaxies overlap. Beyond the Local Group, the 12 dwarf galaxies lie at distances of 3--5.5 Mpc. Three of them reach or exceed a mean surface brightness within the half-light radius of mu_V~28 mag/arcsec^2, a regime in which almost no galaxies at these distances were previously known. Ten of them are candidate members of the M81 group: eight have three-dimensional distances from M81 smaller than or comparable to the radius of its zero-velocity surface, and two lie close to the peripheral member NGC 4236, one of which is a candidate satellite of this galaxy. These results demonstrate Euclid's transformative potential for low-surface-brightness science, enabling the study of an unprecedented range of environments from the MW halo out to the Local Volume within a single, homogeneous survey.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文利用Euclid DR1光学图像，对约4086万个恒星形成星系应用基于HMC采样的贝叶斯推断框架，从投影形状中约束星系内禀3D椭率、三轴性与尺寸的群体统计特性，样本量比此前研究提升两个数量级以上，并确认了低质量端扁长比例随红移降低而下降的演化趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系内禀3D形状对理解其形成与演化至关重要，但从二维投影反推3D形状本身困难，且以往基于JWST、HST的形状推断研究受限于样本量，在低红移、低质量区间统计约束弱、分箱粗糙，难以判断所观测到的形状演化趋势（如扁长比例下降）是否只是小样本统计涨落。Euclid DR1的大样本为检验该方法的可扩展性与稳健性、并获得更精细分箱下的形状演化提供了契机。

Method: 采用贝叶斯推断框架，对Euclid DR1光学图像中恒星质量$10^{9}$–$10^{10.5}M_\odot$、红移$0<z<1.2$的40,864,304个恒星形成星系，利用Hamiltonian Monte Carlo（HMC）对Sérsic轴比与有效半径进行采样，在群体层面约束平均3D椭率、三轴性、尺寸及其协方差；并纳入超过900万个$z<0.5$星系以覆盖此前受样本量限制的低红移区间，与JWST早期观测及HST遗留观测在重叠的质量–红移分箱上作对比。

Result: 样本量相对JWST早期观测提升440–52,800倍，相对HST遗留观测在重叠分箱提升440–17,600倍，从而支持更细分箱并改善对形状演化的统计约束。所有分箱中平均椭率均较高（$\mu_E\sim0.600$–$0.821$），表明底层3D形状分布统计上以扁平几何为主。复现了此前小样本研究的关键演化趋势，尤其是低质量端（$10^{9}$–$10^{9.5}M_\odot$）扁长比例向$z=0$下降的现象，该趋势在深度与分辨率不同的多个独立数据集的一致分析中同样出现，说明其并非小样本统计假象。

Conclusion: 基于Euclid DR1的贝叶斯大样本形状推断方法可扩展、稳健，能够在更精细分箱下恢复并强化此前的形状演化结果；低质量星系扁长比例向低红移下降可能是星系演化的真实特征而非统计假象。文中还指出了面向后续Euclid数据发布的方法学改进方向。

Abstract: Understanding the intrinsic 3D shapes of galaxies provides crucial insights into their formation and evolution, but determining these from projections remains challenging. We apply a Bayesian inference framework to optical images of galaxies from the Euclid Data Release 1, leveraging its large sample size to test the scalability and robustness of this methodology. Applying Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) sampling to Sérsic axis ratios and effective radii, we constrain the population-level mean 3D ellipticity, triaxiality, and size, together with their covariances for $40\,864\,304$ star-forming galaxies with stellar masses $M_\ast=10^{9}$--$10^{10.5}M_\odot$ at redshifts $0<z<1.2$. Our sample size exceeds previous shape inference studies by factors of $440$--$52\,800$ relative to early observations by JWST, and factors of $440$--$17\,600$ relative to legacy observations from HST in overlapping mass-redshift bins, enabling finer binning alongside improved statistical constraints on shape evolution. We additionally include over 9 million galaxies at $z<0.5$, a regime historically limited by sample size in previous shape inference studies. We recover key evolutionary trends identified in previous smaller-sample studies. We find high ellipticities ($μ_E \sim 0.600$--$0.821$) across all bins, indicating that the underlying 3D shape distributions are statistically dominated by flattened geometries. Most notably, we recover the decline of the prolate fraction towards $z=0$ at low masses ($M_\ast=10^{9}$--$10^{9.5}M_\odot$) reported by previous studies. This trend is seen consistently in comparable analyses of independent data sets spanning different depths and resolution regimes, showing that it is not an artefact of small-sample statistics, suggesting it may be a genuine feature of galaxy evolution. We also identify future methodological improvements for subsequent Euclid data releases.

</details>

Abstract: We present new radio-continuum observations of the well-known Wolf-Rayet (WR) nebula PCG 11 (Cohen's Ring; named after and in honour of the co-author on the discovery paper, Martin Cohen) using observations from the Australian Square Kilometre Array Pathfinder (ASKAP) Evolutionary Map of the Universe (EMU), Australia Telescope Compact Array (ATCA) and MeerKAT surveys. We find that the elliptical shell surrounding Cohen's Ring has a flux density of 62$\pm$2\,mJy at 1000 MHz. The angular size of the elliptical emission is measured to be 54\arcsec$\times$45\arcsec\ (at P.A.=69.4\D), which agrees well with observations of the source in H$α$ and at 24\,$μ$m in the infrared. Using the measured angular size and a distance of 3.94$\pm$0.27\,kpc, we determine that the nebula has a linear size of $1.03(\pm0.07)\times0.86(\pm0.06)$\,pc. Together with our new and previously measured flux density estimates, we determine a spectral index for Cohen's Ring to be $α$=0.06$\pm$0.02, indicating that the emission from the WR nebula is of thermal origin. As one of the few WR ring nebulae with linked molecular gas, Cohen's Ring provides a valuable laboratory for studying the long-term impact of massive-star feedback on surrounding molecular clouds.

Abstract: The present-day kinematics of multiple populations in globular clusters can retain information about their initial conditions and can therefore help trace how the different populations formed. We combine JWST and HST photometry and proper motions to study the internal dynamics of four populations in NGC 2808 out to about eight half-light radii: the first population (1P) and three second-population groups with increasing light-element processing (2Pa, 2Pb, and 2Pc), providing the first detailed kinematic characterisation of the chemically intermediate 2Pa. We find significant kinematic differences between the populations. While all four have similar sky-projected radial velocity dispersions, 2Pb and 2Pc have lower tangential dispersions than 1P and are strongly radially anisotropic, whereas 2Pa shares the only mildly anisotropic kinematics of 1P. *Xihan Deng,Christopher Martin,Aaron Smith,Sultan Hassan,Drew M. Miles,Nikolaus Z. Prusinski,Vincent Picouet,Mateusz Matuszewski,Zeren Lin,Jake S. Bennett*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出概念验证的机器学习框架 CDE（基于自定义卷积神经网络），从含噪 Lyα 积分视场光谱数据中重建星系际介质气体柱密度图，并在 z~2.3 原星系团 KCWI 观测上初步应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 z~2–6 时期星系际介质的大尺度结构对理解星系演化至关重要，宇宙网中的冷纤维被认为是驱动星系增长的主要气体供给通道。该红移区间 Lyα 发射落入光学波段，是地面 IFU 探测弥散 IGM 的主要探针，但受信噪比低与共振散射复杂性限制，探测困难。

Method: 构建模拟数据集：将 IllustrisTNG100 模拟与简化的辐射转移计算及 KCWI 噪声近似处理相结合；在此基础上训练自定义卷积神经网络 CDE，输入含噪 Lyα IFU 发射立方体，输出气体柱密度图；随后应用于 z~2.3 原星系团的 KCWI 实际观测数据。

Conclusion: 结果表明 CDE 框架有潜力将弥散 Lyα 观测与 IGM 模拟相连接，但重建结果仍依赖于所采用的模型假设，属于概念验证性质的工作。

Abstract: Understanding the large-scale structure of the intergalactic medium (IGM) is essential for studying galaxy evolution, particularly at $z \sim 2$--$6$, when cold filaments in the cosmic web are considered a primary mechanism for fueling galaxy growth. At these redshifts, Ly$α$ emission shifts into the optical regime and serves as the primary tracer of diffuse IGM for ground-based integral field units (IFUs). However, detections remain limited by low signal-to-noise ratios (S/N) and the complexities of resonant scattering. We present a proof-of-concept machine-learning framework to reconstruct IGM gas column-density maps from noisy Ly$α$ IFU emission cubes. Using Keck Cosmic Web Imager (KCWI) observations of $z \sim 2.3$ protoclusters as a case study, we train a custom convolutional neural network (CNN), the Column Density Estimator (CDE), on a simulated dataset combining IllustrisTNG100 with simplified radiative-transfer calculations and an approximate treatment of KCWI noise. The CDE successfully reconstructs broad filamentary structures in the simulated test set under these adopted assumptions. Applied to observed KCWI data, the CDE predicts gas column-density maps containing filamentary structures extending over $\gtrsim 40''$ ($\sim 1$ comoving Mpc), consistent with an IGM interpretation. The observed reconstructions remain conditional on the adopted models. These results illustrate the potential of the CDE framework for connecting diffuse Ly$α$ observations with simulations of the IGM.

</details>


### [89] [B-fields And Dust in InterstelLar FiLAments Using Dust POLarization (BALLAD-POL): V. Magnetic Fields and Grain Alignment Mechanisms in Serpens Main Using JCMT/POL-2 Polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16385)
*Sadhana Singh,Thiem Hoang,Pham Ngoc Diep,Nguyen Bich Ngoc,Woojin Kwon*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要厘清分子云中偏振分数随强度下降（退偏振）的物理起源：究竟是磁场几何缠绕（tangling）导致，还是尘埃颗粒排列效率在致密区域降低所致，以便正确利用偏振数据推断磁场结构与颗粒排列理论（RAT）的适用性。

Result: 磁场强度弱于引力但强于湍流；拟合得到α=0.68±0.01，存在退偏振；P与S无相关性，而P×S随N(H2)增加而下降，说明磁场缠绕并非退偏振主因；P与P×S均随a_align增大而减小，支持排列效率降低为主因；P与P×S与δ_mag,sp均无相关性，表明磁弛豫增强的排列不足以补偿致密区a_align增大带来的排列减弱。

Conclusion: Serpens Main的退偏振主要由致密区域尘埃颗粒排列效率下降（a_align增大）引起，而非磁场缠绕；磁弛豫机制不足以抵消这一效应。

Abstract: We investigate magnetic fields ($B$-field) and grain alignment mechanisms in Serpens Main using 850 $μ$m dust polarization observations from JCMT/POL-2. We derive a map of $B$-field strength and calculate the mass-to-flux ratio and Alfvénic Mach number, suggesting the subdominance of $B$-fields over gravity but their dominance over turbulence. Fitting the Ricean mean model to the nondebiased polarization fraction ($P$) as a function of emission intensity ($I$) yields a slope $α= 0.68\pm0.01$, revealing depolarization. To understand its origins, we examine the dependence of $P$ on gas column density ($N({\rm H}_{2})$), dust temperature ($T_{\rm d}$), and polarization angle dispersion function ($S$). We find no correlation between $P$ and $S$, whereas for $P\times S$, an observational proxy for alignment efficiency decreases with increasing $N({\rm H}_{2})$. This suggests that $B$-field tangling is not the primary cause of depolarization and instead points to reduced grain alignment in denser regions. To test this interpretation within radiative torque (RAT) alignment theory, we calculate the minimum size of aligned grains ($a_{\rm align}$) and magnetic relaxation strength ($δ_{\rm mag,sp}$) using $N({\rm H}_{2})$, $T_{\rm d}$, and our estimated $B$-field map. We find that both $P$ and $P \times S$ decrease with increasing $a_{\rm align}$, supporting reduced grain alignment as the main cause of depolarization in Serpens Main. However, neither $P$ nor $P \times S$ shows a correlation with $δ_{\rm mag,sp}$, suggesting that enhanced grain alignment due to magnetic relaxation is insufficient to compensate for the reduced alignment associated with increasing $a_{\rm align}$ toward denser regions.

</details>

Vielzeuf,S. Vinciguerra,M. von Wietersheim-Kramsta,N. A. Walton,L. Wang,J. R. Weaver,A. H. Wright*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: Euclid DR1 非宇宙学科学产品的光度红移与物理参数流程：以 COSMOS 子样本为参考集，采用近邻算法 NNPZ 推断星系红移、物理参数与绝对星等，并通过内部指标、外部数据集与标度关系验证，结果符合预期与 DR1 前模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Euclid 任务需为数以十亿计星系提供高精度光度红移，其中宇宙学核心科学只需红移，而非宇宙学科学还需物理参数与绝对星等；本文旨在说明 DR1 中该部分数据产品的产出流程与可靠性，并为全天空推广提供一致性保障。

Method: 由 PHZ 组织单元构建流程：输入 Euclid 与地面多波段测光；以 Euclid 辅助天区 COSMOS 观测构建参考样本，其红移与物理参数由 COSMOS2020 多波段测光导出并与核心科学结果保持一致；采用近邻算法 NNPZ，在颜色-星等空间为每个目标星系寻找最近邻并据此推断其属性，同时引入必要的调整与先验以推广至整个河外天区。

Result: 利用内部指标及与辅助数据集（COSMOS2020、3D-HST、DESI）和标度关系（如恒星形成主序）的对比进行性能测试，结果显示对 Euclid 宽巡天与深场均符合预期及 DR1 前模拟水平，数据产品内部一致且质量较高。

Conclusion: DR1 非宇宙学科学产品通过 NNPZ 与 COSMOS 参考样本相结合的方案，能够稳定输出源分类、光度红移、物理参数与绝对星等，验证表现达标，可作为后续数据发布的基线流程。

Abstract: Euclid is a European Space Agency mission mapping the extragalactic sky, aiming to provide the most-accurate 3D view of the Universe at optical and near-infrared wavelengths, by measuring the photometric redshifts of billions of galaxies. Within the Euclid Consortium, this task is executed by the PHZ Organisation Unit. PHZ takes as input the multi-wavelength photometry of Euclid and ground-based data, and returns as output the source classification, photometric redshifts for the Euclid core science (i.e., cosmology), and photometric redshifts plus physical parameters and absolute magnitudes for non-cosmological science. As part of the first Euclid Data Release (DR1), we describe the details of the DR1 pipeline for non-cosmological science, along with the released data products, and their validation. The non-cosmological science data products are obtained with NNPZ, a nearest-neighbour algorithm developed within PHZ that infers the properties of each target galaxy from its closest matches, in colour-magnitude space, within a reference sample of galaxies with known redshifts and physical parameters. We built this reference sample from Euclid observations of COSMOS, one of the Euclid Auxiliary Fields, labelling each galaxy with redshifts and physical parameters derived from the COSMOS2020 multi-wavelength photometry, consistently with the Euclid core-science results. Combined with the adjustments and priors required to generalise the results across the entire extragalactic sky, this approach yields high-quality and internally consistent data products. We tested the performance using both internal metrics and comparisons to ancillary data sets (e.g., COSMOS2020, 3D-HST, and DESI) and scaling relations (e.g., the star-forming main sequence); the results are in line with expectations and pre-DR1 simulations for both the Euclid Wide Survey and Euclid Deep Fields.

</details>


### [91] [Mitigating variance shrinkage in multi-task galaxy property inference from imaging data via statistical manifold alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16598)
*Zhijian Luo,Jianzhen Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 提出 ViTMultiTask，用视觉 Transformer 结合多尺度 MMD 损失，从五波段测光图像直接预测光度红移与恒星族参数，缓解点式损失导致的方差收缩问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模测光巡天（LSST、CSST）缺乏足够光谱样本，需直接从图像推断光度红移与恒星族参数；传统 MSE/Huber 等逐点损失引发"方差收缩"（回归到均值），压缩预测分布、扭曲双峰等非线性物理流形并截断重尾分布。

Method: 构建 ViTMultiTask 视觉 Transformer 多任务框架，在 RKHS 中引入多尺度最大均值差异（MMD）损失，实现高阶联合分布对齐；使用 83,846 个 Subaru HSC 五波段（g,r,i,z,y）星系与 DESI DR1 BGS 光谱交叉样本训练，独立测试集 5,000 个星系。

Result: 点估计精度：z 的 σ_NMAD=0.020，log(M*/M⊙) 为 0.169 dex，D_n4000 为 0.073，Hδ_A 为 1.185 Å。消融实验显示加入 MMD 损失可有效缓解方差收缩，更准确复现观测分布（含双峰与重尾），同时点估计精度与基线统计一致（RMSE 变化 ≤3.5%），灾难性离群率与基线相当。

Conclusion: 基于核的流形对齐为深度成像时代全面的星系性质推断提供了有统计依据且实用的范式。

Abstract: Inferring photometric redshifts ($z$) and key stellar population properties ($\log(M_*/\mathrm{M}_\odot)$, $D_n4000$, and $\mathrm{H}δ_A$) directly from multi-band imaging is increasingly critical for spectroscopically limited large surveys such as LSST and CSST. However, conventional point-wise loss functions (e.g., mean squared error or Huber loss) employed in deep neural networks often induce ``variance shrinkage''---a regression-to-the-mean effect that contracts predicted variances, distorts non-linear physical manifolds (such as bimodal distributions), and truncates heavy-tail populations. Here, we present ViTMultiTask, a vision transformer framework that incorporates a multi-scale maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) loss to enforce higher-order joint distribution alignment in reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS). Trained on 83,846 galaxies from Subaru HSC five-band ($g,r,i,z,y$) imaging cross-matched with DESI Data Release 1 Bright Galaxy Survey spectra, the model achieves high point-estimate precision on an independent test set of 5,000 galaxies: $σ_{\mathrm{NMAD}} = 0.020$ for $z$, $0.169\,\mathrm{dex}$ for $\log(M_*/\mathrm{M}_\odot)$, $0.073$ for $D_n4000$, and $1.185\,\text{Å}$ for $\mathrm{H}δ_A$. Ablation experiments demonstrate that incorporating the MMD loss effectively mitigates variance shrinkage and more accurately reproduces the observed spectroscopic parameter distributions (including bimodal structures and heavy tails), while maintaining point-estimate accuracy statistically consistent with the baseline (RMSE changes $\le 3.5\%$) and catastrophic outlier rates fully comparable to the baseline. This kernel-based manifold alignment offers a statistically grounded and practical paradigm for comprehensive galaxy property inference in the deep imaging era.

</details>


### [92] [The Black Hole Mass of the Sunflower Galaxy NGC 5055: Direct Measurement via Stellar Kinematics with Keck/OSIRIS Laser Guide Star Adaptive Optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16490)
*Tien H. T. Ho,Dieu D. Nguyen,Alister W. Graham,Mark Durré,Hai N. Ngo,Tinh Q. T. Le,Elena Gallo,Igor V. Chilingarian,Tuan N. Le,Roberto Soria,Fan Zou,Huy G. Tong,Nghi K. N. Le,Que T. Le*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 该论文首次利用Keck/OSIRIS近红外积分场光谱（LGS自适应光学辅助）对近邻螺旋星系NGC 5055 (M63) 进行恒星动力学超massive黑洞测量，得到 M_BH = 4.56^{+1.73}_{-1.82} × 10^6 M_⊙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低质量端（尤其无核球的晚型/螺旋星系）的直接动力学黑洞质量测量样本极少，黑洞标度关系在该区域缺乏锚点；NGC 5055 被认为是银河系在黑洞、核星团与恒星总质量上的近邻类比对象，值得精确测量其黑洞质量。

Method: 使用Keck/OSIRIS近红外积分场光谱，结合激光导星自适应光学，达到0.045''角分辨率；从CO带端吸收特征中用pPXF提取二维恒星运动学；测得速度弥散从 r~1'' 处的 ~90 km/s 上升至中心峰值 ~135 km/s。用四组JAM模型（柱状与球状速度椭球取向，两种基于HST档案成像的MGE分解）拟合运动学。

Result: 黑洞质量 M_BH = 4.56^{+1.73}_{-1.82} × 10^6 M_⊙；恒星轨道近各向同性（β≈0），符合旋转晚型星系的典型特征；表面亮度剖面未显示经典核球证据；NGC 5055 在黑洞质量、核星团和恒星总质量方面与银河系高度相似。

Conclusion: 若证实为无核球星系，NGC 5055 将加入少数具有直接动力学黑洞质量的螺旋星系行列（如 NGC 2748、NGC 4395），为晚型星系低质量端黑洞标度关系提供新的锚点。

Abstract: We present the first stellar-dynamical supermassive black hole mass measurement in the nearby spiral galaxy NGC 5055 (M63) using near-infrared integral-field spectroscopy with Keck/OSIRIS assisted by the laser guide star adaptive optics system. The observations deliver two-dimensional stellar kinematics extracted from the CO bandhead absorption features via pPXF at an angular resolution of $0.045''$. The stellar velocity dispersion rises steeply from $\sim90~{\rm km~s^{-1}}$ at $r\sim1''$ to a central peak of $\sim135~{\rm km~s^{-1}}$, kinematically tracing the gravitational influence of a central mass concentration. Modeling these kinematics with an ensemble of four JAM models, spanning cylindrical and spherical velocity-ellipsoid alignments and two independent MGE decompositions of archival HST imaging, yields a black hole mass of $M_{\rm BH}=4.56^{+1.73}_{-1.82}\times10^6~{\rm M_\odot}$. The stellar orbits are consistent with near-isotropy ($β\approx0$), typical of rotating late-type galaxies. NGC 5055 emerges as a remarkable local analogue to the Milky Way in terms of its black hole, nuclear star cluster, and total stellar mass. The surface brightness profile of NGC 5055 shows no evidence for a classical bulge; if confirmed bulgeless, NGC 5055 would join the rare class of spirals with a direct dynamical $M_{\rm BH}$ determination in the absence of a bulge, a regime currently populated by only a handful of galaxies including NGC 2748 and NGC 4395. This measurement provides a new anchor for black hole scaling relations in late-type galaxies at the low-mass end of the black hole mass spectrum.

</details>


### [93] [Active Learning for Molecular Discovery: A Retrospective Test toward TMC-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16741)
*R. I. El-Kholy,Z. M. Hayman*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 对TMC-1的预注册回顾性测试表明，随着探测累积对机器学习候选列表重新排序可提升预测效果（池化AUC 0.867 vs 最佳基线0.706），并使检索半数已探测物种所需观测数从332降至200；但主要瓶颈在于候选生成而非排序，且不确定性采样失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习推荐列表能帮助优先安排新星际分子的搜寻，但"随着探测结果累积而对列表重新排序是否真的优于原始列表"此前从未被检验。作者希望用预注册的回顾性实验量化重排序的价值，并诊断当前流程的真正瓶颈。

Method: 针对TMC-1开展预注册回顾性测试。使用已发表的1,510个推荐候选（整合为1,434个）及截至2026年9月的已报道物种。在2022–2025年四个年度时间原点，用七个公开特征构建逻辑回归排序，并在每个原点仅用当时已知探测重新拟合；与固定基线、随机搜索及不确定性采样（标准主动学习规则）比较，并做日期边界平移与两种替代特征设定下的稳健性检验。

Result: 重排序区分后续探测与从未探测候选的池化AUC为0.867，最佳单准则基线为0.706（差值0.160，95%区间0.095–0.230），在日期边界平移和两种替代特征机制下优势仍存在；但在前100个候选中不明显（12 vs 7次探测）。自适应检索达到列表中63个已探测物种的半数所需观测数中位为200，最佳固定排序为332，随机搜索期望为717.5。不确定性采样表现差于随机（AUC 0.210），后期探测位于预测不确定性低、接近已知化学的区域。验证集101个物种中37个不在列表里，其中仅13个出现在由源数据库重建的池中（11个自由基中2个，离子均无）。

Conclusion: 随探测累积的重新排序确实优于固定列表，可显著提高搜寻效率；但不确定性采样不适用，候选生成而非排序才是主要约束。作者发布100个推荐目标及代码和冻结输入，供前瞻性检验。

Abstract: Machine-learning recommendation lists can help prioritize searches for new interstellar molecules, but whether reranking such a list as detections accumulate improves on it has not been tested. We present a pre-registered retrospective test toward TMC-1, using a published machine-learning list of 1,510 recommended candidates, consolidated to 1,434 candidates, and the species reported there through September 2026. At four annual origins from 2022 to 2025, a logistic-regression ranking on seven released features, refit on the detections known at each origin, separates later detections from never-detected candidates with a pooled AUC of 0.867, against 0.706 for the best single-criterion baseline (difference 0.160; 95\% interval 0.095-0.230). The advantage persists under a shifted date boundary and two alternative feature regimes, but is not established among the top 100 candidates (12 versus 7 detections). A searcher that refits on its own observations reaches half of the 63 detected species on the list in a median of 200 observations, against 332 for the best fixed ordering and 717.5 expected for random search. Uncertainty sampling, the standard active-learning rule, performs worse than random (AUC 0.210): later detections lie at low predictive uncertainty, close to known chemistry. Of 101 species in our validation set, 37 are absent from the list, and only 13 of these appear in a pool rebuilt from its source databases, including 2 of 11 radicals and neither ion. Candidate generation, not ranking, is the main constraint. We release 100 recommended targets, with code and frozen inputs, for prospective testing.

</details>
