Riccio,I. Risso,F. Rizzo,E. Romelli,M. Roncarelli,B. Rusholme,R. Saglia,Z. Sakr,D. Sapone,B. Sartoris,M. Sauvage,M. Schirmer,P. Schneider,M. Scodeggio,A. Secroun,E. Sihvola,P. Simon,C. Sirignano,G. Sirri,L. Stanco,C. Surace,P. Tallada-Crespí,A. N. Taylor,H. I. Teplitz,I. Tereno,S. Toft,R. Toledo-Moreo,F. Torradeflot,I. Tutusaus,J. Valiviita,T. Vassallo,D. Vibert,Y. Wang,J. Weller,A. Zacchei,F. M. Zerbi,I. A. Zinchenko,E. Zucca,P. Bergamini,J. García-Bellido,C. M. Gutierrez,K. Tanidis*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用Euclid NISP无缝光谱模式（blue grism）生成"可自定义中心波长的窄带式"发射线图，并首次在0.4<z<0.9的COSMOS星系样本上验证其科学用途。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统窄带成像受限于固定滤光片波长、覆盖红移不连续；无缝光谱虽含空间+光谱信息，但缺少便于直接使用的二维发射线图工具。作者希望展示可从grism数据构建任意中心波长的发射线图，用于形态学、运动学和统计测量。

Method: 基于2023年11月Euclid性能验证观测的COSMOS天区blue-grism存档数据，构建0.4<z<0.9星系的发射线图；用其识别恒星形成团块、测径向恒星形成轮廓、探测投影内速度梯度、解混Hα-[N II]复合线并检验光谱红移。统计上：对885个星系解混Hα-[N II]复合线重建质量-金属丰度关系，对2634个星系叠加Hα径向轮廓，并与Y_E恒星连续谱图像（匹配Hα PSF）比较尺寸。

Result: 质量-金属丰度关系与Euclid一维光谱结果一致，说明两种解混方法总体一致；Hα径向轮廓大致跟随Y_E恒星连续谱但略更延展，四个Y_E尺寸分箱平均R_50,Hα/R_50,Y_E = 1.15，与既有文献相符。

Conclusion: Euclid blue grism可有效产出灵活的二维发射线图，支持团块识别、径向轮廓、速度梯度与谱线解混等分析，并在统计测量上与一维光谱流程一致，为大规模星系演化研究提供新途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate the capability of the slitless spectroscopic mode of the Euclid Near-Infrared Spectrometer and Photometer (NISP) to produce emission-line maps analogous to narrow-band images with user-defined central wavelengths. We present the first such maps of galaxies at $0.4 < z < 0.9$, constructed from blue-grism exposures obtained during the Euclid performance-verification observations of the Cosmic Evolution Survey (COSMOS) field in November 2023. We demonstrate the use of these maps for identifying star-forming clumps, measuring radial star-formation profiles, detecting projected internal velocity gradients, deblending the H$α$--[N II] complex, and assessing spectroscopic redshift measurements. As a first statistical application, we reconstruct the galaxy mass--metallicity relation for 885 galaxies observed with the blue grism at $0.4 < z < 0.9$ by deblending the H$α$--[N II] complex in the emission-line maps, and measure stacked H$α$ radial profiles for 2634 galaxies over the same redshift range. The mass--metallicity relation is consistent with that obtained from the Euclid 1D spectroscopic measurements, demonstrating overall agreement between the two deblending approaches. The recovered H$α$ radial profiles broadly follow the $Y_{\mathrm{E}}$ stellar continuum but remain modestly more extended after matching the $Y_{\mathrm{E}}$ images to the H$α$ PSF, consistent with previous results in the literature. Across the four $Y_{\mathrm{E}}$ size bins, we measure a mean $R_{50,\mathrm{H}α}/R_{50,Y_{\mathrm{E}}}=1.15$.

</details>


### [95] [Dissecting and diagnosing the LISA Galactic binary population](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16611)
*Alice E. Cravioglio,Arianna I. Renzini*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文用模拟的银河系双白矮星（DWD）星表，结合迭代减除流程，刻画LISA可分辨与不可分辨（混淆前景）两类子群的差异，揭示可分辨性主要由啁啾质量驱动，并存在显著的成分、空间与频率选择偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA将个别分辨数千个银道面DWD双星，但绝大多数淹没在不可分辨的引力波混淆前景中；哪些系统能被分辨取决于双星参数、整体族群与仪器灵敏度，因此可分辨样本并非银河系本征族群的代表性样本。解读LISA未来星表必须先理解这些选择效应。

Method: 构建模拟的银河系DWD星表，在不同观测时长下应用迭代减除（iterative subtraction）流程，对可分辨与不可分辨子群进行统计刻画；分析啁啾质量、演化类型（inspiraling/outspiraling/monochromatic）、成分亚型与空间分布的偏差；提出度量“在某一频段复现给定比例混淆功率所需的最少源数目”的诊断量。

Result: 可分辨性主要受啁啾质量支配：高质量、inspiraling（啁啾）系统被强烈偏好，低质量、outspiraling或单频系统难以分辨，并由此传导到不同成分亚型的分辨率差异。空间上，可分辨源集中于银核（bulge），因其为本征密度最高区域。混淆前景呈现内部双峰啁啾质量结构，且不同频段的背景并非由同一子群均匀贡献。此外，即便积分100年，仍残留混淆地板（confusion floor）。

Conclusion: LISA可分辨DWD星表与混淆背景均存在显著选择偏差，使用它们约束银河系DWD族群时须加以修正；建议在种群推断中显式建模这些选择效应与残余混淆地板。

Abstract: LISA will individually resolve thousands of Galactic double white dwarf (DWD) binaries, while the majority stays buried in an unresolved gravitational wave (GW) confusion foreground. Which systems get resolved depends on the binary's parameters, on the global population and on instrumental sensitivity, so the resolved population is not a representative sample of the underlying Galactic population. Interpreting LISA's future catalog therefore requires understanding these selection effects. Here we characterize the resolved and unresolved subpopulations of a simulated Milky Way DWD catalog, using an iterative subtraction pipeline applied over different observation times. Resolvability is driven primarily by chirp mass: higher chirp mass, inspiraling systems are strongly favored over lower mass, outspiraling or monochromatic ones, and this reflects on the rate with which different composition subtypes are resolved. Spatially, resolved sources trace the Galactic bulge, because it is intrinsically the densest region of the Galaxy. The confusion background shows an internal bimodal chirp-mass structure, and is not generated uniformly by the same subpopulation in different frequency bands. We introduce a diagnostic for the minimum number of sources needed to reproduce a given fraction of the confusion power in a frequency band, and show that a residual confusion floor persists even after 100 years of integration. These biases are significant and need to be accounted for when using LISA's resolved DWD catalog and the confusion background to constrain the Galactic DWD population.

</details>


### [96] [Satellite pairs in the Local Group: from spatial clustering to dynamical associations in observations and $Λ$CDM simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16687)
*Mariana P. Júlio,Marcel S. Pawlowski,Noam I. Libeskind*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文比较银河系与仙女座星系（M31）卫星星系的近邻配对现象与HESTIA约束宇宙学模拟中的本星系群类似体，发现银河系卫星配对效率显著偏高（约53%，模拟仅17–27%），而M31接近各向同性（约28%）；多数配对候选并非长期束缚双星，而是瞬态排列或共同群吸积的遗迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星星系的空间关联与配对统计是检验ΛCDM框架下结构形成与宿主吸积历史的重要探针。银河系与M31观测数据更新后，有必要将观测配对统计与多个宇宙学实现的本星系群类似体进行系统对照，以判断观测是否偏离典型ΛCDM预期，并厘清这些配对的物理本质。

Method: 基于银河系与M31的最新观测数据，并结合HESTIA约束宇宙学模拟中三个不同的本星系群类似体。构造能重现各宿主径向分布的各向同性群体作为对照，通过最近邻比较识别紧密卫星关联，并利用视向速度、自行和引力束缚估计判断其物理性质；同时施加恒星质量截断（M⋆>10^4 M⊙）以匹配模拟分辨率。

Result: 银河系相对于各向同性预期呈现显著的紧密卫星关联过量，配对效率约53%，接近模拟类似体（约17%–27%）的两倍，且该过量在施加恒量质量截断后依然存在。M31卫星系统则与各向同性基本一致，配对效率约28%。然而仅有少数配对候选具有相干运动学或可能处于引力束缚状态，说明多数配对是瞬态排列或共同群吸积的残留。

Conclusion: 银河系拥有异常丰富的相空间相关卫星群，可能反映其近期吸积历史；而M31更符合典型ΛCDM预期。这提示仅凭单一宿主（银河系）推断一般规律存在风险，需结合多宿主样本与模拟实现加以审视。

Abstract: We investigate the incidence and physical nature of satellite galaxy pairs in the Local Group using updated observational data for the Milky Way (MW) and Andromeda (M31), together with analogues from the HESTIA constrained cosmological simulations. Our comparison uses three distinct simulated Local Group analogues drawn from the HESTIA suite, allowing us to assess the observed pair statistics against multiple cosmological realisations of the Local Group environment. We identify close nearest-neighbour associations by comparing the observed satellite distributions with isotropic populations that reproduce the radial distributions of each host, and investigate their physical nature using line-of-sight velocities, proper motions, and gravitational binding estimates. We find that the MW exhibits a significant excess of close satellite associations relative to isotropic expectations, with a pairing efficiency of $\sim53\%$, nearly twice that found in the simulated analogues ($\sim17$-$27\%$). This excess persists after applying a stellar mass cut ($M_\star > 10^4\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$) to match the resolution limits of the simulations. In contrast, the M31 satellite system is broadly consistent with isotropy, with a pairing efficiency of $\sim28\%$. However, only a minority of pair candidates exhibit coherent kinematics or are likely to be gravitationally bound, indicating that most represent transient alignments or remnants of common group infall rather than long-lived binary satellites. These results suggest that the MW hosts an unusually rich population of phase-space-correlated satellites, likely reflecting its recent accretion history, whereas M31 is more consistent with typical $Λ$CDM expectations.

</details>


### [97] [Magellanic Outflow and Chemistry Survey (MAGOS): the Outflow-Luminosity Scaling Relations in the Large and Small Magellanic Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16830)
*Suinan Zhang,Kei E. I. Tanaka,Yichen Zhang,Takashi Shimonishi,Yu Cheng,Kenji Furuya,Asako Sato*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 该文利用ALMA 0.85毫米观测，对大、小麦哲伦云中40个大质量年轻星体的CO(3–2)外流进行首次系统巡天（MAGOS），并与银河系对比外流—光度标度关系。LMC（约0.5倍太阳金属丰度）外流质量—光度关系斜率与银河系类似，说明CO仍是有效示踪剂；SMC（约0.2倍太阳金属丰度）外流在高光度端明显不足、标度关系更平缓，可能源于低金属丰度下光致解离导致CO丰度降低，亦不能排除外流本身较弱的可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解低金属丰度环境下的大质量恒星形成，对于检验恒星形成规律在宇宙演化过程中是否具有普适性至关重要。此前缺乏对大质量原恒星外流的系统性、多金属丰度对比观测，尤其是麦哲伦云这一理想低金属丰度实验室中的样本。

Method: 作为MAGOS巡天的一部分，用ALMA在0.85毫米波段以约0.1 pc的线性分辨率观测LMC与SMC中40个光度为(1–23)×10⁴ L_⊙的大质量年轻星体；利用CO(3–2)识别与主要连续谱源成协的蓝移、红移外流，并将外流质量、动量、能量和力与光度的标度关系同银河系样本进行比较。

Result: 在LMC的29个源和SMC的10个源中探测到外流。LMC的CO外流在质量—光度关系上的斜率与银河系相近，表明在约0.5倍太阳金属丰度下CO仍是大质量原恒星外流的有效示踪剂；SMC的CO外流尽管弥散较大，但在高光度端明显不足，标度关系比LMC和银河系更平缓。

Conclusion: SMC的观测结果与高光度、低金属丰度（约0.2倍太阳金属丰度）条件下光致解离增强导致CO丰度下降的解释相符，但基于现有数据尚不能排除外流本身内在较弱的可能性。该工作首次在低金属丰度环境中系统刻画大质量原恒星外流的标度关系，为检验其普适性提供了关键观测约束。

Abstract: Understanding massive star formation in low-metallicity environments is crucial for constraining the universality of star formation across cosmic evolution. In this Letter, we present the first systematic survey of massive protostellar outflows in the Large and Small Magellanic Clouds (LMC and SMC, $Z\sim$0.2--0.5 $Z_\odot$), as part of the Magellanic Outflow and Chemistry Survey (MAGOS). MAGOS targets 40 massive young stellar objects (MYSOs) with luminosities of (1--23)$\times 10^4$ $L_\odot$ with the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA) at 0.85 mm at a linear resolution of $\sim$0.1 pc. Using CO (3--2), we identify blue- and red-shifted outflows associated with the primary continuum sources toward 29 targets in the LMC and 10 targets in the SMC. Comparing the outflow mass, momentum, energy, and force versus luminosity scaling relations in MAGOS with those in the Galaxy, we find that the LMC CO outflows have a similar slope in the mass-luminosity relation, suggesting that CO remains an effective tracer of massive protostellar outflows at $Z\sim$0.5 $Z_\odot$. In contrast, despite the large scatter, the SMC CO outflows are deficient at high luminosity and show flatter scaling relations than the LMC and the Galaxy. This is consistent with reduced CO abundances due to enhanced photodissociation at high luminosity and low metallicity ($Z\sim$0.2 $Z_\odot$), although the possibility of intrinsically weak outflow launching cannot be ruled out with the current data.

</details>


### [98] [The origin of Potassium in Galactic chemical evolution - The effect of C-O shell mergers in massive stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17224)
*Marta Molero,Luca Boccioli,Almudena Arcones*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文重新分析三套产额集共194个超新星前模型，量化C-O壳层并合对钾元素合成的贡献，并探讨其对银河系化学演化及并合事件发生率的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系化学演化模型普遍低估观测到的[K/Fe]，钾及其他奇Z元素（质子数为奇数的元素）的起源仍不清楚。C-O壳层并合被认为是高效合成钾的潜在场所，但不同产额集与观测的符合情况及其物理含义尚需系统检验。

Method: 分析来自三个独立产额集的194个大质量恒星超新星前模型，覆盖广泛的初始质量、金属丰度和转动速度，包括单星与双星剥离星；识别发生C-O壳层并合的 progenitor，并构建包含与不包含其贡献的银河系化学演化模型进行对比。

Result: 三套产额集中增强的钾产量均可追溯到少数C-O并合前身星，其钾抛射量比同质量非并合前身星高约10-50倍。若无并合贡献，无模型能重现观测，[K/Fe]在大部分金属丰度区间低于太阳值；计入并合贡献后，[K/Fe]仅在各自网格中并合发生的金属丰度窗口内被提升。双星性作用较小，单星即可产生相近的钾量。

Conclusion: C-O并合合成的钾基本上是原初的，观测到的[K/H]随[Fe/H]近似线性增长只有在各金属丰度下C-O壳层并合频率相近时才能重现。因此钾可作为推断大质量恒星中C-O壳层并合随时间发生率的示踪元素。

Abstract: The Galactic chemical evolution (GCE) of potassium (K) and of most other odd-Z elements remains poorly understood: models generally underproduce the observed [K/Fe] over a wide metallicity range, depending on the adopted massive star yields. A proposed site for efficient K nucleosynthesis is the merger between the oxygen and carbon shells (C-O shell merger) that can occur in massive stars a few hours before core collapse. Some GCE models with specific yield sets have been shown to reconcile predictions with observations. Here we re-analyse these yield sets to quantify the contribution of C-O shell mergers to K production and what the Galactic evolution of K implies about the occurrence of these events. We analyse 194 pre-supernova models from three independent yield sets, covering a wide range of initial masses, metallicities and rotational velocities, and including both single and binary-stripped stars. We identify the progenitors undergoing a C-O shell merger and compute GCE models with and without their contribution. In all three sets, the enhanced K production can be traced back to a small number of C-O merger progenitors, which eject up to ~10-50 times more K than non-merger progenitors of similar mass. Without them, no model reproduces the observations and [K/Fe] is sub-solar over most of the metallicity range. With them, [K/Fe] is enhanced only within the metallicity window where mergers occur in each grid. Binarity itself plays a minor role, as single stars alone produce similar amounts of K. Since the K synthesised in a C-O merger is essentially primary, the observed nearly linear increase of [K/H] with [Fe/H] can be reproduced only if C-O shell mergers occur with a similar frequency at all metallicities. K could therefore be used to infer the occurrence rate of C-O shell mergers in massive stars as a function of time.

</details>


### [99] [Bidimensional Exploration of the warm-Temperature Ionised gaS (BETIS) III. The impact of eDIG on strong-line abundance determinations in galactic halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17135)
*R. González-Díaz,E. Pérez Montero,J. M. Vílchez,F. F. Rosales-Ortega,L. Galbany*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用MUSE积分视场光谱首次对4个侧向星系eDIG的气相化学丰度进行空间分辨研究，发现激波污染区O/H与N/O系统性偏低约0.1–0.15 dex，O/H在盘与晕之间变化很小，而N/O存在显著的径向与垂向变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eDIG（盘外星系弥散电离气体）是连接星系盘与晕的反馈过程的关键示踪物，但其气相化学丰度此前缺乏空间分辨的系统研究；同时需检验在多种电离源共存时丰度推导方法的可靠性。

Method: 基于BETIS样本中4个侧向星系的MUSE积分视场光谱，用HII-CHI-Mistry v5.5代码推导12+log(O/H)、log(N/O)与log(U)的二维分布图；按光电离与激波的相对贡献将eDIG划分为恒星形成主导区与激波污染区，并评估该方法在多电离源情形下的可靠性。

Result: 激波污染区中HII-CHI-Mistry给出的O/H和N/O系统性偏低，N/O尤为明显，相对恒星形成主导区典型偏移约0.1–0.15 dex，且电离态更低更均匀（log(U)≈-3.1）；恒星形成主导区电离参数范围更宽（log(U)≈-3.3至-2.8），氧丰度接近太阳值。这些丰度偏移与模型网格的采样步长相当。O/H在盘与晕之间差异很小，而N/O呈现显著的径向与垂向变化，在恒星形成反馈较弱的环境中尤为突出。

Conclusion: O/H在盘晕间近似均一，与恒星形成反馈和星系喷泉高效循环富氧气体的图像一致，并与流体力学模拟相符；N/O的空间变化则反映反馈强度差异。激波污染会显著偏置推导的丰度（尤其N/O），在eDIG化学分析中需谨慎区分电离机制。

Abstract: The extraplanar diffuse ionised gas (eDIG) is a key tracer of the feedback processes that connect galactic discs and halos. We present the first study of spatially resolved gas-phase chemical abundances in the eDIG of edge-on galaxies, based on integral field spectroscopy obtained with MUSE. Using a sample of four galaxies from the BETIS sample, we derived maps of the oxygen abundance, 12+log(O/H), the nitrogen-to-oxygen ratio, log(N/O), and the ionisation parameter, log(U), with the HII-CHI-Mistry v5.5 code, and we tested the reliability of this method when different ionisation sources are present. We separated the eDIG into star-formation-dominated and shock-contaminated regimes according to the relative contribution of photoionisation and shocks, and investigated their impact on the derived chemical abundances. We find that HII-CHI-Mistry returns systematically lower O/H and N/O values in shock-contaminated regions, especially for N/O, with typical offsets of ~0.1-0.15 dex relative to star-formation-dominated regions, together with a more homogeneous and lower ionisation state (log(U) ~ -3.1). In contrast, star-formation-dominated regions span a wider range of ionisation parameters (log(U) ~ -3.3 to -2.8) while maintaining approximately solar oxygen abundances. Despite these differences, the abundance offsets are comparable to the sampling step of the HII-CHI-Mistry model grid. The inferred oxygen abundance shows little variation between discs and halos. This is compatible with a scenario in which star formation feedback and galactic fountains efficiently recycle oxygen-rich gas between both components, in agreement with hydrodynamical simulations. In contrast, the N/O ratio shows significant radial and vertical variations, particularly in environments with weaker star formation feedback.

</details>


### [100] [Radiation, Thermochemistry, and Stellar Feedback in DISPATCH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17290)
*William Lake,Paolo Padoan,Åke Nordlund,Troels Haugbølle*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出 DISPATCH 的一个扩展，将方向性恒星辐射、漫红外辐射输运与热化学，同自引力磁流体力学、单体恒星吸积及原恒星喷流相耦合，用以研究大质量恒星形成中吸积流能否在辐射与出流反馈下存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量恒星能否长大，取决于其吸积流能否抵御正在形成的星群所产生的辐射和出流反馈；需要能同时解析单体恒星环境与大尺度星团环境、并能长时间模拟巨分子云的数值框架。

Method: 基于自适应网格（AMR）扩展 DISPATCH：采用局部时间步进，使恒星附近与星团尺度的环境以不同时间步推进；直接辐射多波段（含电离与非电离）输运，用短特征线保持源方向与阴影；漫红外光提供 M1 与静态射线两种输运方案；热化学含原子/分子氢、氦、金属冷却与尘埃，并使用依赖成分的状态方程；恒星质量通过气体吸积实时增长，而非预设初始质量函数。

Result: 测试中，多源照明保留了具有几何形状的阴影，漫辐射再处理加热了被遮蔽物质；喷流、电离前沿和辐射压测试表现符合预期并收敛；电离前沿可跨异步加密边界传播，晚期速度与解析预测相差在百分之几以内。

Conclusion: 该框架可用来确定辐射与机械反馈如何改变湍流云中吸积的持续时间和效率，从而把气体供给、单体恒星与大质量恒星初始质量函数联系起来。

Abstract: The growth of massive stars depends on whether their accretion flows survive radiation and outflow feedback from the forming stellar population. We present an extension of DISPATCH that couples directional stellar radiation, diffuse infrared transport, and thermochemistry to self-gravitating magnetohydrodynamics, individual stellar accretion, and protostellar jets. This AMR-based method uses DISPATCH's local timestepping, allowing stellar environments and their larger scale cluster environments to step on separate timescales, and providing the framework needed to scale to long-duration simulations of GMCs. Direct radiation is transported in multiple bands, including both ionizing and nonionizing radiation, and uses short characteristics to maintain source directions and shadowing. Diffuse radiation is implemented for IR reprocessed light, with M1 and stationary ray options for transport. The thermochemical model includes atomic and molecular hydrogen and helium chemistry, and metal cooling and dust. It also includes a composition-dependent equation of state. Stellar masses grow through accretion of gas rather than a prescribed IMF, allowing stellar masses to respond to the evolving gas supply and feedback. In tests, multiple-source illumination retains geometric shadows while diffuse reprocessing heats shielded material, and tests of jets, ionization fronts, and radiation pressure demonstrate expected behavior and convergence. Ionization fronts also cross asynchronous refinement boundaries with late-time propagation speeds within a few percent of analytic predictions. Together, these developments provide a framework for determining how radiative and mechanical feedback modify the duration and efficiency of accretion from turbulent clouds, connecting the gas supply to individual stars with the high-mass stellar initial mass function.

</details>


### [101] [Probing Ultralight Higher-spin Dark Matter using Stellar Kinematics of Dwarf Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17204)
*Praniti Singh,JiJi Fan,Savvas M. Koushiappas,Isabelle S. Goldstein*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用六个经典银河系矮星系的恒星运动学数据，通过引入含三个额外参数的广义超轻暗物质（ULDM）晕剖面，对非零自旋ULDM进行贝叶斯分析，未发现对高自旋ULDM更弥散核心的偏好，但也尚不能排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恒星运动学是探测暗物质分布及其物理本质的有力手段。数值模拟显示，在相同初始条件下，高自旋ULDM在星系中心形成的孤子核心比标量ULDM更弥散，且向NFW外区过渡更平滑，因此可用矮星系运动学数据检验自旋效应对ULDM的影响。

Method: 提出一种广义的ULDM晕剖面参数化，用三个额外参数刻画自旋诱导效应：中心核心密度的归一化、核心-晕质量关系的指数、以及向NFW包层过渡的半径；随后将该剖面应用于六个经典矮星系的贝叶斯分析。

Result: 得到晕质量范围约 $10^{8.5}$–$10^{9.5}\,M_\odot$，并由每个星系单独给出粒子质量 $m \gtrsim 3\times10^{-21}$ eV。核心-晕指数与过渡半径与标量情形一致；核心密度归一化在四个星系中与标量值一致，在Fornax中偏好比标量孤子更致密的核心，而高自旋核心预期的数倍压低未被任何单个星系排除。

Conclusion: 数据未显示出对高自旋ULDM更弥散核心的偏好，但目前的观测精度尚不足以将其排除。

Abstract: Stellar kinematics of the classical Milky Way dwarf galaxies provide a powerful probe of the dark matter distribution and its underlying physics. In this article we use them to explore ultralight dark matter (ULDM) with a non-zero spin. Numerical simulations in the non-relativistic limit have shown that, for the same initial conditions, higher-spin ULDM forms a more diffuse soliton core at the galactic center, with a smoother transition to the NFW outer region, than scalar ULDM. We introduce a generalized parametrization of the ULDM halo profile that captures these spin-induced effects through three additional parameters: the normalization of the central core density, the exponent of the core-halo mass relation, and the radius of the transition to the NFW envelope. Applying this profile in a Bayesian analysis of six classical dwarf galaxies, we find halo masses in the range $10^{8.5}$-$10^{9.5}\,M_\odot$ and, from each galaxy individually, a particle mass $m \gtrsim 3\times10^{-21}$ eV. The core-halo exponent and the transition radius are consistent with their scalar values. The core-density normalization is also consistent with the scalar value in four galaxies and prefers a core denser than a scalar soliton in Fornax, while the factor-of-several suppression expected for higher-spin cores is not excluded by any single galaxy. The data therefore show no preference for the more diffuse cores of higher-spin ULDM, but do not yet rule them out.

</details>


### [102] [The full Gaia colour-magnitude diagram with white dwarfs reveals a declining Milky Way star formation history](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17337)
*K. Sysoliatina,Akash Vani*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: C A L O M E R A框架将白矮星与主序星统一在自洽银河系模型下，用Gaia DR3数据标定后，重现各色等区域计数误差<3%，白矮星数目过量从81%降到<0.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往白矮星群体与非简并星群体分别独立建模，彼此不一致，白矮星计数被系统高估（约81%），无法作为可靠的银河系恒星形成史"化石"记录。

Method: 发展半解析化学-动力学群体合成框架CALOMERA：单一年龄子群的垂向密度剖面与银河势自洽计算，耦合现代白矮星冷却序列（Montréal、BaSTI、LPCODE）、依赖金属丰度的前身星寿命和合成测光；对初始质量函数、恒星形成史、初末质量关系及白矮星群体共18个参数，用1 kpc内Gaia DR3的7.98×10^6颗星（含1.1×10^5颗白矮星）标定；并扩展至ATMO模型的次恒星天体。

Result: 各色等区域星数与总计数分别吻合到3%和0.5%以内；恢复的恒星形成史早期达峰、随后指数衰减，并含τ≈2.4和0.5 Gyr两次近期爆发；三种冷却库拟合质量相当，差异仅在M_G≳15 mag；预测光度函数与100 pc Gaia白矮星样本大致一致；进一步给出银河系白矮星分布与不同深度巡天的差分计数，以及L/T/Y矮星群体及对JWST深场的贡献。

Conclusion: 统一标定的化学-动力学群体合成可同时再现主序星与白矮星计数，消除旧标定中的白矮星过量，并具备向次恒星和深场巡天预测推广的能力。

Abstract: White dwarfs (WDs) provide a fossil record of the Milky Way star formation history (SFH) through the combined effects of stellar evolution and cooling. They have, however, generally been modelled in isolation from the non-degenerate population, so that no single self-consistent set of Galactic assumptions has been required to reproduce both. We present CALOMERA, a semi-analytic chemo-dynamical population synthesis framework in which the vertical density profiles of mono-age subpopulations are computed self-consistently with the Galactic potential and coupled to modern WD cooling sequences, metallicity-dependent progenitor lifetimes and synthetic photometry. We calibrate 18 parameters describing the initial mass function, SFH, initial-final mass relation and WD population against a Gaia DR3 sample of $7.98\times10^{6}$ stars within 1 kpc, including $1.1\times10^{5}$ WDs. The calibrated model reproduces the star counts in each colour-magnitude diagram region to within 3% and the total number count to within 0.5%, compared with the 81% excess in the predicted WD count obtained with the previous main-sequence-only calibration. The recovered SFH peaks early, declines exponentially thereafter, and includes two recent bursts, located at $τ\approx2.4$ and $0.5$ Gyr. Three cooling libraries (Montréal, BaSTI and LPCODE) yield comparably good fits, with differences confined to ${\rm M_G}\gtrsim15$ mag. The predicted luminosity function is in reasonable agreement with the 100 pc Gaia WD sample. We further predict the Galactic WD distribution and differential number counts for surveys of varying depth. Extending the framework to the substellar regime using ATMO models, we predict the Galactic L, T, and Y dwarf populations and their contribution to the deep JWST fields.

</details>


### [103] [The ultraviolet continuum slopes of galaxies at $\mathbf{z \gtrsim 12}$ selected from ${\mathbf{0.6 \, deg}^2}$ of JWST imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17208)
*C. Bondestam,D. J. McLeod,F. Cullen,R. J. McLure,J. S. Dunlop,S. Antonogiannaki,H. -H. Leung,R. Begley,A. C. Carnall,C. T. Donnan,R. S. Ellis,A. Fontana,N. A. Grogin,I. J. B. Holst,G. D. Illingworth,A. M. Koekemoer,F. -Y. Liu,T. M. Stanton,S. D. Stevenson,E. Taylor*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 对55个z≳12星系UV连续谱斜率分析，发现其平均极蓝（⟨β⟩=-2.57±0.04），符合无尘埃衰减的本征恒星+星云连续谱极限，β(z)在z>4平滑变蓝（dβ/dz≈-0.1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期JWST研究表明z≲10存在中等尘埃衰减星系，而到z≈11是否变为无衰减仍待确认；需要大样本、宽视场的z≳12星系UV斜率测量来检验尘埃衰减随红移的演化及β-M_UV关系。

Method: 从公开JWST/NIRCam成像的大天区（>0.6平方度）搜索中选出55个z≳12星系，测量其UV连续谱斜率β（样本M_UV覆盖-21.6至-17.3）；用逆方差加权求平均β，并与本征恒星+星云连续谱极限比较；再补充JWST至z=9的数据及M_UV匹配的HST至z=4的研究，拟合β(z)演化。

Result: 平均⟨β⟩=-2.57±0.04，与无UV尘埃衰减的本征极限一致；z≳12样本中未发现β-M_UV关系，说明无衰减解释适用于所有观测M_UV；联合样本显示β(z)在z>4平滑变蓝，dβ/dz≈-0.1；单个β测量的散布表明存在一小部分β≳-2的尘埃致红星系。

Conclusion: z≳12星系整体处于无尘埃衰减状态，z≲10的中等衰减星系到z≈11基本消失；但个体β散布暗示仍存在少量红化星系，尘埃衰减在极高红移可能并非完全为零。

Abstract: We present an analysis of the ultraviolet (UV) continuum slopes $(β)$ of $55$ galaxies at $z\gtrsim 12$, selected from a wide-area ($>0.6$ sq. deg) search across publicly available JWST/NIRCam imaging. Our sample spans the absolute UV magnitude ($M_{\mathrm{UV}}$) range $-21.6 \leq M_{\mathrm{UV}} \leq -17.3$ and we find an inverse variance-weighted mean $\langleβ\rangle = -2.57\pm0.04$ indicating that, on average, the galaxy population is extremely blue at $z\gtrsim 12$. The average $β$ at these redshifts is consistent with the intrinsic stellar plus nebular continuum limit in the absence of UV dust-attenuation. Consistent with earlier JWST works, our analysis suggests that the moderately dust-attenuated galaxies at $z \lesssim 10$ become essentially attenuation-free by $z \simeq 11$. We find no evidence for a $β-M_{\mathrm{UV}}$ relation in our $z\gtrsim 12$ sample, indicating that the attenuation-free interpretation applies across all observed UV magnitudes. By augmenting our sample with previous JWST constraints down to $z=9$, and incorporating $M_{\mathrm{UV}}$-matched HST studies down to $z=4$, we find a smooth evolution in $β(z)$ over a wide range in redshift. The average value of $β$ becomes progressively bluer at $z>4$, with $\mathrm{d}β/\mathrm{d}z \simeq -0.1$. Finally, although we find that the typical galaxy at $z \gtrsim 12$ is consistent with the attenuation-free limit, the scatter in our individual $β$ measurements is consistent with the existence of a small population of dust-reddened galaxies with $β\gtrsim -2$.

</details>

### [104] [Early-Excess SNe Ia Show Strong C II Absorption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14618)
*L. P. Derose,W. B. Hoogendam,B. J. Shappee,C. Ashall,D. O. Jones*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 该论文研究早期光变曲线过剩与未燃烧碳吸收之间的关系，发现早期过剩的Ia型超新星在约-13天前的C II吸收更强，为爆炸模型提供新约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分Ia型超新星上升光变曲线存在早期过剩，但其物理起源仍不确定；驱动过剩的过程会影响早期抛射物，也可能在最外层留下印记，因此需检验早期过剩是否与未燃烧物质差异相关。

Method: 对35颗非特殊Ia型超新星，采用多成分高斯拟合混合的Si II λ6355与C II λ6580区域，测量伪等值宽度和速度，并按上升光变曲线分类比较早期过剩组与非过剩组。

Result: 早期过剩对象（13颗）在相位约早于-13天时C II吸收比非过剩对象（22颗）更强，最早测量的双样本K-S检验p=0.025；HV Si II成分无明显区分。早期过剩超新星更亮、衰减更慢，但最大光性质相近的对象C II伪等值宽度仍有分布，说明该效应不同于光度-宽度关系。

Conclusion: 在部分情形下，光变形状与光球温度相近时，所观测效应并非由电离驱动，而源于爆炸本身的差异（如碳丰度和密度、白矮星燃烧效率）；结果要求解释早期过剩的Ia型超新星爆炸模型应倾向于产生更强的早期C II吸收。

Abstract: Some type Ia supernovae (SNe Ia) exhibit an early excess in their rising light curves, but the physical origin of this behavior remains uncertain. The processes driving these excesses affect the ejecta at early times and may also imprint on the outermost layers. We investigate whether early excesses are associated with differences in unburned material by measuring pre-maximum carbon absorption. Using multi-component Gaussian fits to the blended Si II $\lambda6355$ and C II $\lambda6580$ region, we measure pseudo-equivalent widths and velocities for a sample of 35 nonpeculiar SNe Ia with rising light curve categorizations. We find that early-excess objects ($n=13$) tend to exhibit stronger C II absorption than non-excesses ($n=22$) at phases earlier than approximately $-13$ d, with a two-sample K-S test of the earliest measurements yielding $p=0.025$. In contrast, the HV Si II components do not clearly separate the two groups. Early-excess SNe Ia tend to be more luminous and decline more slowly, but objects with similar maximum-light properties span a range of C II pseudo-equivalent widths, suggesting that this effect is distinct from the known luminosity-width relation. We show that, at least in some cases, when objects have similar light-curve shape and photospheric temperature, the effect we see is not driven by ionization but by distinct differences in the explosion, such as carbon abundance and density and the efficiency of burning in the WD. Our results provide a new constraint on SN Ia explosion models for early excesses, as they must produce a tendency for stronger early C II absorption.

</details>


### [105] [On the Excitation of Sloshing Oscillations in Hot Coronal Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14676)
*Arpit Kumar Shrivastav,S. Krishna Prasad*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 利用SDO/AIA观测同一活动区内单个日冕环对多次耀斑的响应，发现5次耀斑中有3次激发了沿环往复的"晃动"振荡，另2次仅出现等离子体流；振荡能否被激发取决于耀斑注入持续时间是否短于扰动穿越环路的传播时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热日冕环中常观测到压缩性等离子体振荡，且常伴随足点附近的耀斑局部增亮，耀斑加热会向日冕环脉冲式注入等离子体并产生在两足点间往复的"晃动"振荡。然而，这类振荡的激发与耀斑具体特征之间的关联仍不清楚，本文旨在厘清何种耀斑条件下会激发晃动振荡。

Method: 使用太阳动力学天文台（SDO）上大气成像组件（AIA）的成像数据，选取同一活动区内单个日冕环在多次耀斑事件中的响应构成独特观测样本；对每次耀斑事件分析其伴随的等离子体注入持续时间，并与等离子体扰动穿越整个环路的传播时间进行比较，判别有/无振荡两类情况。

Result: 5次耀斑事件中，3次触发晃动振荡，2次仅观测到等离子体流而无振荡。对比分析表明：当等离子体注入持续时间短于扰动穿越环路的传播时间时，晃动振荡被激发；当注入持续时间与传播时间相当时，则不出现振荡。

Conclusion: 晃动振荡的激发依赖于耀斑的时间特征，即脉冲式注入相对于环内扰动传播时间的短暂性是振荡产生的关键条件；这为理解耀斑—日冕环振荡的激发机制提供了观测依据。

Abstract: Observations of hot coronal loops have often shown the presence of compressive plasma oscillations. These oscillations are usually preceded by a flare-associated localised brightening in the vicinity of one of the footpoints of the coronal loop. The flare heating produces an impulsive injection of plasma into the coronal loop, along with a plasma perturbation that bounces back and forth between the two footpoints, termed as sloshing oscillations. While the association of these oscillations with flares is apparent, the pertinent connection between their excitation and the flare characteristics remains poorly understood. In this study, using data from the Atmospheric Imaging Assembly (AIA) onboard the Solar Dynamics Observatory (SDO), we present a unique set of observations, where the response of a single coronal loop due to repeated flares in the same active region is evident. Our analysis reveals that in three out of five flare events, sloshing oscillations are triggered, while in the other two events, only plasma flows are observed. By examining the duration of plasma injection associated with each flare, we find that sloshing oscillations occur when the injection duration is shorter than the travel time of the plasma perturbation across the loop. In contrast, no oscillations are observed when the injection duration is comparable to the travel time. These results reveal the dependence of sloshing oscillation excitation on the temporal characteristics of flares.

</details>


### [106] [Astrometric modeling of unresolved variable binary systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14690)
*K. Sivkova,P. Kervella,A. Gallenne,L. Decin,N. Nardetto,F. Arenou,A. Mérand,N. R. Evans,B. Apostolova,M. C. Bailleul,R. S. Rathour,W. Kiviaho,G. Bras,V. Hocdé,L. Breuval,C. Vert,D. P. González*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 针对Gaia DR4首次发布的历元天体测量数据，作者开发了vimbinary工具，模拟17个未分辨双星系统（含变源诱导移动体VIM），量化双星运动和VIM效应对视差与自行拟合的偏差，并比较单星模型与双星模型的结果差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gaia DR4将首次提供历元天体测量数据。若把未分辨双星的历元位置简单按单星模型拟合，双星轨道运动及光变导致的测光中心移动（VIM效应）会污染视差和自行估计，因此需要评估模型假设对天体测量参数的影响。

Method: 开发vimbinary框架，用于模拟并解释未分辨双星系统的历元天体测量；对17个良表征的未分辨双星（包括非脉动双星和含一个光变伴星的系统，即VIM）生成类Gaia DR4的历元天体测量数据，并分别用单星模型和双星系统模型进行拟合比较。

Result: 双星运动和VIM效应均能显著影响视差与自行测量，影响幅度强烈依赖于轨道周期、光心振幅和系统流量比；更长的观测时间覆盖并不必然提高视差精度（而自行精度通常随基线延长而改善）；双星系统拟合得到的视差比单星框架更准确。

Conclusion: 在Gaia DR4历元天体测量分析中，未分辨双星必须谨慎处理：宜采用双星系统模型而非单星模型，且不能假定延长基线必然改善视差精度。

Abstract: Gaia DR4 will, for the first time, provide epoch astrometry. When analysing these data, binary sources must be treated carefully, as modelling their astrometry as single stars can bias parallax and proper motion estimates. We developed the vimbinary tool, a framework to simulate and interpret epoch astrometry of unresolved binary systems and to evaluate the impact of model assumptions on the resulting astrometric parameters. We simulated Gaia DR4-like epoch astrometry for a sample of 17 well-characterised unresolved binary systems, including both non-pulsating binaries and systems containing one photometric variable component - variability-induced movers (VIMs). We show that both binary motion and the VIM effect can significantly impact parallax and proper motion measurements, with the magnitude of the effect strongly dependent on the orbital period, photocentric amplitude, and flux ratio of the specific system. We also show that larger time coverage does not necessarily improve parallax precision, in contrast to proper motion, for which precision generally improves with longer baselines. Instead, parallaxes retrieved with binary-system fitting are more accurate than those obtained within the single-star framework.

</details>


### [107] [The Gaia-alerted 2024--2025 brightening of V346 Nor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14723)
*Á. Kóspál,D. J. Lengyel,P. Ábrahám,B. M. Marosi,F. Cruz-Sáenz de Miera,E. Fiorellino,T. Giannini,S. Kraus,A. Labdon,P. W. Lucas,Th. Moore,Zs. Nagy,M. Siwak,A. Udalski*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: V346 Nor 2024年7月突发增亮源于吸积率与视线消光的叠加变化：2021年起吸积率处于历史最高水平，而2025年2—5月亮度峰值主要由消光从约20 mag降至10 mag驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 年轻恒星体通过高度不稳定、爆发式的吸积过程积累质量，常伴随外流与喷发。V346 Nor 于2024年7月突发光学增亮并触发 Gaia 光度科学警报，需要厘清该次增亮背后的物理机制。

Method: 分析新获取与存档的光度、光谱监测数据，追踪过去十年间亮度、颜色与光谱特征的变化；并用吸积盘模型拟合观测，将恒星质量—吸积率乘积 M⋆Ṁ 与视线消光 A_V 作为自由参数。

Result: 自2021年起吸积率（M⋆Ṁ ~ 10⁻⁴ M⊙² yr⁻¹）高于以往任何历史峰值，但在2025年2—5月亮度极大期间基本恒定，该极大由消光从约20 mag降至10 mag所致。自爆发以来已吸积约2%的恒星质量。Hα 的 P Cygni 轮廓表明存在类似经典 FUor 的强外流风；CaII红外三重线变化及暂现CO带首发射指示高度不稳定的内盘与吸积区；禁线持续存在表明喷流活动持续。

Conclusion: V346 Nor 的亮度与颜色变化是吸积率变化与视线消光变化的叠加结果。凭借创纪录的吸积水平和强烈的风与喷流活动，它正处于自20世纪70年代原初爆发以来最活跃的阶段之一。

Abstract: Young stellar objects (YSOs) build their final mass through a highly unsteady, episodic accretion process, often accompanied by outflow and ejection events. Objects undergoing episodic high-accretion states are known as eruptive YSOs. V346 Nor is an embedded eruptive YSO that underwent a sudden optical brightening in 2024 July that triggered an alert from the Gaia Photometric Science Alert system. With the aim of characterizing the physical processes behind this recent brightening event, we analyze new and archival photometric and spectroscopic monitoring data. We study how the brightness, colors, and spectral features changed with time in the past decade. We also fitted an accretion disk model to the observations in which the stellar mass--accretion rate product $M_{\star}\dot{M}$ and the line-of-sight extinction $A_V$ are free parameters. The accretion rate in V346 Nor has been higher ($M_{\star}\dot{M} \sim 10^{-4}\,M_{\odot}^2\,$yr$^{-1}$) since 2021 than during any previous historical peak, although it was relatively constant during the 2025 February-May brightness maximum, which was driven instead by a drop in extinction from $A_V \approx 20$\,mag to $10$\,mag. V346 Nor has accreted about 2\% of the stellar mass since its outburst. A P Cygni profile in H$α$ indicates a strong outflowing wind reminiscent of classical FUors. The variability of the CaII infrared triplet lines and transient CO bandhead emission point to a highly unstable inner disk and accretion region. The persistence of forbidden line emission indicates ongoing jet activity. The brightness and color variations of V346 Nor are the result of a superposition of changes in the accretion rate and the line-of-sight extinction. With record-high accretion levels and strong wind and jet activity, V346 Nor is in one of its most active phases since its original outburst in the 1970s.

</details>


### [108] [Turbulence Properties of Corotating Interaction Regions at 1 AU: a Comprehensive Superposed Epoch Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14779)
*Yuliang Ding,Marco Velli,Chen Shi,Zesen Huang,Nikos Sioulas*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 利用WIND卫星在1 AU处对200多个共转相互作用区（CIR）做叠加历元分析，用MVA定位流界面、小波变换评估湍流性质，揭示了流界面附近压缩与速度剪切对太阳风湍流的调制机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于CIR附近太阳风湍流的观测与模拟结果存在分歧，需要对大量CIR进行系统统计，厘清压缩、速度剪切在不同频率尺度上如何影响湍流参数。

Method: 基于WIND卫星1 AU观测，对200多个CIR开展叠加历元分析；用最小方差分析（MVA）识别流界面，用小波变换在宽时间尺度范围内计算归一化交叉螺旋度σ_c与归一化剩余能量σ_r以及磁/速度谱指数。

Result: 磁与速度涨落谱指数由慢风的较陡平滑过渡到快风的较浅，界面附近速度谱局地变陡，显示压缩增强耗散；宽频带内σ_c在流界面处出现明显下凹，表明内向Alfvén波能量增加；σ_r呈峰-谷-峰结构，主要由大尺度速度剪切造成，低频段剪切人为增强速度涨落能量形成强峰，高频段则从慢风到快风平滑上升。约一半CIR伴随日球层电流片（HCS），且许多HCS与流界面高度吻合，提示二者存在内在联系。

Conclusion: 研究结果有助于调和以往观测与模拟研究之间的分歧，并为理解压缩和速度剪切如何调制CIR附近太阳风湍流提供新见解。

Abstract: We perform a comprehensive superposed epoch analysis (SEA) of more than 200 corotating interaction regions (CIRs) using WIND spacecraft observations at 1 AU. The stream interfaces are identified by Minimum Variance Analysis (MVA), and turbulence properties are evaluated using wavelet transforms over a wide range of temporal scales. The analysis of normalized cross helicity ($σ_c$) and normalized residual energy ($σ_r$) reveals distinct turbulence behaviors across frequencies. The spectral indices of both magnetic and velocity fluctuations smoothly transition from steeper in the slow wind to shallower in the fast wind, while a localized steepening of the velocity spectra near the interface indicates enhanced dissipation due to compression. Across broad frequency bands, $σ_c$ shows a clear dip at the stream interface, signifying increased inward Alfvén wave energy, whereas $σ_r$ displays a peak-valley-peak structure mainly driven by large-scale velocity shear. In lower-frequency ranges, velocity shear artificially enhances velocity fluctuation energy, producing strong peaks in $σ_r$, while higher-frequency ranges show a smooth increase of $σ_r$ from slow wind to fast wind. Nearly half of the analyzed CIRs are accompanied by a heliospheric current sheet (HCS), with many HCSs closely aligned with the stream interface, suggesting an intrinsic link between the two structures. Our findings offer valuable clues for reconciling discrepancies among earlier observational and simulation studies, and provide new insight into how compression and velocity shear modulate solar wind turbulence near CIRs.

</details>


### [109] [Influence of Alfven wave turbulence dissipation in stellar winds of the Sun and M dwarfs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14884)
*Kaito Suzuki,Takaaki Yokoyama,Munehito Shoda*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 本文用一维MHD模拟比较太阳风与M矮星风，将模转换产生的激波加热与阿尔芬波湍流(AWT)作为可选成分；太阳风结果可复现外传阿尔芬波占优并与PSP观测相符，M矮星中AWT主导加热预算，且显著降低质量损失率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M矮星星风物理日益在波驱动框架下研究，而太阳风中AWT被认为对等离子体加热与加速至关重要；但AWT（尤其色球层中的湍流耗散）在M矮星风建模中的作用尚不明确，需定量评估其对日冕加热与质量损失率的影响。

Method: 开展一维磁流体力学模拟，同时应用于太阳风与M矮星风；模型中包含由波模转换产生的激波加热，并将AWT作为可开关（可选）成分；对M矮星进行日冕磁场强度Bcor的参数扫描，并对太阳风结果与Parker Solar Probe观测直接比对。

Result: 太阳风：加入AWT后可复现观测到外传阿尔芬波占优，且阿尔芬点位置与快太阳风中归一化交叉螺度水平与PSP观测吻合良好。M矮星：在所考察参数范围内AWT主导总加热预算；有/无AWT时质量损失率对Bcor的依赖关系在性质上不同；无AWT时质量损失率与前人研究及观测大致一致，含AWT时因湍流耗散降低进入日冕的阿尔芬波(Poynting)通量，质量损失率显著下降。

Conclusion: 色球层湍流耗散效应对M矮星风建模至关重要，忽略AWT会系统性高估质量损失率并改变其与日冕磁场的标度关系。

Abstract: The physics of M-dwarf stellar winds is increasingly being investigated within the framework of wave-driven models. In the case of the solar wind, Alfven-wave turbulence (AWT) is believed to play a crucial role in heating and accelerating the plasma. In this study, we perform one-dimensional magnetohydrodynamic simulations of the solar and M-dwarf winds using a model that incorporates shock heating generated by mode conversion and treats AWT as an optional component. For the solar wind, we confirm that including AWT reproduces the observed predominance of outwardly propagating Alfven waves, supporting the role of turbulent dissipation in driving the wind. We further validate this AWT-driven solar wind directly against Parker Solar Probe observations, finding good agreement in both the location of the Alfven point and the level of the normalized cross helicity in the fast solar wind. In M-dwarf simulations, we find that AWT dominates the overall heating budget within the investigated parameter range. A parameter survey of coronal magnetic field strength reveals that the dependence of the mass-loss rate on Bcor differs qualitatively between cases with and without AWT, highlighting the importance of chromospheric dissipation. In the absence of AWT, the resulting mass-loss rates are broadly consistent with previous studies and observations. In contrast, when AWT is included, the mass-loss rate is significantly reduced because turbulent dissipation decreases the Alfven-wave (Poynting) flux entering the corona. These results suggest that the effects of chromospheric turbulence play a crucial role in modeling M-dwarf winds.

</details>


### [110] [A Failed Filament Eruption and Associated Active Region Heating Driven by External Magnetic Reconnection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14992)
*Yudi Ou,Yuandeng Shen,Dongxu Liu,Reetika Tiwari*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 该文研究NOAA活动区12353中一次未成功的暗条爆发，揭示强约束磁场阻止爆发并通过外部磁重联破坏磁流绳结构、加热活动区日冕环体系的完整物理链条。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗条爆发失败的物理机制及其与日冕加热的关系尚不清晰，特别是磁约束与外部重联在失败爆发中的作用及二者耦合过程缺乏观测约束。

Method: 结合Goode太阳望远镜与太阳动力学观测台的协同观测，并利用非线性无力场外推重建磁场结构；通过微分发射测度（DEM）分析诊断加热，追踪从两低层暗条结构合并到细长暗条失败爆发的全过程。

Result: 爆发前磁流绳已进入易发生扭曲不稳定性（kink）的区间，且低层大气内部重联已发生，二者可能共同贡献初始加速；上升过程中磁流绳始终低于环面不稳定性（torus）阈值，被强非对称覆盖场压缩。观测到界面增亮、双向等离子体团状运动、新亮环系及暗条逐渐瓦解，均与外部重联一致；DEM分析显示外部重联加热了活动区环系，界面处出现局地高温瞬变结构，与外部重联电流片位置相符。

Conclusion: 强约束场阻止了暗条爆发并维持界面压缩，从而促成电流片形成，使外部重联得以破坏磁流绳并加热活动区日冕环系统。

Abstract: We investigate a failed filament eruption and associated coronal heating in NOAA Active Region 12353 on 2015 May 23, focusing on the relationship between magnetic confinement and external reconnection. Coordinated observations from the Goode Solar Telescope and the Solar Dynamics Observatory, together with nonlinear force-free field extrapolations, allow us to trace the event from the merging of two low-lying filamentary structures to the failed eruption of the resulting elongated filament. Before the rapid rise, the modeled flux rope had entered a regime susceptible to kink instability, while low-atmospheric internal reconnection had already occurred; both may have contributed to the initial acceleration. The rising flux rope remained below the torus instability threshold and was confined by a strong, asymmetric overlying field that compressed it against the overlying loops. An elongated interface brightening, bidirectional plasmoid-like motions, newly brightened loop systems, and progressive weakening of the filament are consistent with external reconnection that changed the magnetic connectivity, removed magnetic flux from the rope, and disrupted its coherent structure. Differential emission measure analysis indicates that the external reconnection heated the active region loop system. A transient, localized structure with high-temperature emission was observed at the interaction interface, consistent with the expected location of a current sheet associated with external reconnection. These results suggest that the strong confining field halted the filament eruption and sustained the interface compression required for current sheet formation, enabling external reconnection to disrupt the flux rope and heat the active region loop system.

</details>


### [111] [Evolutionary Characteristics of the Solar Coronal Rotation: Revealed by a Century of Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15061)
*N. B. Xiang,X. H. Zhao,S. W. Feng,C. Y. Shi,X. Y Yu*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 本文利用1874-2017年10.7 cm射电通量序列，通过小波分析与线性回归系统研究日冕自转周期长度（RPL）的多尺度演化，发现主周期约26.72天且随太阳活动周复杂变化，其复杂演化源于小尺度磁场的直接主导作用与太阳磁活动水平对整个太阳大气自转调制的共同结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日冕自转周期长度随太阳活动周如何变化、其与太阳黑子数关系是否稳定、以及驱动这种复杂演化的物理机制尚不清楚；10.7 cm射电通量是可用时间最长的日冕观测代理，为长期系统研究提供了条件。

Method: 采用1874年5月9日至2017年6月30日的10.7 cm射电通量时间序列，使用小波分析（含交叉小波变换与小波相干）和线性回归，分析日冕自转周期长度的多尺度演化及其与太阳黑子数（SN）的关系、对活动周相位的依赖。

Result: 全时段日冕自转以约26.72天的会合周期为主，并随太阳活动周复杂变化；RPL与SN关系随时间改变：1874.9-1943.1反相关、1943.1-1958.2正相关、1958.2-1987.4反相关、1987.4-2016.9正相关，总体呈负相关但关系复杂；活动弱时自转较慢，活动强时（约活动周第3至6年）自转加快；RPL中检测到若干统计显著周期。

Conclusion: 日冕自转周期长度的复杂演化本质上源于两个因素的共同调制：普遍存在的小尺度磁场对日冕自转的直接且主导的作用，以及太阳磁活动水平对整个太阳大气自转的调制。

Abstract: The 10.7 cm radio flux time series from 1874 May 9 to 2017 June 30, the longest available observational proxy of the solar corona, is used to systematically investigate the multi-scale evolution of coronal rotation period lengths (RPLs) using wavelet analysis and linear regression. Throughout the time interval considered, the coronal rotation is characterized by a dominant synodic period of approximately 26.72 days, which varies in a complex manner with the advancing solar cycle. The relationship between the coronal RPLs and sunspot number (SN) changes over time: anticorrelation during 1874.9-1943.1, correlation during 1943.1-1958.2, anticorrelation during 1958.2-1987.4, and correlation during 1987.4-2016.9. Overall, RPLs show a negative correlation with SN, but their relationship is complex. This scenario is reflected in the phase dependence of the RPLs on the solar cycle. The variation of RPLs is complex, but a distinct pattern emerges: during weak activity phases, coronal rotation is slower, whereas during strong activity phases (approximately years 3 to 6 of the solar cycle), it becomes faster. Several statistically significant periods are detected in RPLs. Cross wavelet transform and wavelet coherence further confirm that the complex evolutionary pattern of RPLs fundamentally results from the combined modulation of two factors: the direct and dominant effect of the ubiquitous small-scale magnetic fields on the coronal rotation, and the modulation of the entire solar atmospheric rotation by the solar magnetic activity level.

</details>


### [112] [UOCS. XVI. Census of post mass-transfer binaries across various evolutionary phases in M67](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15230)
*Harshit Pal,Vikrant V. Jadhav,Arumalla B. S. Reddy,Annapurni Subramaniam*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 通过UVIT/UVOT远紫外SED与GALAH高分辨率光谱两条独立途径，在M67中系统性搜寻各类质量转移后的双星系统，发现11颗新的MS+WD双星（含5颗ELM WD、周期100–200天、属Case A/B）和13个周期>1000天、s过程元素增丰的SB1系统（属Case C），首次在同一星团内给出Case A/B/C质量转移的统一图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M67是太阳金属丰度、年老的疏散星团，包含丰富的质量转移（MT）产物。以往Case A、B、C型MT系统多在个别星团中分别研究，缺乏在单一星团内跨演化阶段、覆盖全部MT类型的系统性调查，因此难以获得对双星演化与质量转移机制的完整认识。

Method: 对M67中141颗紫外探测恒星，用AstroSat/UVIT远紫外数据结合Swift/UVOT、GALEX、Gaia、2MASS、WISE等档案数据构建SED，以搜寻隐藏的热伴星；同时对星团成员星利用GALAH DR3+高分辨率光谱进行详细化学丰度分析，借助s过程元素特征识别已演化伴星留下的化学印记。

Result: 新证认11颗此前未被发现热伴星的MS+WD双星，使M67已知MS+WD双星数目约翻倍；其中5颗为确认的ELM WD，轨道周期100–200天且无化学特殊性，对应Case A或B型post-MT系统。另探测到13个长周期（>1000天）SB1系统，其Ba、La、Y等s过程元素增丰，对应由热脉冲AGB伴星污染、伴星现已演化为未被探测WD的Case C型post-MT系统。

Conclusion: M67拥有经由Case A、B、C机制形成的多样化post-MT双星族群，这一统一视角使M67成为研究双星演化的关键基准星团，也凸显了紫外测光与高分辨率光谱在揭示"隐藏恒星演化史"方面的重要作用。

Abstract: M67 is a well-studied, solar-metallicity, old open cluster that hosts a diverse set of exotic stellar populations formed via mass transfer (MT). While Case A, B, and C MT systems have been individually examined in several clusters, a comprehensive investigation of all MT types across different evolutionary stages within a single cluster remains limited. This study aims to detect the presence of post mass-transfer systems using two different methods and thereby obtain a holistic view of binary evolution and mass-transfer mechanisms prevalent in M67. We constructed spectral energy distributions (SEDs) for 141 UV-detected stars in M67 using far-UV data from AstroSat/UVIT, combined with archival data from Swift/UVOT, GALEX, Gaia, 2MASS, WISE, and other sources. Furthermore, we performed a detailed chemical abundance analysis of cluster members using high-resolution spectroscopy from the GALAH DR3+ survey. We identified 11 new MS+WD binaries with previously undetected hotter companions, effectively doubling the known MS+WD binary population in M67. This includes five confirmed ELM WDs with orbital periods of 100-200 days and no chemical peculiarities, confirming Case A or B post-MT systems. We also detected 13 long-period SB1 systems with orbital periods longer than 1000 days and enhancements in s-process elements (Ba, La, Y), indicating Case C post-MT systems enriched by thermally pulsing asymptotic giant branch (AGB) companions which have evolved into undetected WDs. This study demonstrates that M67 hosts a diverse population of post-mass transfer binaries formed via Case A, B, and C mechanisms. The unified view makes M67 a key benchmark for studying binary star evolution and highlights the role of UV observations and high-resolution spectroscopy in uncovering hidden stellar histories.

</details>


### [113] [Statistical Characterization of CME Interaction Responsible for Successive Type II Solar Radio Bursts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15295)
*Anshu Kumari,Divya Paliwal,Jaydeep Kandekar*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 本文首次对太阳活动周23、24及25上升期（1997–2025）的连续型II型射电暴开展了统计分析，基于射电动态谱与SOHO LASCO日冕仪观测构建了73个独立事件目录，并按物理相互作用机制分为CME–冕流相互作用、CME–CME相互作用和单CME三类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续型II型射电暴常被视为CME驱动激波演化及其与结构日冕相互作用的诊断工具，但其物理驱动机制尚不清楚，以往研究多局限于单个事件，缺乏系统性统计。

Method: 利用射电动态谱和SOHO LASCO日冕仪观测，构建1997–2025年间73个独立连续型II型事件目录，依据推断的物理相互作用机制划分为CME–冕流、CME–CME和单CME三类，并对其中56个分类明确的事件进行统计分析。

Result: CME–冕流相互作用在可物理分类事件中占比最大，凸显环境日冕结构对连续型II型射电辐射的重要作用；CME线性速度在各物理类别间差异较小，而CME角宽度在CME–CME与CME–冕流群体间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 激波演化与日冕环境等因素对连续型II型射电暴的产生同样重要；所构建的目录为研究CME驱动激波演化提供了统计基础框架。

Abstract: Successive type II solar radio bursts are often considered as diagnostics for the evolution of CME-driven shocks and their interaction with the structured solar corona. However, the physical mechanisms driving these successive type II emissions are not well understood, with prior studies mostly limited to single event. In this work, we provide the first statistical analysis of successive type II radio bursts over Solar Cycles 23, 24 and the ascending phase of Solar Cycle 25 (1997-2025). We have constructed a catalogue of 73 independent successive type II events using radio dynamic spectra and SOHO LASCO coronagraph observations. The events were divided into three observational classes, based on the inferred physical interaction mechanisms: CME-Streamer interaction, CME-CME interaction and Single-CME. Statistical analyses were performed on the 56 events with clear physical classification. CME-streamer interactions constitute the largest class among the physically classified event, highlighting the potential importance of the ambient coronal environment in shaping successive type II radio emission. Comparative statistical analyses indicate that the CME linear speed has marginal differences among the physical classes, but the CME angular width is significantly different between the CME-CME and CME-Streamer populations. Other factors, such as shock evolution and the ambient coronal environment, are important for the production of successive type II radio burst. The catalogue presented here provides a statistical framework for the investigation of CME-driven shock evolution.

</details>


### [114] [Low effective-temperature diminution of H$α$ emission from metallic centrifugal magnetospheres fed by ion-runaway winds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15352)
*Stanley P. Owocki,Jiri Krticka,M. E. Shultz,Asif ud-Doula*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 论文提出，Teff≲17 kK的B型主序星Hα发射减弱/消失，是因为低光度无法驱动富氢星风，转而由重元素离子失控产生金属离心磁层（mCM）；在Teff≈16.8±0.3 kK的过渡区，氢仍可被加速进入内磁层，可解释ρ Oph C的Hα发射变弱变窄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早型B星（Teff≳17 kK）离心磁层（CM）中Hα发射的起始与强度已可由离心爆发（CBO）模型较好解释，但观测显示Teff≲17 kK的CM B星Hα发射明显减弱甚至消失，这一温度依赖尚缺乏理论解释，本文旨在填补该空白。

Method: 基于CBO模型框架进行解析分析：比较不同有效温度下辐射驱动星风能否有效驱动氢，提出低光度下由重金属离子线驱动发生"离子失控"、形成金属离心磁层（mCM）的机制；并针对过渡温度区Teff≈(16.8±0.3) kK定量讨论氢被驱动到足够速度后滑行进入内CM的情形，结合ρ Oph C的Hα谱线强度与宽度进行对照解释；同时分析无氢情况下金属堆积达到CBO阈值驱动磁重联产生射电辐射的可能性。

Result: 模型可自然解释：1) Teff≲17 kK的CM B星因缺乏富氢星风而导致Hα发射减弱或消失；2) 在窄过渡温度区Teff≈16.8±0.3 kK，氢仍可被驱动至足够速度滑行进入内CM，可解释ρ Oph C中Hα发射强度和线宽均降低的现象；3) 即使无氢，金属密度堆积至CBO水平仍可驱动磁重联，从而产生观测到的圆偏振射电辐射。

Conclusion: CM B星的Hα发射随温度下降的减弱与消失可用星风由氢主导转向金属主导（mCM）来解释，过渡温度区可由残余氢的"滑行"进入内CM说明，射电辐射则可由金属堆积驱动的磁重联维持；作者进一步讨论了金属堆积是否可能产生直接的观测特征，为后续观测检验提供方向。

Abstract: Main-sequence B-stars with a strong magnetic field and moderately rapid rotation form centrifugal magnetospheres (CMs), which often exhibit distinct H$α$ line emission, as well as circularly polarized radio. For CMs of early-type B-stars with $T_{\rm eff} \gtrsim 17$ kK, the onset and strength of the H$α$ emission is well explained in terms of a centrifugal breakout (CBO) model, in which mass fed into the CM by the star's radiatively driven stellar wind builds up to a density that is sufficient for centrifugal forces to overwhelm magnetic confinement. The analysis here now argues that the observed diminution and disappearance of the H$α$ emission for CM B-stars with $T_{\rm eff} \lesssim 17$ kK can be explained by the failure of their lower luminosity to drive hydrogen-rich winds; instead they develop an ion runaway of heavier metal ions that are the seat of line-driving, which thus feed a metallic CM (mCM) for which the requisite hydrogen is diminished or even absent. For mid-B-type stars in a narrow transition range of effective temperature $T_{\rm eff} \approx (16.8 \pm 0.3)$ kK, hydrogen can still be driven to a sufficient speed to coast into the inner CM, which we show can explain the reduced strength and width of H$α$ line emission in the transition CM star $ρ$ Oph C. But even in regions with no hydrogen, the build-up of metal density to CBO levels can still drive the magnetic reconnection needed to produce the observed radio. We discuss whether such build-up of metals could give rise to direct observational signatures.

</details>


### [115] [Long-period longitudinal oscillations in a solar filament beyond the theoretical period limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15545)
*Miguel Rojas-Quesada,Manuel Luna,Lela Talishvilli,Heidy Gutierrez*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 该文报道了一个周期为178–184分钟的日珥纵向振荡，超过标准摆模型约167分钟的截止周期，并指出需考虑日珥高度与磁场三维几何（含曲率与倾角）来解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准摆模型下，磁场几何对近太阳表面的纵向振荡周期给出约167分钟的上限；而实际观测到更长周期的振荡，说明现有理论解释可能存在缺口，需要考察高度效应与三维磁场几何的影响，并评估其对日珥地震学诊断的误差。

Method: 利用SDO/AIA 193 Å与171 Å观测数据，测量日珥（暗条）的纵向振荡周期；将观测周期与标准摆模型的近表面截止周期比较，并从两方面进行理论分析：一是日珥离太阳表面的高度通过降低局地重力加速度提高截止周期，二是磁场三维几何通过局部磁力线曲率所在平面的倾角改变纵向回复力（局部下凹型磁力线可减弱回复力、允许更长周期，只要平衡保持稳定）。

Result: 观测到周期178–184分钟的纵向振荡，显著长于标准摆模型约167分钟的截止周期。高度效应与三维几何效应均可在理论上解释更长周期的产生，但现有观测无法独立约束日珥高度、磁力线曲率和倾角这三者。

Conclusion: 解释日珥大振幅纵向振荡时必须考虑其三维几何与曲率；忽略曲率会给基于此类振荡的磁场强度地震学估计带来系统性误差，因此准确应用日珥地震学需要纳入振荡结构的三维几何。

Abstract: Solar filaments exhibit oscillatory motions in different directions, including large-amplitude longitudinal oscillations (LALOs), which are commonly interpreted as magnetoacoustic oscillations with gravity providing the main restoring force In the standard pendulum model, the magnetic-field geometry imposes an upper limit on the oscillation period of approximately 167 min near the solar surface. Here, we report a longitudinal oscillation observed in a solar filament using SDO/AIA 193 and 171 angstroms observations, with periods ranging from 178-184 min. These values are longer than the near-surface cut-off period derived under the assumptions of the standard pendulum model. To investigate this discrepancy, we consider two possible effects: the prominence height above the solar surface and the three-dimensional geometry of the magnetic field. The former increases the theoretical cut-off period by reducing the local gravitational acceleration, while the latter can modify the longitudinal restoring force through the inclination of the plane containing the local field-line curvature. Specifically, a locally concave-down field-line geometry can reduce the restoring force and permit longer periods, provided that the equilibrium remains stable. However, the available observations do not allow us to independently constrain the prominence height, field-line curvature, and inclination. This result highlights the importance of considering the three-dimensional geometry and curvature of filaments when interpreting their longitudinal oscillations. Since LALOs provide a potential seismological diagnostic for estimating the magnetic-field strength supporting filaments, neglecting their curvature may introduce systematic errors in such estimates. Therefore, accounting for the geometry of the oscillating structure is essential for the accurate application of filament seismology.

</details>


### [116] [Unraveling the Magnetic Fields of Starspots with Transit Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15726)
*Fabian Menezes,Adriana Valio,Alexandre Araujo*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 恒星磁活动随年龄衰减，但该衰减源于星斑"质量"（磁场强度）变化还是"数量"（面积覆盖率）变化的机制问题。本文综合Kepler与CoRoT的星斑凌星成像结果，发现星斑磁场强度始终处于约3500 G的饱和平台，与年龄和自转周期无关；而星斑面积覆盖率与恒星年龄强烈反相关（年轻恒星填充因子可达29%）。由此表明恒星磁活动的演化主要由表面浮现磁通量的数量变化驱动，而非星斑本征强度变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恒星磁活动随恒星年龄下降是已知现象，但其物理机制尚不明确：究竟是单个星斑的磁场变弱（质的改变），还是星斑覆盖的总面积减小（量的改变）。论文旨在通过综合近期两项基于星斑凌星成像的研究来回答这一关键问题，并为恒星发电机模型改进和年龄测定技术提供依据。

Method: 综合两项利用Kepler和CoRoT目标的星斑凌星成像（starspot transit mapping）研究：其一考察星斑磁场强度随恒星年龄与自转周期的变化，其二考察星斑面积覆盖率（填充因子）与恒星年龄的关系，从而从"质"与"量"两个维度对比分析。

Result: 星斑磁场强度始终维持在约3500 G的饱和平台上，不随恒星年龄或自转周期显著变化；星斑面积覆盖率与恒星年龄呈强反相关，年轻恒星填充因子可达29%，并随时间快速下降。

Conclusion: 恒星磁活动的演化主要由表面浮现磁通量的数量变化驱动，而非星斑本征磁场强度的变化；该结论对完善恒星发电机模型和改进年龄测定方法具有直接意义。

Abstract: Stellar magnetic activity is known to decrease as stars age, but the underlying physical mechanism for this decline remains a key question. Does this evolution occur because individual starspots become magnetically weaker (a change in quality), or because the total area covered by spots diminishes (a change in quantity)? We address this question by synthesizing results from two recent studies that leverage starspot transit mapping of Kepler and CoRoT targets. First, we show that the magnetic field strength of starspots remains consistently high, on a saturation plateau of approximately 3500 G, regardless of stellar age or rotation period. This suggests that the intrinsic quality of spots does not significantly evolve. Second, we demonstrate a strong anticorrelation between starspot area coverage and stellar age, with young stars exhibiting large filling factors (up to 29%) that decrease sharply over time. Our combined results provide compelling evidence that the evolution of stellar magnetic activity is primarily driven by a change in the quantity of magnetic flux emerging on the surface, not by a change in the intrinsic strength of the spots themselves. These findings have direct implications for refining stellar dynamo models and improving age-dating techniques.

</details>


### [117] [Euclid DR1 Legacy Science on Ultracool Dwarfs: Brown Dwarfs in the Blanco 1 Open Cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15740)
*Eduardo L. Martín,Ramarao Tata,Marusa Zerjal,Javier Olivares Romero,Nafise Sedighi,Styliani Tsilia,Nicolas Lodieu,Nikola Vitas*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 利用Euclid DR1覆盖Blanco 1疏散星团的大视场数据，识别出10颗共动的晚M/L型褐矮星候选成员，质量约0.03–0.06 M☉，其亚恒星族群数量约为对数正态质量函数预期的两倍，并将光谱序列延伸至L型褐矮星。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blanco 1是年轻（100–130 Myr）、邻近（234 pc）的疏散星团，其年轻褐矮星样本稀少，且此前观测多局限于星团核心，难以检验亚恒星质量函数并追踪外围与潮汐尾中的成员。Euclid的大视场与无狭缝光谱为系统搜寻极冷矮星并测量自行提供了新机会。

Method: 在35.6 deg²的Euclid DR1区域结合Euclid测光与无狭缝光谱选出22颗极冷矮星；将Euclid天体测量位置与VIKING、KiDS、Rubin位置联合，在13 yr中位基线上测量自行；用两种视场模型计算成员概率；9颗由Euclid光谱定级，第10颗依据颜色定级；用褐矮星演化模型估计质量。随后以该样本校准对246 deg²的VIKING与KiDS测光及运动的搜寻，并在核心区利用Subaru HSC存档图像和2011–2025年自行做交叉验证。

Result: 10颗为共动的晚M与L型褐矮星候选，两种模型下成员概率均≥0.5；演化模型给出0.03–0.06 M☉的质量；亚恒星族群约为按Gaia成员标定的对数正态质量函数预测值的2倍。其余12颗为低成员概率的晚M、L、T型矮星。外场搜寻得到60颗极冷候选（约7颗为偶然），核心区HSC像与自行给出35颗M7及更晚型候选，其中至少20颗为95%置信度的运动学成员。

Conclusion: Euclid数据将Blanco 1的光谱序列延伸至L型褐矮星，显著扩充了星团外围与潮汐尾中的候选成员，并提示该年轻星团的亚恒星族群相对简单质量函数模型存在数量过剩，需更大样本与光谱随访验证。

Abstract: A 35.6\,deg$^2$ field of Euclid Data Release~1 that covers part of the corona and tidal tails of the young (100--130\,Myr) nearby (234\,pc) open cluster Blanco~1 is presented. We select 22 ultracool dwarfs from Euclid photometry and slitless spectra, and measure proper motions over a median baseline of 13\,yr by combining Euclid astrometry with VIKING, KiDS and Rubin positions. Ten objects are comoving late-M and L dwarf candidates with membership probabilities of at least 0.5 under two field models. Nine of them are typed from their Euclid spectra, and the tenth is a candidate only if its colour-based type is adopted. Brown dwarf evolutionary models give masses of 0.03--0.06\,$M_\odot$ for the cluster age. The substellar population is about twice that predicted by a lognormal mass function scaled to the cluster's Gaia members. The remaining 12 objects are classified as late-M, L and T dwarfs with low probability of membership in the cluster. Using the Euclid sample to calibrate a search over 246\,deg$^2$ of VIKING and KiDS photometry and motions, we identify 60 ultracool candidates, with about seven expected by chance. In the core region of Blanco 1 archival Subaru Hyper Suprime-Cam images and motions over 2011--2025 yield 35 candidates of types M7 or later, at least 20 of which are kinematic members at 95\% confidence. These results extend the spectroscopic sequence of Blanco 1 into the L-type brown dwarfs and the list of candidate members over the outskirts and tidal tails of the cluster.

</details>


### [118] [The Role of Precursors and MHD Instabilities in Distinguishing Confined and Eruptive Solar Flares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15765)
*Wen He,Ju Jing,Jeongwoo Lee,Haimin Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 该文用68个GOES M4级以上耀斑样本，比较扭曲不稳定性、环面不稳定性、耀斑前重联与磁非势性对耀斑是否伴随日冕物质抛射（爆发型/受限型）的判别能力，发现环面不稳定参数优于扭曲参数，而将其与磁非势性（相对电流螺度、自由磁能）结合可获得最强判别效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定耀斑爆发前的磁场条件以预测耀斑是爆发型还是受限型是太阳物理的重要难题；此前统计显示部分爆发型耀斑出现在理想MHD稳定条件下，因此需要系统检验MHD不稳定性、耀斑前重联及活动区整体磁环境在决定爆发性中的作用。

Method: 选取2011—2024年68个GOES M4级以上耀斑，计算扭曲不稳定性（KI）与环面不稳定性（TI）参数，用前兆增亮（PBs）示踪耀斑前重联，并对各活动区计算相对螺度H_R、载流螺度H_J与自由磁能等非势性量，进行统计对比与组合判别分析。

Result: 18个TI不稳定事件中17个为爆发型，19个KI不稳定事件中12个为爆发型，TI参数判别力更优；PBs多出现在MHD不稳定区，但TI/KI稳定区的12个爆发事件中仅3个有PBs，说明耀斑前重联作用次之；TI参数结合磁非势性判别最强：36个爆发耀斑中24个满足√|H_J|/φ≥0.1或n≥1.13，而25个受限耀斑中24个满足√|H_J|/φ≤0.1且n≤1.13（φ为总无符号磁通，n为衰减指数）。

Conclusion: 除MHD不稳定性与重联外，活动区整体磁环境（磁非势性）对耀斑是否爆发起重要作用；将环面不稳定参数与磁非势性指标联合使用是判别耀斑爆发性的最有效途径。

Abstract: Identifying pre-flare magnetic conditions that determine whether a flare will be eruptive (associated with a coronal mass ejection) or confined remains a major challenge in solar physics. Motivated by previous statistical results showing that some eruptive flares occur under ideal MHD-stable conditions, we investigate the roles of kink instability (KI), torus instability (TI), preflare reconnection traced by precursor brightenings (PBs) and magnetic nonpotentiality in solar eruptiveness using an expanded sample of 68 flares above GOES class M4 from 2011--2024. To characterize the nonpotentiality of each active region (AR), we calculate the relative helicity $H_R$, current-carrying helicity $H_J$ and free magnetic energy. We find that, (1) 17 of 18 TI-unstable events and 12 of 19 KI-unstable events are eruptive, indicating that the TI parameter outperforms the KI parameter (2) although PBs are more commonly found in the MHD-unstable regime, only 3 of the 12 eruptive events in the TI/KI-stable regime show PBs, suggesting that preflare reconnection may play a secondary role compared with other factors. (3) The strongest indicator of eruptiveness is obtained by combining the TI parameter with magnetic nonpotentiality. Specifically, 24 of 36 eruptive flares satisfy $\sqrt{\left| H_J \right|}/φ\geq 0.1$ or $n \geq 1.13$, whereas 24 of 25 confined flares satisfy $\sqrt{\left| H_J \right|}/φ\le 0.1$ and $n \le 1.13$, where $φ$ is the total unsigned magnetic flux and $n$ is the decay index. These results suggest that the overall magnetic environment of ARs may play a significant role in determining the eruptiveness, in addition to the effect of MHD instability and reconnection.

</details>


### [119] [Automated identification of dipole mixed-modes in red-giant stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15882)
*Francisca Espinoza-Rojas,Michaël Bazot,Saskia Hekker,Sébastien Bourguignon*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"观测频率与渐近关系混合模频率重叠度"指标的自动化偶极混合模识别方法，结合遗传算法优化与全局模式分配，可在恒星自转、缺失模式与伪峰干扰下稳健识别红巨星偶极混合模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶极混合模同时携带对流包层与辐射核的信息，是判定红巨星演化阶段与内部结构的关键。然而传统依赖与渐近关系理论频率比对的识别方式，受参数空间简并、恒星自转、模式缺失及功率谱伪峰等因素影响，识别困难且难以规模化。

Method: 将观测频率与渐近偶极频率均表示为经高斯核卷积的"尖峰序列"，构造衡量二者重叠程度的指标；用遗传算法优化该指标获得最佳拟合参数；再基于最佳参数对振荡模式进行全局分配，从而实现自动识别。

Result: 在受自转扰动的RGB与CHeB合成频率上，方法在不同演化阶段均可恢复95%以上的偶极混合模，且对伪峰与缺失模式具有稳健性；在四颗基准星上，识别结果与既有文献一致，ΔΠ1估计通常在1%以内，δν_rot与q通常在10%以内。

Conclusion: 该方法有效应对了复杂偶极模谱带来的挑战，可实现偶极混合模的自动化识别，为演化恒星的均一化、大规模星震学分析提供支持。

Abstract: Dipole mixed modes probe the internal structure of red giants by carrying information from both the convective envelope and the radiative core. Hence, identifying these modes allows for the determination of evolutionary states and stellar interior conditions. This identification typically relies on comparing observed frequencies with theoretical ones derived from the mixed-mode asymptotic relation. However, the process is challenging due to parameter-space degeneracies, stellar rotation, as well as missing modes and spurious peaks in the power density spectrum. In this work, we aim to develop an automated method for identifying dipole mixed modes, suitable for large-scale asteroseismic analyses. We introduce a metric that measures the overlap between observed and asymptotic dipole frequencies, represented as spike trains convolved with Gaussian kernels. After optimising this metric with a genetic algorithm, we use the best-fit parameters to perform a global assignment of the oscillation modes. We then validated the method using synthetic frequencies of RGB and CHeB stars perturbed by stellar rotation, and further tested it on four benchmark stars. Validation using perturbed synthetic frequencies shows that our method recovers over 95% of the dipole mixed modes across different evolutionary states, and this performance remains robust to spurious peaks and missing modes. For the benchmark stars, our dipole mode identification is consistent with previously published results, with mixed-mode parameter estimates typically agreeing within 1% for $ΔΠ$1 and 10% for $δν$rot and $q$. Our analysis demonstrates that the proposed method effectively addresses the challenges associated with the complex dipole mode spectrum. Thus, it enables automated identification of dipole mixed modes, supporting homogeneous, large-scale asteroseismic analyses of evolved stars.

</details>


### [120] [New line-driven mass-loss rate predictions for central stars of planetary nebulae: Luminosity dependence when approaching the Eddington limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15892)
*Jiri Krticka,Jiri Kubat,Iva Krtickova*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 本文利用统一的全球线驱动星风模型，研究行星状星云中心星的星风对光度的依赖关系，并给出电离通量、终端速度与风效率的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近巨星分支星抛除外层后变蓝成为热星，可电离周围物质形成行星状星云，并能驱动线驱动星风；但此前缺乏一致考虑光球与星风相互作用的全球模型来系统研究这些星风对光度的依赖。

Method: 采用全球线驱动星风模型（一致处理光球与星风相互作用）对行星状星云中心星进行建模，重点考察星风参数随光度的变化，并计算电离通量、与观测对比。

Result: 质量损失率对恒星光度高度敏感；终端速度随有效温度升高而增大，对光度仅弱依赖。预测值与观测大体相符，但观测质量损失率散布较大，且预测终端速度略低于观测值。对低于约40 kK的冷星，平面平行模型给出的电离通量不适用。风效率较弱，说明Wolf-Rayet型中心星与重元素质量丰度升高相关。

Conclusion: 全局线驱动星风模型能较好描述行星状星云中心星的星风性质，但其弱风效率意味着Wolf-Rayet型中心星的出现应与重元素丰度升高相联系，而非单纯的星风效应。

Abstract: After expelling most of their envelopes, asymptotic giant branch stars turn bluewards in the Hertzsprung-Russel diagram and become hot objects. These objects ionise the surrounding material, possibly creating a planetary nebula. Because they are hot and rather luminous, they are able to launch a line-driven wind. The luminosity dependence of these winds has not yet been studied in detail using global wind models that consistently consider the interaction of the photosphere and the wind. We provide line-driven wind models of the central stars of planetary nebulae focusing on the dependence of these winds on luminosity. While the mass-loss rate is strongly sensitive to stellar luminosity, the terminal velocity increases with effective temperature and only weakly depends on luminosity. The predicted values agree reasonably well with observations, except that observational mass-loss rates show a rather large scatter, and the predicted terminal velocities are slightly lower than observational values. We provide ionising fluxes and show that for stars cooler than approximately 40 kK, the ionising fluxes computed from plane-parallel models are inadequate. The wind efficiency stays rather weak, which implies that the Wolf-Rayet type of central stars is connected with objects with an elevated mass fraction of heavy elements.

</details>


### [121] [$uvby-β$ photoelectric photometry of FGK-type pulsating star candidates in the Kepler field: observations, stellar parameters and asteroseismic analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15907)
*L. Fox-Machado*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 该论文对Kepler场中218颗FGK型恒星开展uvby-β测光，结合Gaia DR3距离与Bayestar19尘埃图估计消光，用经验测光定标得到金属丰度和有效温度，并对红巨星测定星震学参数，最后用PARSEC模型做贝叶斯等时线拟合，给出恒星基本参数及不确定度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要为Kepler场中较亮（Kp<12）的FGK型恒星建立一套自洽的、基于测光与星震学约束的基本恒星参数（温度、金属丰度、表面重力、质量、半径），并检验测光定标结果与以往测光/光谱研究的一致性，为后续恒星物理与系外行星宿主星研究提供可靠参数样本。

Method: 1) 对218颗FGK星进行uvby-β光电测光；2) 利用观测测光指数、Gaia DR3距离和Bayestar19三维尘埃图估计星际红化；3) 区分矮星与巨星，用经验测光定标确定金属丰度和有效温度；4) 分析Kepler长曝光光变曲线，对呈现类太阳振荡的红巨星测定ν_max与Δν；5) 以测光金属丰度、有效温度、星震学参数和Gaia DR3绝对视星等为观测量，用PARSEC演化模型进行贝叶斯等时线拟合。

Result: 获得了样本的星际红化估计、矮星/巨星分类、测光金属丰度与有效温度，并与以往测光及光谱研究结果进行了对比；对类太阳振荡红巨星测得ν_max和Δν；通过贝叶斯等时线拟合得到有效温度、金属丰度、表面重力、质量和半径及各自的不确定度。

Conclusion: 测光、星震学与Gaia天体测量联合约束的贝叶斯等时线拟合方法可为Kepler场FGK型恒星提供一套自洽且带不确定度的基本参数，测光定标与既有研究总体相符，所得参数集适用于后续恒星与行星宿主星研究。

Abstract: We present $uvby-β$ photoelectric photometry of 218 FGK-type stars brighter than $Kp = 12$ mag in the Kepler field. Interstellar reddening was estimated using the observed photometric indices, distances from Gaia DR3, and the Bayestar19 three-dimensional dust maps. The sample was classified into dwarf and giant stars, and their metallicities and effective temperatures were determined using empirical photometric calibrations. The resulting parameters were compared with those reported in previous photometric and spectroscopic studies. We also analyzed the Kepler long-cadence light curves of the sample and determined the global asteroseismic parameters, including the frequency of maximum oscillation power ($ν_{\max}$) and the large frequency separation ($Δν$), for red giants exhibiting solar-like oscillations. Finally, we performed a Bayesian isochrone-fitting analysis using PARSEC stellar evolutionary models, incorporating the photometric metallicities and effective temperatures, the asteroseismic parameters, and absolute visual magnitudes derived from Gaia DR3 data as observational constraints. This analysis provides estimates of the fundamental stellar parameters, including effective temperatures, metallicities, surface gravities, masses, and radii, together with their associated uncertainties.

</details>


### [122] [Sailing with the winds of HL Tau: the JWST view](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15910)
*T. Nony,F. Bacciotti,C. Dougados,M. G. Navarro,B. Nisini,P. Kavanagh,A. Banzatti,V. Delabrosse,L. Podio,S. Cabrit,A. Caratti o Garatti,D. Coffey,T. P. Ray,F. Massi,D. Fedele,E. Whelan,A. Garufi,S. Antoniucci*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 基于JWST Cycle 3计划，对HL Tau原恒星系统进行1.66–28 μm的NIRSpec与MIRI MRS联合观测，首次系统刻画其外流中分层风的多普勒运动学，并在恒星位置探测到以冷至温（<500 K）水汽为主的水线森林。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原恒星喷出物研究对理解年轻恒星与行星系统形成至关重要；HL Tau具有环状盘和显著的双极外流，是基准系统。作者希望厘清各类风成分的形态与运动学，以及内盘激发的水发射线。

Method: 使用JWST NIRSpec与MIRI MRS拼接观测，覆盖1.66–28 μm，成图外流红移瓣内侧约10″区域及蓝移瓣底部；并在恒星位置取MIRI光谱。

Result: 红移瓣中发现三层嵌套的分层风：高度准直、由8个节组成的快速原子喷流（径向速度约160 km/s）；宽角H₂风（约45 km/s）具复杂弧状子结构；约35 km/s的暖气体壳层（CO ν=1–0低激发跃迁），与ALMA的CO (J=2–1)（3–25 km/s）空间重合。蓝移瓣前1″呈类似分层，但喷流更快、准直度更低。恒星光谱显示大量水线，气体温度冷至温（<500 K），可能指示延展的升华冰储库。

Conclusion: 分层与运动学可支持磁驱动盘风情景，即各成分源自距恒星逐渐增大的盘区；但外层CO成分呈层流规则形态，而内喷流节与风中弧状结构可能反映脉冲式喷出机制。

Abstract: Studying protostellar ejections is crucial to understanding the formation of young stars and planetary systems. The Class I/II star HL Tau, with its ringed disk and prominent bipolar outflows, is a benchmark system for these studies. We provide a first overview of our Cycle 3 JWST program targeting HL Tau, focusing on the morphology and kinematics of the various wind components as well as on the water emission lines excited in the inner disk. Our observations cover the 1.66-28 $μ$m spectral range, combining NIRSpec and MIRI MRS mosaics to map the inner 10" of the red-shifted lobe of the outflow and the base of the blue-shifted lobe. In the red-shifted lobe we observe a stratified wind of three nested layers: a fast, highly collimated atomic jet structured in a chain of eight knots and with radial velocities of 160 km/s; a wide-angle H$_2$ wind with velocities of 45 km/s exhibiting complex arc-like substructures; and a warm gas shell, of radial velocity of 35 km/s, visible in low-excitation transitions of CO ($ν$=1-0). This CO shell appears co-spatial with the CO (J=2-1) emission detected by ALMA with velocities from 3 to 25 km/s. In the first 1" of the blue-shifted lobe we detect a similar stratification, but the jet is faster and less collimated. The observed stratification and kinematics of the flow may support a magnetically driven disk-wind scenario in which the distinct components originate in the disk at progressively larger distances from the star. However, while the external components observed in CO have the laminar and regular appearance predicted for such winds, the knots in the inner jet and the arcs in the wind could be the manifestation of a pulsed ejection mechanism. Additionally, in MIRI spectra taken at the star position we detect a forest of water lines dominated by cold-to-warm (< 500 K) gas. Their distribution suggests an extended reservoir of sublimated ices.

</details>


### [123] [A Remote-Sensing Framework for Interpreting CME Transit Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15935)
*Sotiris Stamkos,Angelos Vourlidas,Evangelos Paouris,Spiros Patsourakos*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个遥感诊断框架，通过将观测到的日冕物质抛射（CME）前沿电子密度演化与理想化自相似壳层模型及基于雪铲（snowplow）模型的压缩基准进行对比，来诊断CME在行星际传播过程中的结构变化。应用于2012年1月27日CME，发现其前沿密度演化呈现"混合系统"特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CME在日球层传播过程中，其结构会同时受到压缩、耗竭、物质再分布和侵蚀等多种竞争性物理过程的影响。现有观测难以区分这些过程各自的贡献，缺乏一种能够把观测密度演化与理论基准定量对照的诊断手段。

Method: 利用STEREO/SECCHI COR2-A、COR2-B与SOHO/LASCO C3的多视角成像，沿CME前沿按位置角分区提取电子密度剖面，并与前向建模的GCS壳层合成剖面逐区对比；对每个扇区做幂律拟合以量化径向密度衰减率，同时引入雪铲式压缩基准作为对照，并采用保守的超额密度判据识别物质堆积。

Result: 密度径向衰减呈现显著的方位角差异。COR2-A中多个西侧扇区的衰减比合成壳层更平缓、趋近雪铲式压缩区制，而其他扇区则比合成壳层更陡；COR2-B与LASCO C3从互补视角也显示扇区依赖的偏离，但具体分布随观测几何变化。按保守判据，仅在COR2-A的局部扇区中识别出明确的密度堆积，表明压缩在空间上是结构化的而非沿前沿均匀分布。此外，与雪铲基准的比较结果强烈依赖所假设的上游太阳风条件。

Conclusion: CME前沿的密度演化更适合描述为一种混合系统：既包含压缩主导的区域，也包含可能反映物质耗竭、再分布或侵蚀式演化的局部陡化区域。该框架为区分CME在日球层传播中改变其结构的各种竞争过程提供了实用途径。

Abstract: We develop a remote-sensing framework to diagnose coronal mass ejection (CME) transit effects by comparing observed density evolution with an idealized self-similar reference shell and snowplow-based compression benchmarks. We apply the method to the 27 January 2012 CME using STEREO/SECCHI COR2-A and COR2-B and SOHO/LASCO C3. Electron-density profiles are extracted along the CME front in position-angle sectors and compared with synthetic profiles from a forward-modeled GCS shell. Sector-wise power-law fits quantify the radial density falloff and reveal substantial azimuthal variability. In COR2-A, several western sectors flatten relative to the synthetic shell and approach the snowplow-like compression regime, while other sectors show steeper-than-synthetic declines. COR2-B and LASCO C3 provide complementary viewpoints and also show sector-dependent departures, although the detailed pattern changes with viewing geometry. Conservative excess-density criteria identify clear pile-up only in localized COR2-A sectors, indicating that compression is spatially structured rather than uniform across the front. Comparison with snowplow benchmarks also depends strongly on the assumed upstream solar-wind conditions. These results show that CME-front density evolution is better described as a mixed system containing compression-dominated regions together with localized steepening that may reflect depletion, redistribution of material, or erosion-like evolution. The framework provides a practical way to separate competing processes that modify CME structure during heliospheric transit.

</details>


### [124] [Do magnetic cycles of Sun-like stars exhibit Gnevyshev-Ohl-like rule?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15956)
*Suyog Garg,Bidya Binay Karak*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 利用威尔逊山天文台色球S指数数据，检验类太阳恒星周期是否存在Gnevyshev-Ohl（奇偶）效应，并考察其与自转周期间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳磁周期存在偶数周后接更强奇数周的振幅交替现象（Gnevyshev-Ohl规则），作者希望检验该规则是否也存在于类太阳恒星的恒星周期中，从而为恒星发电机机制及其对自转的依赖提供线索。

Method: 以MWO色球S指数作为恒星磁场代理；用准Planck函数与偏斜高斯函数拟合提取周期峰值，仅选取至少含4个可用周期的恒星；先以"一对偶-奇或奇-偶周期中后一周更强"定义GO规则，再采用替代表述（连续三周期中中间周期为最大或最小），并计算GO规则满足比例与自转周期、Rossby数的相关性。

Result: 按第一种定义，许多恒星（尤其慢自转体）较好地遵循GO趋势，其满足比例可达1.0，但该比例与自转周期、Rossby数均无显著相关。按替代表述、使用偏斜高斯拟合数据时，GO规则比例与自转周期的Pearson相关系数为0.25（p=0.299），与Rossby数为0.59（p=0.007）。

Conclusion: 类太阳恒星中可能存在GO式奇偶趋势，尤其在慢自转星中较明显；但高质量周期样本不足使结论受限，结果仅初步暗示恒星发电机机制及其与自转的依赖关系。

Abstract: One intriguing feature of the solar magnetic cycle is its alternating cycle amplitude: the even-numbered cycles are usually followed by stronger odd-numbered cycles, popularly known as the Gnevyshev-Ohl rule (or the even-odd effect). In this study, we probe whether Gnevyshev-Ohl-like trends exist in stellar cycles of Sun-like stars, utilizing the Mount Wilson Observatory (MWO) chromospheric $S$-index data as a proxy for the stellar magnetic field. We apply quasi-Planck and skewed-Gaussian function fits to extract the cycle peaks from the MWO $S$-index data and consider the stars that have a minimum of four usable cycles. First, we say that Gnevyshev-Ohl-like rule is obeyed in any star for a pair of even-odd or odd-even cycles, if the second cycle in the pair is stronger. For this definition, we find that many of the stars considered, especially slow rotators, hold the Gnevyshev-Ohl-like trends quite well, with the fraction of cycles obeying the GO-rule been $1.0$ for these stars. However, we do not find any significant correlation between the fraction of cycles obeying the rule and the rotation period or the Rossby number of the star. Next, we examine Gnevyshev-Ohl-like rule in the stellar cycles using alternative definitions for the rule. Saying that the rule holds as long as the middle cycle has a maximum or minimum in sets of consecutive three cycles, we obtain a Pearson's correlation coefficient of 0.25 $(p=0.299)$ and 0.59 (0.007), between the GO-rule fraction and the rotation period and Rossby number respectively, for the skewed-Gaussian fit data.
