Inspite of the fact, that our analysis suffers from a lack of sufficient number of good-quality cycles, we be believe that our results can still shed some light on the dynamo mechanism in other Sun-like stars and its dependence on rotation.

</details>


### [125] [ZTF J2229+1928: A 23 Minute Eclipsing Double White Dwarf Binary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16019)
*Viktoriia Voitiuk,Aaron Householder,Kevin B. Burdge,Christopher Layden,Joheen Chakraborty,Emma T. Chickles,Jack Dinsmore,Gabor Furesz,Juliana García-Mejía,Aurora Y. Kesseli,Geoffrey Mo,David Osip,Andrew Vanderburg,Yapeng Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 本文报告发现 DA 型双白矮星食双星 ZTF J2229+1928，其轨道周期仅 23 分钟，处于毫赫兹引力波频段，预计 LISA 四年观测信噪比约 5.3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极短周期（数十分钟）的分离食双白矮星样本极为稀少，它们是未来空间引力波天文台（如 LISA）在毫赫兹频段的重要验证源与前景源，因此需要系统搜寻并刻画此类天体。

Method: 在 GPU 加速的 ZTF 测光数据搜寻中识别候选体，利用 6.5 米 Magellan Clay 望远镜上的高速成像仪 proto-Lightspeed 进行跟踪观测并建立食模型；结合质径关系推求半径与质量，用 Keck/LRIS 光谱拟合温度，并将模型标度至宽波段能谱分布以估计距离与 LISA 信噪比。

Result: 测得两子星半径 R1=0.0146(+0.0009/-0.0008) R☉、R2=0.0186(+0.0010/-0.0008) R☉，质量 M1=0.49(+0.08/-0.07) M☉、M2=0.37(+0.06/-0.05) M☉；有效温度约 11,100 K 与 8,200 K，距离约 965 pc；预计 LISA 四年任务期间 SNR≈5.3。

Conclusion: ZTF J2229+1928 以 23 分钟轨道周期稳稳落入毫赫兹引力波波段，是不断扩充的食超致密双星样本中的新成员，将成为未来天基引力波天文台的重要源。

Abstract: We report the discovery of ZTF J2229+1928, a detached and eclipsing DA double white dwarf binary in a 23-minute orbit. The system was identified in a GPU-accelerated search of ZTF photometry and confirmed through follow-up observations with the high speed imager proto-Lightspeed on the 6.5-meter Magellan Clay telescope. Combining the proto-Lightspeed eclipse model with mass-radius relations gives component radii and masses of $R_1 = 0.0146^{+0.0009}_{-0.0008} \, R_\odot$, $R_2 = 0.0186^{+0.0010}_{-0.0008} \, R_\odot$, and $M_1 = 0.49^{+0.08}_{-0.07} \, M_\odot$, $M_2 = 0.37^{+0.06}_{-0.05} \, M_\odot$. Fitting the Keck/LRIS spectra gives component temperatures of approximately $11,100$ K and $8,200$ K, and scaling this model to the broadband spectral energy distribution gives a distance of $\sim965$ pc. Its short orbital period places it firmly in the millihertz gravitational-wave regime, and we estimate an expected signal-to-noise ratio $\mathrm{SNR} \approx 5.3$ with the upcoming LISA mission over its nominal four-year duration. ZTF J2229+1928 thus adds to the small but growing sample of eclipsing ultracompact binaries that will serve as important sources for future space-based gravitational-wave observatories.

</details>


### [126] [The ZTF Variability Archive (ZVAR): Initial Data Release (DR1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16166)
*Matthew J. Graham,Samuel Whitebook,Andrew J. Drake,Thomas Prince,Thomas Culino,Ilaria Caiazzo,Jan van Roestel,Zach Vanderbosch,Theo du Laz,Przemek Mroz,Kevin Burdge*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: ZVAR DR1 发布了 ZTF 对约13亿个与 PS1 交叉匹配源在2018年3月至2023年7月（5.37年）的图像差分成测光、候选周期和变星度量数据，周期敏感范围为5分钟至2.7年，并提供数据访问工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZTF 自2018年起积累了大量多波段测光数据，其中包含数百万周期性变星、活动星系核、恒星遗迹和次恒星天体，但需要系统性地整理并提供周期性及变异性信息，以便社区高效利用。

Method: 基于 ZTF 在 Palomar 天文台对北天的观测，构建变异性档案 ZVAR：对约13亿个具有 Pan-STARRS1 DR1 标识的 ZTF 源，生成图像差分成测光（image difference forced photometry）、候选周期以及变异性度量，覆盖 gri 波段，并开发配套的数据访问工具。

Result: ZVAR DR1 覆盖2018年3月至2023年7月共5.37年数据，对5分钟至2.7年的周期敏感；绝大多数源在 gri 波段累计约一千次测光测量。

Conclusion: ZVAR DR1 为大规模时域变异性研究提供了统一、可直接访问的数据产品，支持周期性变源及各类变异性天体的系统性研究。

Abstract: The Zwicky Transient Facility (ZTF) has observed the northern sky observable from Palomar Observatory since March 2018, accumulating a median of a thousand photometric measurements across $gri$ for each of more than a billion sources. Among these billions of objects are millions of periodically variable stars, active galactic nuclei, stellar remnants, and substellar objects. We describe the initial data release (DR1) of the ZTF Variability Archive (ZVAR) which provides image difference forced photometry, candidate periods, and variability metrics for approximately 1.3 billion sources in ZTF having an identification in Pan-STARRS1 DR1 (PS1). The data cover the 5.37 year interval between March 2018 and July 2023. ZVAR is sensitive to periods between 5 minutes and 2.7 years. We also describe tools for accessing the ZVAR data.

</details>


### [127] [Local Intensity-based Correction for SMFT Longitudinal Magnetograms: Validation with SDO/HMI Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16193)
*Suo Liu,Haiqing Xu,Jiangtao Su*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: SMFT单波长Stokes观测在弱场近似下的线性定标会系统性低估黑本影磁场；作者采用HSOS标准流程中的局部强度定标（LIC）修正，并以SDO/HMI同期同视场纵向磁图为基准，对2019与2024年26个活动区进行验证，发现LIC普遍提高相关性（平均Δr=+0.041，最大+0.197），证明其是稳健有效的预处理步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 怀柔太阳观测站SMFT基于弱场近似的单波长线性定标在黑本影区系统性地低估真实磁场强度（即"磁低估"效应），需要通过修正减小该偏差，并定量评估修正效果是否可靠。

Method: 采用HSOS标准数据处理中的局部强度定标（LIC）进行修正；对2019和2024年观测的26个活动区，使用SIFT特征匹配与基于RANSAC的单应性估计实现SMFT（992×830，0.242″/像素）与HMI（4096×4096，0.505″/像素）磁图的像素级对齐（内点率40%–88%），再以同期同视场HMI全盘纵向磁图为基准，比较修正前后的相关系数。

Result: LIC修正对全部26个活动区均提升了与HMI的皮尔逊相关系数r，平均提升Δr=+0.041，最大提升Δr=+0.197（AR24114，NOAA 13667）；但在低估最严重的像素上仍存在残余偏差，表明单波长线性定标存在固有局限。

Conclusion: LIC修正稳健且有效，应作为SMFT纵向磁图定量分析的常规预处理步骤；同时需认识到单波长线性定标无法完全消除强场区的系统偏差。

Abstract: The Solar Magnetic Field Telescope (SMFT) at Huairou Solar Observing Station (HSOS) derives photospheric magnetic fields from single-wavelength Stokes measurements using a linear calibration based on the weak-field approximation. In sunspot umbrae, this linear approach systematically underestimates the true magnetic field strength---a well-known effect of magnetic underestimation. To mitigate this bias, we apply the Local Intensity-based Correction (LIC) as implemented in the standard HSOS data reduction procedure and validate its performance against SDO/HMI full-disk longitudinal magnetograms that are co-temporal and co-spatial with the SMFT observations for 26 active regions observed in 2019 and 2024. Pixel-level alignment between SMFT images ($992\times830$, $0.242^{\prime\prime}$~pixel$^{-1}$) and HMI snapshots ($4096\times4096$, $0.505^{\prime\prime}$~pixel$^{-1}$) is achieved using SIFT feature matching and RANSAC-based homography estimation, yielding inlier fractions of 40\% to 88\%. The LIC correction consistently improves the Pearson correlation coefficient $r$ with respect to HMI for all these 26 regions, with a mean improvement of $Δr = +0.041$ and a maximum of $Δr = +0.197$ for AR24114 (NOAA 13667). Residual discrepancies persist in the most severely underestimated pixels, highlighting an inherent limitation of any single-wavelength linear calibration. Nevertheless, our results demonstrate that the LIC correction is a robust and effective preprocessing step, and we recommend its routine application in quantitative analyses of SMFT longitudinal magnetograms.

</details>


### [128] [Stellar Loci XI: Identifications and Parameter Determinations of Binary Stars by multi-band photometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16403)
*Yuanchang Wang,Haibo Yuan,Zexi Niu,Kai Xiao,Bowen Huang,Ziqi Gao,Maosheng Xiang*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个65波段紫外到红外SED拟合框架，用于识别未分辨主序双星并推断其参数，方法在模拟和实际样本中均验证稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型测光巡天包含大量未分辨双星，但识别它们并测量其子星性质仍具挑战性。

Method: 结合校正后Gaia DR3 XP光谱合成的miniJPAS星等与2MASS、WISE测光；基于LAMOST定标、依赖金属丰度的恒星轨迹及矮星颜色-绝对星等关系，构建单星与未分辨双星的正演模型；用MCMC对每条SED同时拟合两类模型，并利用Gaia距离作为HRD约束推断金属丰度、消光和子星测光参数，由PARSEC模型导出质量与质量比。

Result: 模拟显示方法能稳健恢复双星参数；对q≳0.4的双星敏感；金属丰度与LAMOST光谱值散差0.11 dex，消光与外部消光图散差约0.02 mag；与外部Gaia XP双星表分类大体一致；M67成员测试支持参数内部一致性；对质量筛选的Gaia NSS双星，主星质量残差散差0.071 M☉，在差异较大情形下次星质量与质量比估计更可靠。

Conclusion: 该方法可扩展至大型测光样本，支持双星族群研究与单个系统分析。

Abstract: Large photometric surveys contain numerous unresolved binaries, but identifying them and measuring component properties remains challenging. We present a 65-band UV-to-IR SED-fitting framework to identify unresolved main-sequence binaries and infer their parameters. Photometry combines synthetic miniJPAS magnitudes from corrected Gaia DR3 XP spectra with 2MASS and WISE measurements. Using LAMOST-calibrated, metallicity-dependent stellar loci and a dwarf-star color-absolute-magnitude relation, we construct forward models for single stars and unresolved binaries. We fit both model families to each SED with MCMC, using Gaia distances as Hertzsprung-Russell-diagram constraints to infer metallicity, reddening, and component photometric parameters. Component masses and mass ratios are derived from PARSEC models. Simulations show the method robustly recovers binary parameters. Tests on a LAMOST comparison sample indicate sensitivity to binaries with $q\gtrsim0.4$; the preferred model reproduces spectroscopic metallicities with 0.11 dex scatter, while inferred reddenings agree with external extinction maps with $\sim0.02$ mag scatter. Comparison with an external Gaia XP-based binary catalog shows broadly consistent binary classifications for common sources. Our metallicities more closely follow the LAMOST spectroscopic scale, and mass-ratio estimates in the intermediate-$q$ regime better trace expected $Δ$-HRD offsets. Tests using M67 members support internal consistency of inferred parameters. For quality-selected Gaia non-single-star binaries, primary-mass estimates agree with Gaia NSS values with 0.071 $M_\odot$ residual scatter; in cases with larger discrepancies, further tests indicate that secondary-mass and mass-ratio estimates are more reliable than the Gaia NSS values. The method scales to large photometric samples, enabling population studies and analyses of individual systems.

</details>


### [129] [Solar models using OPLIB monochromatic opacities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16669)
*M. Vidal,M. Deal,O. Richard,Y. Lebreton,J. Wang,J. Colgan,C. J. Fontes*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 本文在Cesam2k20恒星演化代码中实现了OPLIB单色不透明度数据，并将由此得到的太阳校准模型与日震学数据对比，发现使用单色不透明度（OP和OPLIB）得到的模型整体上比使用预计算不透明度表更符合日震学约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不透明度是描述恒星能量输运的关键要素，现有OPAL、OP、OPAS、OPLIB等多种不透明度表，且存在两种形式：按预设化学混合比预计算的Rosseland平均不透明度表，以及可分元素、可针对精确混合比计算Rosseland平均值的单色不透明度谱。作者希望利用单色不透明度谱提升模型的精度，并评估其实际改进效果。

Method: 将OPLIB单色不透明度数据引入Cesam2k20恒星演化代码，采用此前处理OP单色不透明度数据时已使用的方法，构建太阳校准模型，并与日震学数据进行对比。

Result: 使用单色不透明度数据（OP和OPLIB）生成的校准太阳模型，其整体上与日震学约束的吻合度优于使用预计算不透明度表生成的模型。

Conclusion: 单色不透明度数据（尤其是OPLIB）在恒星演化建模中相较预计算不透明度表能带来可观测的改进，支持在Cesam2k20中推广使用单色不透明度。

Abstract: Context. Opacity is a key ingredient in describing the transport of energy in stars. There are several methods for calculating stellar opacities, resulting in different tables (OPAL, OP, OPAS, and OPLIB). These opacities are available in two distinct forms: tables that regroup the Rosseland mean opacity pre-calculated at predefined chemical mixtures, and monochromatic opacity spectra of individual chemical elements, which allow for the computation of Rosseland mean opacity for a precise mixture. Aims. This study presents our implementation of the OPLIB monochromatic opacity data in Cesam2k20 and compares the solar-calibrated model obtained with these opacities to helioseismic data in order to evaluate the improvements made by using monochromatic opacities. Methods. We implemented the OPLIB monochromatic opacity data in the Cesam2k20 stellar evolution code following the method already used for the OP monochromatic opacity data. Results. We found that calibrated solar models produced with monochromatic opacity data (OP and OPLIB) agree better overall with helioseismic constraints than those produced with pre-calculated opacity tables.

</details>


### [130] [The Same But Different: SiO$_2$ Clouds in Two L-Dwarfs with JWST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16783)
*Joshua D. Lothringer,Jeff A. Valenti,Sarah E. Moran,Rhys Collins,Siddharth Gandhi,Seti Norris,William O. Balmer,Peter Gao,Brayden Roberts,Mark S. Marley,Hannah R. Wakeford,David K. Sing*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 利用JWST高信噪比1–18微米光谱，对两颗场L矮星（J0624、J2148）比较1D-RCE模型与反演模型，发现无RCE模型能完美拟合，反演强烈指示SiO₂云的存在（J0624为非晶、J2148为晶态），并支持含富铁镁硅酸盐云。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST光谱精度使亚恒星大气与云性质研究成为可能；此前缺乏对云L矮星宽波段光谱的系统模型对比与云成分约束，需检验1D-RCE模型的适用性并刻画云的多样性与复杂性。

Method: 对两颗L矮星的高SNR 1–18微米JWST光谱（覆盖约98%出射热辐射，含H₂O、CO、CO₂、CH₄、Na、K特征）先与三个独立1D-RCE模型网格（BT-Settl、exoREM、Sonora Diamondback）比较；再用petitRADTRANS进行大气与云性质反演；另用非贝叶斯优化框架探索更广的云场景。

Result: exoREM在网格模型中拟合最佳，但无任何1D-RCE模型令人满意；RCE模型可约束温度与云性质，金属丰度、C/O比与表面重力约束较差。反演强烈支持两者均存在SiO₂云，J0624为非晶、J2148为晶态；优化框架进一步显示两者还含富铁镁硅酸盐（Mg₀.₅Fe₀.₅SiO₃、MgFeSiO₄）。

Conclusion: 云L矮星大气拟合复杂，云性质既复杂又多样；宽波段JWST观测对云L矮星具有强大约束力，并揭示相似光谱型天体间的显著差异。

Abstract: The precision of brown dwarf spectra from JWST enables unprecedented insight into the atmospheric and cloud properties of sub-stellar atmospheres. Here, we compare both self-consistent radiative-convective equilibrium (RCE) and retrieval atmosphere models to high-SNR 1--18~$μ$m spectra of two field L-dwarfs from JWST, J0624 (L5) and J2148 (L6--L6.5). Our observations include spectral features from H$_2$O, CO, CO$_2$, CH$_4$, Na, and K and cover approximately 98\% of the outgoing thermal emission from the two objects. We first compare the data to three independent grids of 1D-RCE models: BT-Settl, exoREM, and Sonora Diamondback. While exoREM provides the best fit of the grid models, no 1D-RCE model provides a satisfactory fit to the data, highlighting the complexity of fitting cloudy L-dwarf spectra. Nonetheless, the temperature and cloud properties can be constrained with the 1D-RCE models, while metallicity, C/O ratio, and surface gravity are not well-measured. Next, we use petitRADTRANS to retrieve atmosphere and cloud properties, finding strong evidence for the presence of SiO$_2$ clouds in both objects. The SiO$_2$ is found to be amorphous in structure for J0624 but crystalline in J2148, highlighting important differences in objects of similar spectral type. We also used a non-Bayesian optimization framework to explore a wider variety of cloud scenarios, finding evidence for the inclusion of iron-rich magnesium silicates (Mg$_{0.5}$Fe$_{0.5}$SiO$_3$, MgFeSiO$_4$) in addition to SiO$_2$ in both objects. These results show both the complexity and diversity of brown dwarf cloud properties, while also highlighting the constraining power of broad-wavelength JWST observations of cloudy L-dwarfs.

</details>


### [131] [Inertial modes in a turbulent rotating magneto-convection simulation with the solar-like differential rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16771)
*Yuto Bekki,Hideyuki Hotta,Laurent Gizon*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 分析Hotta(2025)旋转磁对流模拟的九年高采样数据，用SVD提取低频惯性模本征函数，重现了观测到的赤道Rossby模、高纬模和高频逆行模，并揭示高纬模由斜压不稳定性激发、正行柱状模在m≈6处发生定性转变，湍流磁场主要通过改变背景态间接影响惯性模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期观测已发现多种太阳惯性模，但其非线性性质仍鲜有研究；作者希望在一套能自洽重现类太阳较差自转的先进旋转磁对流模拟中识别并刻画这些惯性模，以检验其激发机制与观测的对应关系。

Method: 采用Hotta(2025)模拟的九年高节奏数据，利用奇异值分解（SVD）提取各类低频惯性模的空间本征函数，并分析其频率、近表面振幅与空间形态，与太阳观测结果进行对比。

Result: 识别出赤道Rossby模、高纬模和高频逆行模等主要逆行惯性模类，其频率与近表面振幅大体与观测一致；得益于强斜压背景，m=1高纬模的大振幅（约10 m/s）及特征极区螺旋图案被成功重现；正行柱状模在m≈6处发生定性转变，m≳6为快速传播的对流模，m≲6为热扩散慢模且频率与热Rossby模的观测推断吻合；湍流磁场主要经背景态修正间接影响惯性模。

Conclusion: 太阳惯性模的观测性质与"在磁主导区制下维持类太阳较差自转"的模拟总体一致，高纬模可由斜压不稳定性（即使在湍流对流中）激发；但模拟在大m处仍过度产生正行柱状模，这是待改进之处。

Abstract: Recent observations have revealed a variety of solar inertial modes, but their nonlinear properties remain largely unexplored. We aim to identify and characterize inertial modes in a state-of-the-art simulation of rotating magnetoconvection, in which solar-like differential rotation was self-consistently reproduced. We analyzed nine years of high-cadence data from the Hotta (2025) simulation. The spatial eigenfunctions of various low-frequency inertial modes were extracted using singular value decomposition. We identified major classes of retrograde inertial modes, including equatorial Rossby modes, high-latitude modes, and high-frequency retrograde modes, with frequencies and near-surface amplitudes roughly consistent with the solar observations. In particular, thanks to the strongly baroclinic background in the simulation, the observed large amplitude ($\sim 10$ m/s) and the characteristic polar spiral pattern of the high-latitude mode at azimuthal order $m=1$ are successfully reproduced. This indicates that high-latitude modes are excited by baroclinic instability, which can occur even amid turbulent convection. We further show that the prograde columnar modes undergo a qualitative transition at $m \sim 6$, separating the fast-propagating convective modes at $m \gtrsim 6$ from thermally diffusive slow modes at $m \lesssim 6$ whose frequencies closely match recent observational inferences of thermal Rossby modes. Finally, we find that turbulent magnetic fields primarily influence the inertial modes indirectly through modifications of the background state. Our results suggest that the observed properties of solar inertial modes are broadly consistent with a simulation in which solar-like differential rotation is maintained in a magnetically dominated regime, although the simulation still overproduces prograde columnar modes at large $m$.

</details>


### [132] [Radiative Transfer Modeling of Multi-Shell Molecular Outflows in the Class 0 Protostar IRAS 15398-3359](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16805)
*Yushi Ishizuka,Shigehisa Takakuwa,Kazuya Saigo,Jinshi Sai,Shu Ishibashi*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 利用ALMA观测IRAS 15398$-$3359的H$_2$CO与CS发射，识别出四个壳层状外流成分，并用非LTE辐射转移与合成观测验证椭球几何加类哈勃速度场模型，揭示外流事件的间歇性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年的高角分辨率原恒星外流观测显示出单一壳层难以描述的复杂空间与速度结构，因此需要研究外流的多壳层结构及其运动学，以理解质量抛射事件的时间变化。

Method: 对Class 0原恒星IRAS 15398$-$3359的主外流进行ALMA的H$_2$CO和CS发射观测分析，识别出四个对应更延展CO外流腔壁的壳层成分；开展H$_2$CO非LTE辐射转移计算，并对三维模型做合成ALMA观测，与通道图和位置-速度结构比较；允许各壳层的几何、空间配置与运动学独立变化进行拟合。

Result: 共同的椭球几何形态与类哈勃速度场可大致再现所识别壳层的通道图和位置-速度结构。内外壳层的运动学时标与倾角分别约为1000 yr（$19^\circ$与$15^\circ$）和约750 yr（$5^\circ$与$8^\circ$）。外侧双极对的动量注入率约为内侧对的2.2倍。内外壳层的$\dot{M}_{\rm wind}/\dot{M}_{\rm acc}$比值分别为0.7–3%和2–7%。

Conclusion: 壳层之间的差异表明质量抛射事件具有间歇性，外流活动的强度与方向可能在约$10^2$–$10^3$ yr的短时标上发生变化。

Abstract: Recent high-angular-resolution observations of protostellar outflows have revealed complex spatial and velocity structures that are difficult to describe with a single shell. We have investigated the primary outflow of the Class 0 protostar IRAS 15398$-$3359 in the H$_2$CO and CS emission observed with ALMA and identified four shell-like components, which trace the cavity walls of the more extended CO outflows. Non-LTE radiative-transfer calculations of H$_2$CO and synthetic ALMA observations of the three-dimensional model show that a common ellipsoidal geometrical form and Hubble-law-like velocity field can broadly reproduce the channel-map and position--velocity structures of the identified shells when the geometry, spatial configuration, and kinematics of each shell are allowed to vary independently. The kinematic timescales and inclination angles of the inner and outer shells are $\sim$1000 yr ($19^\circ$ and $15^\circ$) and $\sim$750 yr ($5^\circ$ and $8^\circ$), respectively. The momentum injection rate of the outer bipolar pair is approximately 2.2 times that of the inner pair. The inferred $\dot{M}_{\rm wind}/\dot{M}_{\rm acc}$ ratios are $0.7$--$3\%$ and $2$--$7\%$ for the inner and outer shells. The observed shell-to-shell differences suggest episodicity of the mass ejection events, where the strength and direction of the outflow activity may vary over short timescales of order $10^2$--$10^3$ yr.

</details>


### [133] [Magnetic blue large-amplitude pulsators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16927)
*Andrzej Pigulski,Piotr A. Kołaczek-Szymański,Marta Święch,Piotr Łojko,Kacper J. Kowalski*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 本文提出，部分蓝大振幅脉动星（BLAPs）可能通过双星并合形成，并合产物带有强磁场，其脉动频率谱中均匀间隔的多重峰（三重峰）可用斜脉动子模型解释；该模型可说明首个BLAP（OGLE-BLAP-001）及另两颗BLAP的三重频率结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BLAPs位于赫罗图中热亚矮星与主序星之间，无法由单星演化产生，其形成通道是关键未解问题；已提出的并合情景预言并合产物可能产生磁场，而强磁场中的脉动可能呈现类似快速振荡Ap星的等间距多重峰结构，需要检验观测频率谱是否符合这一预言。

Method: 从BLAP形成通道出发，讨论双低质量白矮星并合或白矮星与主序星并合情景；类比磁白矮星的磁场产生机制，引入斜脉动子模型，对BLAP脉动频率谱中均匀间隔的多重峰（通常为三重峰）进行解释性分析，并与观测到的频率结构比对。

Result: 首个BLAP——OGLE-BLAP-001中发现的三个均匀间隔脉动频率模式可以用斜脉动子模型（即并合产物强磁场导致的等间距多重峰）解释；另有两颗BLAP也表现出类似行为。

Conclusion: BLAP频率谱中的等间距三重峰结构支持部分BLAP起源于双星并合并伴随磁场形成的图景；磁场与斜脉动子机制是解释这类脉动特征的有力候选，需更多样本检验。

Abstract: With the discovery of blue large-amplitude pulsators (BLAPs), one of the most important questions to be answered was in which evolutionary channel(s) these radially pulsating stars could form. Located in the Hertzsprung-Russell diagram between hot subdwarfs and main sequence, BLAPs cannot be the products of single-star evolution. Therefore, virtually all scenarios explaining their origin assume binarity at some stage of evolution. One of these scenarios proposed that some BLAPs may have formed as a result of the merger of two low-mass white dwarfs or a white dwarf and a main-sequence star. The merger scenario may result in the formation of a magnetic field in the merger product, as is postulated for magnetic white dwarfs. Pulsations in a star with a strong magnetic field can manifest themselves in a way known from rapidly oscillating Ap stars, i.e. in the form of evenly spaced multiplets (usually triplets) in the frequency spectrum. This phenomenon can be explained by the oblique pulsator model. We have shown that the three evenly spaced frequency modes discovered in the first BLAP, OGLE-BLAP-001, can be explained in this way. Similar behaviour has also been found in two other BLAPs.

</details>


### [134] [ALMA quiet Sun observations compared with a simulated MURaM-ChE chromosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17028)
*Maria Loukitcheva,Sami K. Solanki,Damien Przybylski,Stephen M. White,Robert Cameron*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: MURaM-ChE增强网络模拟能较好重现ALMA观测到的色球毫米波段亮温，尤其与2017年4月23日数据高度吻合，而Bifrost参考快照亮温偏低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估色球模型能否重现ALMA观测到的毫米波段色球亮温，并借毫米辐射诊断约束色球大气模型。

Method: 使用MURaM-ChE增强网络模拟，计算ALMA Band 3（3.2 mm）和Band 6（1.3 mm）的合成亮温，与宁静太阳、网络及增强网络区域的ALMA观测对比；分析亮温分布统计特性、对磁场强度的依赖及随波长的变化，并以Bifrost增强网络快照作为参照。

Result: MURaM-ChE在增强网络区重现了观测亮温的范围与变幅，与23 April 2017数据吻合最佳；Bifrost快照在两个波段均偏低，甚至低于宁静太阳观测值。模型间系统性偏差在两个波段均存在，可能源于大气密度分层差异及由此导致的有效形成高度不同。观测与模拟均显示亮温随磁场强度和波长总体升高，但离散较大。

Conclusion: MURaM-ChE对增强网络区色球条件给出了物理上自洽的描述，并抓住了毫米辐射的关键特征；毫米亮温对大气分层和磁结构高度敏感，说明ALMA观测具有约束色球模型的诊断潜力。

Abstract: Aims. We assess the ability of the MURaM-ChE enhanced network simulation to reproduce the chromospheric millimeter brightness observed with ALMA and investigate the properties of the simulated emission. Methods. We analyze synthetic brightness temperatures from the MURaM-ChE model in ALMA Bands 3 (3.2 mm) and 6 (1.3 mm) and compare them with ALMA observations of quiet-Sun, network, and enhanced network regions. We examine the statistical properties of the brightness distributions, their dependence on magnetic field strength, and their variation with wavelength, using results from Bifrost enhanced network snapshots as additional reference cases. Results. The MURaM-ChE model reproduces the range and variability of brightness temperatures observed in enhanced network regions, with particularly close agreement found for the 23 April 2017 data set. The reference Bifrost snapshots produce lower brightness temperatures in both bands, with values below even those measured by ALMA in the quiet Sun. The systematic offset between the models is present in both bands and is likely associated with differences in atmospheric density stratification and the resulting effective formation heights. Both the observations and simulations show an overall increase in brightness temperature with magnetic field strength and wavelength, although significant scatter is present. Conclusions. The results indicate that the MURaM-ChE model provides a physically consistent representation of chromospheric conditions in enhanced network regions and captures key properties of the observed millimeter emission. The comparison highlights the sensitivity of millimeter brightness to atmospheric stratification and magnetic structure and demonstrates the diagnostic potential of ALMA observations for constraining chromospheric models.

</details>


### [135] [Non-evolutionary effects on period change in Magellanic Cepheids. III. Empirical connection with additional low-amplitude variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17079)
*Rajeev Singh Rathour,Radosław Smolec,Vincent Hocdé,Nicolas Nardetto,Oliwia Ziółkowska,Igor Soszyński,Andrzej Udalski*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 在544颗含额外低振幅变率信号的麦哲伦云第一泛音经典造父变星中，约75.5%呈现不规则周期变化，首次把额外低振幅变率与不规则周期变化联系起来；这种不规则性与主脉动振幅反相关、与额外信号振幅无关，表明额外周期性更像弱饱和脉动态的标志而非致因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典造父变星的O-C方法可同时追踪演化性与短时标（非演化性）周期变化。作者此前在约33%的麦哲伦云经典造父变星中发现不规则周期变化，但当时刻意排除了存在额外低振幅变率的样本，因此尚不清楚这类"多重周期"第一泛音造父变星的周期变化行为是否与普通第一泛音群体不同。

Method: 选取544颗具有周期比 P_x/P_1O ~ 0.60–0.65 或 P_1O/P_x ~ 0.68 的麦哲伦云造父变星，使用OGLE测光数据，沿用前期研究的方法对其O-C图进行分类与表征，统计不规则周期变化的发生率，并与之前单模第一泛音样本比较；进一步分析不规则强度与主脉动振幅、额外信号振幅的关系，并在第3类样本中测量逐周期（C2C）周期变化与抖动幅度。

Result: 在339颗第一泛音造父变星（LMC 195颗、SMC 144颗）中发现不规则周期变化，发生率为约75.5%，显著高于此前单模第一泛音样本的约68.1%；合并后第一泛音发生率为约69.7%。在固定脉动周期下，不规则强度与主脉动振幅反相关，但与额外信号振幅不相关。此外，在37%的第3类样本中探测到逐周期周期变化，其抖动约比非Blazhko RRc星大7倍，且随脉动周期的标度关系更平缓。

Conclusion: 本研究首次给出额外低振幅变率与第一泛音造父变星不规则周期变化之间的经验联系。结果显示额外周期性更可能标记一种弱饱和的脉动状态，而非不规则性的驱动因素；同时揭示了该类星体存在显著的逐周期周期抖动，为理解造父变星周期变化的物理机制提供了新约束。

Abstract: The O-C method in classical Cepheids traces both evolutionary and shorter-timescale (non-evolutionary) period changes. In our previous works, we found irregular period changes in $\sim$33% of Magellanic classical Cepheids, but excluded Cepheids showing additional low-amplitude variability. Here, we establish whether first-overtone Cepheids hosting such variability differ in their period-change behaviour from the general first-overtone population. We analyse 544 Magellanic Cepheids with period ratios $P_{\rm x}/P_{\rm 1O} \sim 0.60-0.65$ or $P_{\rm 1O}/P_{\rm x} \sim 0.68$, using OGLE photometry, and classify and characterise their O-C diagrams following our previous studies. We find irregular period changes in 339 first-overtone Cepheids (195 in the LMC and 144 in the SMC), an incidence rate of $\sim$75.5%, a statistically significant increase over the $\sim$68.1% of our previous single-mode first-overtone sample. The combined first-overtone incidence becomes $\sim$69.7%. This provides the first empirical connection between additional low-amplitude variability and irregular period changes in first-overtone Cepheids. At a fixed pulsation period, the irregularity strength anticorrelates with the dominant pulsation amplitude but not with the additional-signal amplitude, suggesting that the additional periodicity marks a weakly saturated pulsation state rather than driving the irregularity. We further detect cycle-to-cycle (C2C) period variation in 37% of the class 3 sample, with jitter roughly seven times larger than in non-Blazhko RRc stars and a shallower scaling with pulsation period.

</details>


### [136] [Starspots on eclipsing giant stars II.: TESS continuous viewing zone giant V521 Dra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17080)
*A. Haris,B. Seli,K. Oláh,S. Rappaport,J. S. Thomsen,M. Turkki,T. Hackman,M. Tuomi,K. Yakut,A. Casasbuenas,M. A. Díaz,J. H. Terwel,B. N. Hauptmann,R. Gagliano,T. L. Jacobs,M. H. Kristiansen,H. M. Schwengeler,M. Omohundro,I. Terentev,B. Powell,V. Kostov,Zs. Kővári*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 本文利用TESS对分光食双星V521 Dra约5.3年的2分钟cadence观测，结合食图法（eclipse mapping）与解析黑子建模，研究红巨星主星的表面黑子演化，发现黑子经度持续漂移，为较差自转及对流区近表面剪切提供证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食双星是研究恒星天体物理的绝佳实验室；通过分析食光变曲线的调制可探测恒星表面不均匀性。V521 Dra为潮汐锁定、轨道周期4.27天的食双星（红巨星主星+主序次星，均为K型），拥有约5.3年连续TESS高精度观测，是研究多年尺度磁活动的罕见机会，作者希望约束其主星黑子图案的演化。

Method: 采用两种方法研究主星黑子活动：食图法与解析黑子建模。同时用多种手段确定系统恒星性质，包括食光变曲线联合建模、径向速度数据、光谱能量分布（SED）分析以及完整轨道光变曲线的前向建模。

Result: 恒星在食内与食外光变中均表现出强烈的黑子诱导调制；在203次主食中共识别出540个黑子食信号；食图法揭示最多存在四个黑子；黑子经度随时间持续漂移，表明存在较差自转；不同黑子的漂移角速度不同，暗示主星对流区存在近表面剪切。结合时序光谱与SED约束了双星系统的天体物理参数。

Conclusion: V521 Dra是研究恒星磁活动长期演化的优良目标，其黑子经度漂移与不同角速度的证据支持较差自转与对流区近表面剪切的存在。

Abstract: Eclipsing binaries are exceptional laboratories for stellar astrophysics. By analysing the modulations in their eclipse light curves, we can probe their surface inhomogeneities. The eclipse-mapping technique enables us to scan starspots in detail, using the transit of a companion star across the surface of a spotted star. When multiple consecutive eclipses are observed, this method may also reveal the evolution of spot properties and provide evidence for stellar differential rotation. We study the magnetic activity of V521 Dra, a tidally locked eclipsing binary with a red giant primary and a main-sequence secondary, both of K spectral class, on a 4.27 d orbit. This is an exceptional system with ~5.3 years of TESS 2 min cadence observations. This offers a unique opportunity to study magnetic activity in uninterrupted blocks over several years with very high photometric precision. We aim to constrain the evolution of the spot pattern of V521 Dra A. We used two methods to study the spot activity of the primary: eclipse mapping and analytic spot modelling. We analysed the stellar properties of the system with a set of methods, including combined modelling of the eclipse light curves, radial velocity data, spectral energy distribution analysis, and forward modelling of the whole orbital light curve. The star shows strong spot-induced modulations both in the eclipse and out-of-eclipse parts of the light curve. We identified 540 spot eclipse signals in 203 primary eclipses. Eclipse mapping reveals the presence of up to four spots. The spot longitudes drift continuously in time, indicating differential rotation. Spots in the eclipse-mapping data show differing angular velocities in their drift, implying a near-surface shear in the convective zone of the primary. Using time series spectroscopy and the spectral energy distribution, we constrained the astrophysical parameters of the binary system.

</details>


### [137] [AGB Atmospheres in Binary Environments: A First Step with CO5BOLB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17125)
*Gabriel Tomassini,Andrea Chiavassa,Bernd Freytag,Eric Lagadec*

Main category: astro-ph.SR

TL;DR: 本文用CO5BOLD辐射流体动力学模拟，在引力势中加入伴随星点质量项与离心项，研究双星系统中伴随星质量对AGB星大气结构的影响。结果显示脉动动力学基本不受伴随星影响，而密度结构沿双星轴出现明显各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGB星大气由对流与脉动驱动，动力学复杂；在双星系统中伴随星可能进一步影响这些动力学过程，进而影响质量损失与星周环境形态，但伴随星对大气本身的影响尚缺乏定量刻画。

Method: 采用CO5BOLD进行辐射流体动力学（RHD）模拟，同时建模对流内部与大气。修改引力势以纳入次级质量与离心项，将伴随星视为位于计算域外、处于稳定潮汐锁定圆轨道上的点质量，并忽略科里奥利力。对多个不同伴随星质量进行模拟，利用奇异值分解（SVD）识别脉动能量的空间分布并量化其性质，同时分析密度结构以识别伴随星引起的各向异性。

Result: 识别出两个动力学上不同的区域：脉动主导层与外层低频外流区。二者脉动性质基本不受伴随星影响，其频率、振幅与相干性在所有模型中保持一致。相反，密度结构对伴随星响应明显，沿双星轴形成显著各向异性，且随伴随星质量增加而增强。

Conclusion: 在所探索的参数空间内，AGB星自身的脉动动力学主导于伴随星的潮汐影响，而密度结构仍是双星性最敏感的示踪量。

Abstract: Asymptotic Giant Branch (AGB) stars are characterized by complex atmospheric dynamics driven by convection and pulsations. In binary systems, the presence of a companion can further influence these dynamics, potentially affecting mass-loss processes and the morphology of the circumstellar environment, yet this influence on the atmosphere itself remains poorly quantified. This study aims to fill this gap by employing CO5BOLD simulations with a modified gravitational potential to explore how companion mass affects the atmospheric structure of AGB stars. We used radiative hydrodynamics (RHD) simulations with CO5BOLD to model the convective interior and atmosphere of AGB stars. We modified the gravitational potential to include a secondary mass and a centrifugal term, representing the companion as a point-mass potential on a steady, tidally locked circular orbit outside the computational domain, with the Coriolis force neglected. We performed simulations with different companion masses and using singular value decomposition (SVD), we identified the spatial distribution of pulsation energy and quantified its properties. We also examined the density structure to identify anisotropies induced by the companion. We isolate two dynamically distinct regimes, a pulsation-dominated layer and an outer, low-frequency outflow region, whose pulsation properties remain largely unaffected by the companion: their frequency, amplitude, and coherence stay consistent across all models. The density structure, by contrast, responds clearly to the companion, forming a pronounced anisotropy along the binary axis that strengthens with companion mass. This indicates that the intrinsic pulsational dynamics of the AGB star dominate over the tidal influence of the companion within the explored parameter space, while the density structure remains the most sensitive tracer of binarity.

</details>
